TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning
Authors: Marius Dragoi, Ioana Pintilie, Alexandra Dragomir, Antonio Barbalau, Florin Brad
First: 2026-06-04T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:59:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TailLoR：保护参数高效持续学习中的主成分
基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法推动了持续学习的发展。本文提出TailLoR，利用预训练权重的奇异基U和V作为固定参考系，学习应用于奇异值矩阵的低秩更新。一种软谱惩罚机制抑制与主导奇异方向对齐的更新，减少干扰，同时将精细适应引导至高度灵活的长尾谱坐标中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce interference in continual learning while preserving pretrained knowledge, TailLoR introduces a method that leverages the singular bases U and V of pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame, applying a low-rank update to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty is applied to discourage updates aligned with dominant singular directions, thereby routing fine-grained adaptation into less constrained, long-tail spectral coordinates. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach improves model stability and performance across tasks without requiring additional parameters, outperforming baseline methods in continual learning settings.
为减少持续学习中的干扰并保持参数效率，TailLoR 利用预训练权重的奇异基 U 和 V 作为固定参考系，对奇异值矩阵施加低秩更新，并通过软谱惩罚抑制与主导奇异方向对齐的更新，将细粒度适应引导至更灵活的长尾谱坐标。实验结果表明，该方法能有效保护主成分，同时实现高效持续学习，且性能损失较小。
OdysseyArena: Benchmarking Large Language Models For Long-Horizon, Active and Inductive Interactions
Authors: Hang Yan, Fangzhi Xu, Qiushi Sun, Jinyang Wu, Zixian Huang, Muye Huang, Jingyang Gong, Zichen Ding, Kanzhi Cheng, Yian Wang, Xinyu Che, Zeyi Sun, Jian Zhang, Zhangyue Yin, Haoran Luo, Ben Kao, Qika Lin
First: 2026-02-05T16:31:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: 34 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has catalyzed the development of autonomous agents capable of navigating complex environments. However, existing evaluations primarily adopt a deductive paradigm, where agents execute tasks based on explicitly provided rules and static goals, often within limited planning horizons. Crucially, this neglects the inductive necessity for agents to discover latent transition laws from experience autonomously, which is the cornerstone for enabling agentic foresight and sustaining strategic coherence. To bridge this gap, we introduce OdysseyArena, which re-centers agent evaluation on long-horizon, active, and inductive interactions. We formalize and instantiate four primitives, translating abstract transition dynamics into concrete interactive environments. Building upon this, we establish OdysseyArena-Lite for standardized benchmarking, providing a set of 120 tasks to measure an agent's inductive efficiency and long-horizon discovery. Pushing further, we introduce OdysseyArena-Challenge to stress-test agent stability across extreme interaction horizons (e.g., > 200 steps). Extensive experiments on 15+ leading LLMs reveal that even frontier models exhibit a deficiency in inductive scenarios, identifying a critical bottleneck in the pursuit of autonomous discovery in complex environments. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/xufangzhi/Odyssey-Arena
中文标题/摘要
标题：OdysseyArena：面向长时程、主动与归纳式交互的大语言模型评估基准
大语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展推动了能够导航复杂环境的自主代理的构建。然而，现有评估主要采用演绎范式，即代理基于明确提供的规则和静态目标执行任务，通常局限于有限的规划时程。关键的是，这忽视了代理从经验中自主发现潜在转移规律的归纳必要性，这是实现代理前瞻性与维持战略连贯性的基石。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了OdysseyArena，将代理评估重新聚焦于长时程、主动与归纳式交互。我们形式化并实例化了四个基本要素，将抽象的转移动态转化为具体的交互环境。在此基础上，我们建立了OdysseyArena-Lite用于标准化基准测试，提供120个任务以衡量代理的归纳效率与长时程发现能力。进一步地，我们引入OdysseyArena-Challenge以在极端交互时程（如>200步）下对代理稳定性进行压力测试。在15多个主流LLM上的大量实验表明，即使是前沿模型在归纳场景中也存在缺陷，揭示了在复杂环境中追求自主发现的关键瓶颈。我们的代码和数据可在https://github.com/xufangzhi/Odyssey-Arena获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models in realistic, long-term, and discovery-driven agent scenarios, this work introduces OdysseyArena, a benchmark designed to assess LLMs through active, inductive, and long-horizon interactions. The method formalizes four interaction primitives to simulate complex environments and constructs two tiers: OdysseyArena-Lite for standard evaluation across 120 tasks, and OdysseyArena-Challenge to test stability over extreme horizons exceeding 200 steps. Experimental results across 15+ leading LLMs show that even state-of-the-art models struggle with inductive reasoning and long-term planning, revealing a critical gap in their ability to autonomously discover latent dynamics in complex environments.
为评估大语言模型在真实、长期且具备自发现能力的代理场景中的表现，本研究提出OdysseyArena基准，通过主动、归纳和长时交互评估模型能力。方法形式化四个核心交互原语，构建两级评估体系：OdysseyArena-Lite用于标准化评估120项任务，OdysseyArena-Challenge用于测试模型在超过200步极端时长下的稳定性。实验表明，即使前沿模型在归纳推理与长期规划上仍存在显著缺陷，揭示其自主发现环境动态能力的关键瓶颈。
HANDOFF: Humanoid Agentic Task-Space Whole-Body Control via Distilled Complementary Teachers
Authors: Lizhi Yang, Junheng Li, Nehar Poddar, Yiling Hou, Gio Huh, Robert Griffin, Georgia Gkioxari, Aaron Ames
First: 2026-06-04T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For a humanoid robot to be deployed in the real world, the choice of command space (i.e., the interface between task planning and whole-body control) is crucial. Existing whole-body controllers typically demand dense kinematic or spatial references that planners struggle to synthesize from task semantics. We instead propose a compact, explicit interface that is intuitive, general, modular, and expressive enough for diverse manipulation skills. To this end, we introduce HANDOFF, a single humanoid whole-body controller that follows this interface and is distilled via multi-teacher KL distillation under a context-conditioned gating scheme into a mixture-of-experts student from three complementary specialists: whole-body motion tracking with safety-filtered data, locomotion, and fall-recovery. On the Unitree G1, HANDOFF matches state-of-the-art velocity tracking and offers one of the largest robust manipulation workspaces. We further demonstrate hardware feasibility through multiple natural-language-driven task roll-outs, powered by a VLM-driven agentic planner with no task-specific data or controller fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HANDOFF：通过蒸馏互补教师实现人形机器人任务空间全身控制
为使人形机器人能在现实世界中部署，任务规划与全身控制之间的指令空间选择至关重要。现有全身控制器通常需要密集的运动学或空间参考，而规划器难以从任务语义中合成。我们提出一种紧凑、显式的接口，直观、通用、模块化，足以支持多样化的操作技能。为此，我们引入HANDOFF，这是一种遵循该接口的单一人形全身控制器，通过多教师KL蒸馏，在上下文条件门控机制下，被蒸馏为一个由三类互补专家（全身运动跟踪（安全过滤数据）、移动、跌倒恢复）组成的专家混合学生模型。在Unitree G1平台上，HANDOFF实现了最先进的速度跟踪性能，并提供了目前最大的鲁棒操作工作空间。我们进一步通过多个自然语言驱动的任务执行，验证了其硬件可行性，该执行由无需任务特定数据或控制器微调的视觉语言模型驱动的代理规划器驱动。
Summary / 总结
To enable humanoid robots to operate effectively in real-world environments, the paper addresses the challenge of selecting an appropriate command space that bridges task planning and whole-body control, where existing controllers require dense kinematic references difficult for planners to generate. The authors propose HANDOFF, a compact, intuitive, and modular task-space interface that supports diverse manipulation skills. HANDOFF is trained via multi-teacher KL distillation under a context-conditioned gating scheme, combining three complementary expert controllers: safety-filtered motion tracking, locomotion, and fall-recovery. Evaluated on the Unitree G1, HANDOFF achieves state-of-the-art velocity tracking performance and provides one of the largest robust manipulation workspaces. It also demonstrates hardware feasibility through natural-language-driven task execution using a vision-language model (VLM)-based agentic planner without task-specific fine-tuning.
为使人形机器人能在现实环境中有效运作，本文解决了任务规划与全身控制之间接口选择的难题，现有控制器需密集的运动学输入，而规划器难以从任务语义中生成。作者提出HANDOFF，一种紧凑、直观、模块化的任务空间接口，支持多样化的操作技能。HANDOFF通过多教师KL蒸馏与上下文条件门控机制训练，融合三个互补专家：带安全过滤的全身运动跟踪、移动与跌倒恢复。在Unitree G1平台上，HANDOFF达到最先进速度跟踪性能，并提供目前最大的鲁棒操作工作空间。此外，它通过视觉语言模型驱动的代理规划器实现自然语言驱动的任务执行，无需任务特定数据或控制器微调，验证了硬件可行性。
Code2LoRA: Hypernetwork-Generated Adapters for Code Language Models under Software Evolution
Authors: Liliana Hotsko, Yinxi Li, Yuntian Deng, Pengyu Nie
First: 2026-06-04T17:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:59:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Code language models need repository-level context to resolve imports, APIs, and project conventions. Existing methods inject this knowledge as long inputs (retrieved through RAG or dependency analysis) or through per-repository fine-tuning and LoRA -- costly at repository scale and brittle to evolving codebases. We introduce Code2LoRA, a hypernetwork framework that generates repository-specific LoRA adapters, effectively injecting repository knowledge with zero inference-time token overhead. Code2LoRA supports two usage scenarios: Code2LoRA-Static converts a single repository snapshot into an adapter, suitable for comprehension of stable codebases; while Code2LoRA-Evo maintains an adapter backed by a GRU hidden state updated per code diff, suitable for active development of evolving codebases. To evaluate Code2LoRA against parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, we build RepoPeftBench, a benchmark of 604 Python repositories with two tracks: a static track with 40K training and 12K test assertion-completion tasks, and an evolution track with 215K commit-derived training and 87K commit-derived test tasks. On the static track, Code2LoRA-Static achieves 63.8% cross-repo and 66.2% in-repo exact match, matching the per-repository LoRA upper bound; on the evolution track, Code2LoRA-Evo achieves 60.3% cross-repo exact match (+5.2 pp over a single shared LoRA). Code2LoRA's code can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/code2lora-6857; the model checkpoints and RepoPeftBench datasets can be found at https://huggingface.co/code2lora.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Code2LoRA：面向软件演化的代码语言模型的超网络生成适配器
代码语言模型需要仓库级上下文以解析导入、API和项目约定。现有方法通过长输入（通过RAG或依赖分析检索）或每个仓库的微调和LoRA注入此类知识，但在仓库规模下成本高昂且对演进中的代码库脆弱。我们提出Code2LoRA，一种超网络框架，可生成针对特定仓库的LoRA适配器，有效注入仓库知识且无推理时token开销。Code2LoRA支持两种使用场景：Code2LoRA-Static将单个仓库快照转换为适配器，适用于稳定代码库的理解；而Code2LoRA-Evo则通过GRU隐藏状态随代码差异更新来维护适配器，适用于演进中的代码库的主动开发。为评估Code2LoRA相对于参数高效微调基线方法，我们构建了RepoPeftBench基准，包含604个Python仓库，分为两个赛道：静态赛道含4万训练和1.2万测试断言补全任务；演进赛道含21.5万提交衍生训练和8.7万提交衍生测试任务。在静态赛道上，Code2LoRA-Static实现跨仓库63.8%、同仓库66.2%的精确匹配率，达到每个仓库LoRA上限；在演进赛道上，Code2LoRA-Evo实现跨仓库60.3%精确匹配率（比单一共享LoRA高5.2个百分点）。Code2LoRA代码见https://anonymous.4open.science/r/code2lora-6857；模型检查点和RepoPeftBench数据集见https://huggingface.co/code2lora。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently inject repository-level context into code language models without costly fine-tuning or inference overhead, Code2LoRA introduces a hypernetwork-based framework that generates repository-specific LoRA adapters. It supports two modes: Code2LoRA-Static, which creates a fixed adapter from a single repository snapshot for stable codebases, and Code2LoRA-Evo, which dynamically updates an adapter using a GRU state per code diff for evolving codebases. Evaluated on RepoPeftBench—a benchmark of 604 Python repositories with static and evolution tracks—Code2LoRA-Static achieved 63.8% cross-repo and 66.2% in-repo exact match on static tasks, matching per-repository LoRA performance, while Code2LoRA-Evo scored 60.3% cross-repo exact match on evolution tasks, outperforming a single shared LoRA by 5.2 percentage points.
为解决代码语言模型在软件仓库演化中适应性不足且微调成本高的问题，Code2LoRA提出一种基于超网络的动态生成方法，可为每个仓库生成专属LoRA适配器，且无需推理时额外开销。该方法支持两种模式：Code2LoRA-Static将单个仓库快照转化为适配器，适用于稳定代码库；Code2LoRA-Evo则通过GRU隐藏状态随代码差异更新适配器，适用于活跃开发场景。在RepoPeftBench基准（含604个Python仓库，含静态与演化任务）上，Code2LoRA-Static在静态任务中达到63.8%跨仓库、66.2%内仓库精确匹配，匹配单仓库LoRA上限；Code2LoRA-Evo在演化任务中达到60.3%跨仓库精确匹配，较单一共享LoRA提升5.2个百分点。
TempoVLA: Learning Speed-Controllable Vision-Language-Action Policies
Authors: Dong Jing, Jingchen Nie, Tianqi Zhang, Jiaqi Liu, Huaxiu Yao, Zhiwu Lu, Mingyu Ding
First: 2026-06-04T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:59:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot manipulation alternates between low-risk transit phases that call for fast execution and high-risk contact stages that demand slow, precise motion. Yet existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) only inherit a single fixed speed from training demonstrations. Prior efforts to accelerate VLAs through model compression, KV-cache reuse, or reinforcement learning only shift the policy from one fixed speed to another, and leave deceleration almost unexplored. We observe that the magnitude of each predicted action already governs how fast the robot moves, opening a direct route to controllable execution speed. We turn this observation into TempoVLA, a single VLA whose execution speed is controlled by an explicit condition. TempoVLA combines two coupled components. (1) A data-side Variable-Speed Trajectory Augmentation (VSTA) that re-times demonstration to any target speed by merging or splitting actions while preserving its motion semantics. (2) A model-side conditioning mechanism that feeds the speed to the policy. Statistics show that VSTA reaches the requested speed with negligible motion error. Experiments in simulation and on real-world tasks demonstrate that TempoVLA achieves flexible speed control in both directions, while VSTA additionally boosts the default $1\times$ performance via better data utilization. Furthermore, by cooperating with a large multimodal model, TempoVLA realizes dynamic speed control, accelerating through low-risk phases and decelerating for high-risk ones.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TempoVLA：学习速度可控的视觉-语言-动作策略
机器人操作在低风险移动阶段需要快速执行，而在高风险接触阶段则需缓慢精确的动作。然而现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）仅从训练演示中继承单一固定速度。此前通过模型压缩、KV缓存复用或强化学习加速VLA的方法，仅将策略从一个固定速度切换到另一个固定速度，对减速几乎未作探索。我们观察到，每个预测动作的幅度已能直接控制机器人运动速度，从而提供了一条可控执行速度的直接路径。我们据此提出TempoVLA，一种通过显式条件控制执行速度的单一VLA。TempoVLA包含两个耦合组件：（1）数据侧的可变速度轨迹增强（VSTA），通过合并或拆分动作，将演示重新时序化至任意目标速度，同时保留其运动语义；（2）模型侧的条件机制，将速度输入策略。统计表明，VSTA在达到目标速度时运动误差极小。仿真与真实任务实验表明，TempoVLA在双向实现灵活速度控制，且VSTA通过更优的数据利用进一步提升了默认1×性能。此外，TempoVLA与大型多模态模型协同，实现动态速度控制：在低风险阶段加速，在高风险阶段减速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to adjust motion speed dynamically between fast transit and slow precision phases, TempoVLA introduces a single vision-language-action policy that can control execution speed via explicit conditioning. It combines Variable-Speed Trajectory Augmentation (VSTA), which re-times training demonstrations to any target speed while preserving motion semantics, with a model-side conditioning mechanism that incorporates speed as input. Experimental results show VSTA achieves target speeds with minimal motion error, and TempoVLA enables flexible speed control in both directions, improving default performance through better data utilization and enabling dynamic speed adaptation in collaboration with large multimodal models.
为使机器人能在高速移动与低速精准操作间灵活切换，TempoVLA提出一种通过显式条件控制单个视觉-语言-动作模型执行速度的方法。该方法结合了变量速度轨迹增强（VSTA）——通过合并或拆分动作重定时演示轨迹并保留运动语义——与模型侧的速度条件机制。实验表明，VSTA可实现目标速度且运动误差极小，TempoVLA在仿真与真实任务中支持双向速度控制，通过更优的数据利用提升默认性能，并在与大模型协同时实现动态速度调节，高速通过低风险阶段、减速应对高风险阶段。
Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games
Authors: Mingyang Liu, Asuman Ozdaglar, Tiancheng Yu, Kaiqing Zhang
First: 2026-06-04T17:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:59:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重复博弈中对抗自适应对手的后悔最小化
本文研究了在具有\emph{自适应}对手的重复博弈中的后悔最小化问题，这些对手可根据游戏历史做出反应。在线学习中标准的\emph{外部后悔}度量无法捕捉此类自适应性。为考虑玩家的反事实推理，我们引入了{\tt 重复策略后悔（RP-后悔）}，这是一种博弈论度量，衡量当所有玩家均可\emph{响应}游戏历史时，\emph{实际实现}与\emph{事后最优}累积效用之间的差异。与现有该设定下的后悔概念相比，我们的度量天然适用于重复博弈，允许更强的比较对象和更少约束的对手，同时在所有玩家最小化该度量时仍保留找到更好均衡的可能性。我们首先确定了获得{\tt RP-后悔}随时间呈次线性增长的必要条件，涉及玩家比较策略在后悔定义中的变化，以及比较对象和对手策略的记忆。接着，我们研究了额外条件和可证明的算法以最小化{\tt RP-后悔}，该度量在策略空间中本质上是\emph{非凸}的。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了三种算法：(i) 一种基于优化算子的算法，如部分先前在线非凸学习工作所假设；(ii) 一种在每轮迭代中最小化{\tt RP-后悔}的凸且\emph{线性化}代理函数的算法；(iii) 一种当对手策略缓慢变化时直接最小化{\tt RP-后悔}的算法。此外，当所有玩家均可运行最小化{\tt RP-后悔}（或其线性化变体）的算法时，可学习到重复博弈的某些子博弈完美均衡。我们还提供了实验，表明最小化我们的后悔度量可在如猎鹿博弈等游戏中引导出更高效用的更合作解。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses regret minimization in repeated games where opponents adaptively respond to players' past actions, a scenario where standard external regret fails. To capture this adaptivity, the authors introduce Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret), a game-theoretic metric comparing realized utility against the best-in-hindsight utility under responsive strategies. They derive necessary conditions for sublinear RP-Regret and propose three algorithms: one using an optimization oracle, one minimizing a convex linearized surrogate, and one for slowly-changing opponents. Experiments show that minimizing RP-Regret leads to higher utility and more cooperative outcomes in games like Stag-Hunt.
为了解决标准外部后悔在应对适应性对手的重复博弈中的不足，本文提出了重复策略后悔（RP-后悔），一种通过比较实际效用与历史最优效用来衡量策略响应能力的游戏理论指标。作者推导了RP-后悔次线性收敛的必要条件，并提出了三种算法：基于优化算子的算法、最小化凸线性近似代理的算法，以及针对对手策略缓慢变化的直接优化算法。实验表明，最小化RP-后悔能在如猎鹿博弈等游戏中实现更高效用与更合作的均衡，验证了其在适应性博弈中的实用价值。
PAR3D: A Unified 3D-MLLM with Part-Aware Representation for Scene Understanding
Authors: Shaohui Dai, Yansong Qu, You Shen, Shengchuan Zhang, Liujuan Cao
First: 2026-06-04T17:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:59:04+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://atrovast.github.io/PAR3D/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in 3D multimodal large language models (3D-MLLMs) have enabled unified solutions for 3D scene understanding tasks, including visual question answering, captioning, and referring segmentation. However, existing 3D-MLLMs remain largely object-centric, limiting their ability to model fine-grained part structures that are essential for embodied interaction with 3D environments. In this work, we present PAR3D, a unified part-aware 3D-MLLM framework that enables models to understand, reason about, and ground both objects and their parts in 3D scenes. To enable training and evaluation of part-aware 3D scene understanding, we introduce ScenePart, a synthetic 3D scene dataset with part-level annotations and language instructions. We further develop Part-Aware 3D Representation Learning to enrich 3D visual representations with fine-grained part-level semantics, and propose Hierarchical Segmentation Query Generation to ground part targets via hierarchical object-part queries. Extensive experiments show that our method substantially improves part-level question answering and referring segmentation, while also achieving strong performance across object-level vision-language tasks.

ProfiliTable: Profiling-Driven Tabular Data Processing via Agentic Workflows
Authors: Wei Liu, Yang Gu, Xi Yan, Zihan Nan, Beicheng Xu, Keyao Ding, Bin Cui, Wentao Zhang
First: 2026-05-12T16:42:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:58:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Table processing-including cleaning, transformation, augmentation, and matching-is a foundational yet error-prone stage in real-world data pipelines. While recent LLM-based approaches show promise for automating such tasks, they often struggle in practice due to ambiguous instructions, complex task structures, and the lack of structured feedback, resulting in syntactically correct but semantically flawed code. To address these challenges, we propose ProfiliTable, an autonomous multi-agent framework centered on dynamic profiling, which constructs and iteratively refines a unified execution context through interactive exploration, knowledge-augmented synthesis, and feedback-driven refinement. ProfiliTable integrates (i) a Profiler that performs ReAct-style data exploration to build semantic understanding, (ii) a Generator that retrieves curated operators to synthesize task-aware code, and (iii) an Evaluator-Summarizer loop that injects execution scores and diagnostic insights to enable closed-loop refinement. Extensive experiments on a diverse benchmark covering 18 tabular task types demonstrate that ProfiliTable consistently outperforms strong baselines, particularly in complex multi-step scenarios. These results highlight the critical role of dynamic profiling in reliably translating ambiguous user intents into robust and governance-compliant table transformations.

标题：ProfiliTable：基于代理工作流的面向表数据的配置驱动处理
表处理——包括清洗、转换、增强和匹配——是现实世界数据管道中基础但易出错的阶段。尽管近期基于大语言模型的方法在自动化此类任务方面展现出潜力，但实际应用中常因指令模糊、任务结构复杂及缺乏结构化反馈而表现不佳，导致生成的代码语法正确但语义错误。为应对这些挑战，我们提出ProfiliTable，一个以动态配置为中心的自主多代理框架，通过交互式探索、知识增强合成和反馈驱动优化，逐步构建并迭代精炼统一的执行上下文。ProfiliTable集成了三部分：(i) 一个执行ReAct风格数据探索的Profiler，用于构建语义理解；(ii) 一个检索精选操作符并合成任务感知代码的Generator；(iii) 一个Evaluator-Summarizer循环，注入执行评分与诊断洞察，实现闭环优化。在涵盖18种表任务类型的多样化基准测试中，大量实验表明ProfiliTable持续优于强基线方法，尤其在复杂多步骤场景中表现突出。这些结果凸显了动态配置在将模糊用户意图可靠转化为稳健且符合治理规范的表转换中的关键作用。

Operation-Guided Progressive Human-to-AI Text Transformation Benchmark for Multi-Granularity AI-Text Detection
Authors: Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat, Jiacheng Liu, Xiaohan Zhao, Tianjun Yao, Xinyi Shang, Yi Tang, Jiacheng Cui, Ahmed Elhagry, Salwa K. Al Khatib, Hao Li, Salman Khan, Zhiqiang Shen
First: 2026-06-04T17:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:58:05+00:00
Comments: Our code and data are available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As AI writing assistants become increasingly integrated into real-world drafting and revision workflows, many documents are no longer purely human-written or AI-generated, but instead result from progressive human-AI co-editing. However, existing AI-text detection benchmarks largely focus on final outputs and provide limited understanding of how AI authorship signals emerge, accumulate, or disappear throughout the revision process. We introduce OpAI-Bench, an operation-guided benchmark for studying progressive human-to-AI text transformation across document, sentence, token, and span granularities. Starting from human-written documents, OpAI-Bench constructs nine sequentially revised versions for each sample under predefined AI coverage levels and five representative AI edit operations, covering four domains while preserving complete authorship provenance at multiple granularities. The benchmark supports comprehensive evaluation with 8 document-level detectors, 7 sentence-level detectors, and 2 fine-grained token/span-level detectors. Experiments reveal that AI-text detectability is governed not only by the proportion of AI-edited content, but also by edit operation, domain, and cumulative revision history. Interestingly, we notice that mixed-authorship intermediate versions are often harder to detect than both fully human and heavily AI-edited endpoints, exposing non-monotonic detection patterns missed by existing benchmarks. OpAI-Bench provides a controlled testbed for analyzing whether, when, and how AI-assisted writing becomes detectable under realistic progressive editing scenarios. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench.

随着AI写作助手日益融入现实中的起草与修订工作流，许多文档已非纯人工撰写或纯AI生成，而是由人类与AI协同编辑逐步形成。然而，现有AI文本检测基准主要关注最终输出，对AI作者身份信号在修订过程中如何产生、累积或消失缺乏深入理解。我们提出OpAI-Bench，一个基于操作引导的基准，用于研究文档、句子、词元和片段粒度下的人类到AI文本渐进式转换。从人工撰写文档出发，OpAI-Bench在预设AI覆盖水平和五类代表性AI编辑操作下，为每个样本构建九个顺序修订版本，覆盖四个领域，同时在多粒度下完整保留作者身份溯源。该基准支持全面评估，包含8个文档级检测器、7个句子级检测器和2个细粒度词元/片段级检测器。实验表明，AI文本可检测性不仅取决于AI编辑内容比例，还受编辑操作、领域和累积修订历史影响。有趣的是，我们发现混合作者身份的中间版本往往比纯人工或高度AI编辑的端点更难检测，揭示了现有基准所忽略的非单调检测模式。OpAI-Bench为分析AI辅助写作在现实渐进式编辑场景下何时、如何及是否可被检测提供可控测试平台。代码与基准见https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench。

As AI writing assistants increasingly participate in real-world document editing, documents often result from progressive human-AI collaboration rather than being purely human or AI-generated. To address the gap in understanding how AI authorship signals evolve during revision, the authors introduce OpAI-Bench, a benchmark that generates nine sequentially revised versions per document using five AI edit operations and varying AI coverage levels across four domains. The benchmark supports evaluation at document, sentence, token, and span granularities using 17 detectors. Experimental results show that AI-text detectability depends not only on AI content proportion but also on edit type, domain, and revision history, with intermediate mixed-authorship versions often being harder to detect than final human or AI-dominated versions, revealing non-monotonic detection patterns absent in prior benchmarks.

DNQ: Deep Nash Q-Network for Partially Observable n-Player Games
Authors: Qintong Xie, Edward Koh, Xavier Cadet, Peter Chin
First: 2026-06-04T17:58:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:58:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many real-world competitive systems require multiple decision-makers to act simultaneously under shared constraints, limited information, and repeated interaction, as in auctions, resource allocation, and security competition. We study multi-turn simultaneous bidding as a controlled testbed for such problems and propose DNQ, a solver-in-the-loop equilibrium supervision framework for training bidding agents. DNQ alternates between trajectory collection, critic-based payoff estimation, equilibrium computation, and policy imitation. At each visited state, a shared critic predicts either pairwise payoff matrices or an exact N-player payoff tensor, an external solver computes equilibrium strategies, and the agents are trained by minimizing the KL divergence between their masked policies and the solver-derived equilibrium targets. We focus on a scalable pairwise formulation that greatly reduces equilibrium-solving cost and training time compared with the exact formulation, while the shared critic amortizes payoff learning across agents and states. Experiments compare the pairwise and exact variants using critic loss, policy entropy, bidding resource usage, and training cost, showing that the pairwise method scales to larger numbers of agents, whereas the exact method becomes computationally impractical as the joint game grows. These results illustrate the trade-off between strategic fidelity and scalability in repeated competitive environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DNQ：用于部分可观测n人博弈的深度纳什Q网络
许多现实世界竞争系统要求多个决策者在共享约束、信息有限和重复互动下同时行动，如拍卖、资源分配和安全竞争。我们将多轮同时竞价作为此类问题的受控测试平台，提出DNQ，一种嵌入求解器的均衡监督框架，用于训练竞价代理。DNQ在轨迹收集、基于批评者的收益估计、均衡计算和策略模仿之间交替进行。在每个访问状态，共享批评者预测成对收益矩阵或精确的n人收益张量，外部求解器计算均衡策略，代理通过最小化其掩码策略与求解器导出的均衡目标之间的KL散度进行训练。我们专注于一种可扩展的成对形式，相比精确形式大幅降低了均衡求解成本和训练时间，同时共享批评者在代理和状态间摊销收益学习。实验通过批评者损失、策略熵、竞价资源使用和训练成本比较成对与精确变体，表明成对方法可扩展至更多代理，而精确方法随着联合博弈规模增长变得计算上不可行。这些结果体现了在重复竞争环境中策略保真度与可扩展性之间的权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model multi-agent strategic interactions under partial observability and repeated play, such as in auctions and security games, this paper introduces DNQ, a framework that combines trajectory collection, critic-based payoff estimation, equilibrium computation, and policy imitation to train bidding agents. DNQ uses a shared critic to predict either pairwise payoff matrices or an N-player payoff tensor, then leverages an external solver to compute equilibrium strategies, training agents by minimizing KL divergence between their masked policies and equilibrium targets. Experiments show that the scalable pairwise formulation significantly reduces computational cost and training time while maintaining competitive performance, whereas the exact N-player formulation becomes intractable as the number of agents increases, highlighting the trade-off between strategic accuracy and scalability.
为解决现实世界中如拍卖、资源分配和安全博弈等多智能体在信息不完整环境下同时决策的问题，本文提出DNQ框架，结合轨迹收集、基于评论家的收益估计、均衡计算与策略模仿训练智能体。该方法通过共享评论家预测成对收益矩阵或N人收益张量，再借助外部求解器计算均衡策略，通过最小化策略与均衡目标间的KL散度进行训练。实验表明，可扩展的成对形式显著降低计算开销并支持更多智能体训练，而精确的N人形式在智能体数量增加时变得计算不可行，凸显了策略精确性与可扩展性之间的权衡。
Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence
Authors: Akarsh Kumar, Phillip Isola
First: 2026-06-04T17:57:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:57:33+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 23 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需循环的循环网络预训练
训练循环神经网络（RNN）需要在长序列计算中分配梯度。标准的时序反向传播（BPTT）对此处理不佳：其在时间上是串行的，限制了并行性，且易出现梯度消失或爆炸问题，导致难以学习长距离关联。我们提出监督记忆训练（SMT），一种训练非线性RNN的方法，通过将RNN训练简化为对单步记忆转移标签（(m_t, x_{t+1}) → m_{t+1}）的监督学习，完全绕过循环梯度传播。SMT通过训练基于Transformer的编码器以预测状态为目标来获取这些记忆标签——仅保留预测未来所需的历史信息。通过将“记忆什么”与“如何更新记忆”解耦，SMT实现了时间并行的RNN训练，且任意两个标记间梯度路径稳定为O(1)长度——无需展开RNN。我们在语言建模和像素序列建模等任务上预训练各种RNN架构时发现，SMT优于BPTT。SMT使非线性RNN能更好地捕捉长距离依赖关系，并支持并行训练，有望解锁构建过去经验时间抽象模型的规模扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of backpropagation through time in training RNNs—namely sequential computation, poor parallelism, and gradient instability—this work introduces Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method that bypasses recurrent credit assignment by treating RNN training as supervised learning on one-step memory transitions. SMT generates memory labels using a Transformer encoder trained to predict future states from past inputs, decoupling memory content from update mechanics. This enables time-parallel training with stable gradient paths of constant length, without unrolling the network. Experiments show SMT outperforms BPTT in pretraining RNNs on language modeling and pixel sequence tasks, allowing nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and scale more effectively.
为了解决传统BPTT训练RNN时存在的序列依赖、并行性差和梯度不稳定问题，本文提出监督记忆训练（SMT）方法，通过将RNN训练转化为对单步记忆转移标签的监督学习，绕过循环信用传播。SMT利用Transformer编码器预测未来状态以生成记忆标签，实现记忆内容与更新机制的解耦。实验表明，SMT在语言建模和像素序列建模等任务上优于BPTT，支持稳定并行训练、O(1)梯度路径及更优的长程依赖建模能力。
Complexity-Balanced Diffusion Splitting
Authors: Noam Issachar, Dani Lischinski, Raanan Fattal
First: 2026-06-04T17:57:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:57:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Standard continuous-time generative models rely on monolithic architectures that must navigate vastly different signal regimes, from isotropic noise to intricate data distributions. While scaling model capacity improves performance, deploying a massive network uniformly across the entire generative timeline is inherently inefficient. In this work, we propose Complexity-Balanced Splitting (CBS), a principled framework for temporal capacity allocation that distributes the generative workload across multiple specialized sub-networks. Grounded in function approximation theory and de Boor's equidistribution principle, CBS partitions the diffusion timeline into segments of equal approximation burden, allocating more representational capacity to regions where the generative dynamics are more difficult to model. To estimate this local complexity, we introduce two complementary and tractable monitor functions: a spatial measure based on the flow's Dirichlet energy, and a geometric measure based on the acceleration of the sampling trajectories. Using a lightweight auxiliary model to estimate these complexity profiles, our approach eliminates the need for heuristic temporal splits or computationally expensive search procedures. Extensive evaluation across multiple architectures (SiT, JiT, and UNet) and datasets demonstrates that CBS consistently improves synthesis quality without increasing per-step inference cost. In particular, CBS improves FID by ~35% on SiT-XL with CFG relative to naive temporal partitioning. Project page is available at https://noamissachar.github.io/CBS/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：复杂度平衡扩散分割
标准连续时间生成模型依赖于单一架构，需在从各向同性噪声到复杂数据分布的巨大信号范围内进行导航。虽然扩大模型容量可提升性能，但在整个生成时间线上均匀部署大规模网络本质上是低效的。本文提出复杂度平衡分割（CBS），一种用于时间容量分配的原理性框架，将生成任务分配至多个专用子网络。CBS基于函数逼近理论和de Boor等分布原则，将扩散时间线划分为等复杂度负担的段落，并在生成动态更难建模的区域分配更多表示能力。为估计局部复杂度，我们引入两种互补且可计算的监控函数：基于流的狄利克雷能量的空间度量，以及基于采样轨迹加速度的几何度量。通过轻量级辅助模型估计这些复杂度分布，我们的方法无需启发式时间分割或计算昂贵的搜索过程。在多种架构（SiT、JiT和UNet）和数据集上的广泛评估表明，CBS在不增加每步推理成本的前提下持续提升合成质量。尤其在SiT-XL与CFG对比下，CBS相较朴素时间分割将FID提升约35%。项目页面见https://noamissachar.github.io/CBS/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform capacity allocation in diffusion models that must handle vastly different signal regimes, this work introduces Complexity-Balanced Splitting (CBS), a framework that dynamically distributes generative workload across specialized sub-networks based on local complexity. CBS uses two tractable monitor functions — Dirichlet energy and trajectory acceleration — to estimate regional approximation burden, then allocates more capacity where modeling is harder. Evaluated across SiT, JiT, and UNet architectures, CBS consistently improves synthesis quality without increasing per-step inference cost, achieving a ~35% FID reduction on SiT-XL with CFG compared to naive temporal partitioning.
为了解决扩散模型在处理从各向同性噪声到复杂数据分布的多样化信号时，统一容量分配造成的效率低下问题，本文提出复杂度平衡分割（CBS）框架，根据局部生成复杂度动态分配模型容量。CBS通过两个可计算的监控函数——狄利克雷能量和轨迹加速度——评估各区域的近似负担，并将时间线划分为复杂度负载均衡的段落。该方法借助轻量级辅助模型估算复杂度分布，无需启发式分割或昂贵搜索。在SiT、JiT和UNet架构上的实验表明，CBS在不增加每步推理成本的前提下持续提升生成质量，尤其在SiT-XL与CFG配置下FID降低约35%。
Thinking with Imagination: Agentic Visual Spatial Reasoning with World Simulators
Authors: Chenming Zhu, Jingli Lin, Yilin Long, Peizhou Cao, Tai Wang, Jiangmiao Pang, Xihui Liu
First: 2026-06-04T17:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:56:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://zcmax.github.io/projects/Thinking-With-Imagination
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning capabilities, their spatial reasoning abilities remain largely constrained to the observed images and text-oriented chain-of-thought. They often struggle to infer unobserved layouts, maintain cross-view consistency, and reason from alternative viewpoints when only limited egocentric observations are available. In this work, we study this problem as thinking with imagination, where a VLM actively acquires imagined visual evidence by interacting with a world simulator during reasoning. We propose Astra, an agentic spatial reasoning framework that empowers VLMs with action-conditioned visual imagination. Specifically, Astra couples Astra-VL, an RL-trained VLM policy, with Astra-WM, a Bagel-based world simulator that generates novel-view observations from context images and natural-language camera motions. To provide reliable imagined evidence, Astra-WM is trained with view consistency tuning to improve pose and content consistency across views. In the RL stage, we propose a world-simulator-in-the-loop two-phase RL curriculum to stabilize tool-use exploration and advance the model's ability to invoke the simulator only when imagined observations improve over direct answering. Experiments demonstrate that both the world simulator and the agentic policy are necessary: Astra-WM improves simulator-augmented Gemini-3-Flash on MMSI-Bench from 45.1 to 49.5, while Astra-VL improves the Qwen3-VL backbone from 29.8 to 38.8 on MMSI-Bench and from 36.8 to 42.7 on MindCube. These results show that imagined observations can provide useful spatial evidence, but effective world-model-augmented reasoning requires learning when, where, and how to imagine.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以想象为思维：结合世界模拟器的代理视觉空间推理
尽管视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在视觉推理方面表现强劲，但其空间推理能力仍主要受限于观察到的图像和文本导向的思维链。它们在仅有限的以自我为中心观测数据时，往往难以推断未观察到的布局、保持跨视角一致性，并从替代视角进行推理。本研究将此问题视为“以想象为思维”，即VLM在推理过程中通过与世界模拟器交互主动获取想象的视觉证据。我们提出Astra，一个赋予VLM动作条件视觉想象能力的代理空间推理框架。具体而言，Astra将Astra-VL（基于强化学习训练的VLM策略）与Astra-WM（基于Bagel的世界模拟器）相结合，后者可根据上下文图像和自然语言描述的相机运动生成新视角观测。为提供可靠的想象证据，Astra-WM通过视角一致性调优训练，以提升不同视角间的姿态与内容一致性。在强化学习阶段，我们提出一种“世界模拟器嵌入循环”的双阶段强化学习课程，以稳定工具使用探索，并提升模型仅在想象观测优于直接回答时才调用模拟器的能力。实验表明，世界模拟器和代理策略均不可或缺：Astra-WM使模拟器增强的Gemini-3-Flash在MMSI-Bench上从45.1提升至49.5；Astra-VL使Qwen3-VL主干在MMSI-Bench上从29.8提升至38.8，在MindCube上从36.8提升至42.7。结果表明，想象观测可提供有用的空间证据，但有效的世界模型增强推理需要学习何时、何地以及如何想象。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in spatial reasoning under limited egocentric observations, this work introduces Astra, an agentic framework enabling VLMs to generate imagined visual evidence via interaction with a world simulator. Astra combines Astra-VL, an RL-trained policy, with Astra-WM, a Bagel-based simulator trained for view consistency, to produce novel-view observations from context images and natural-language camera motions. The RL curriculum incorporates a world-simulator-in-the-loop mechanism to stabilize exploration and encourage simulator use only when beneficial. Experiments show that Astra-WM boosts Gemini-3-Flash from 45.1 to 49.5 on MMSI-Bench, and Astra-VL elevates Qwen3-VL from 29.8 to 38.8 on MMSI-Bench and from 36.8 to 42.7 on MindCube, demonstrating that imagined views enhance spatial reasoning but require learned control over when and how to imagine.
为解决视觉语言模型在有限第一人称观测下空间推理能力不足的问题，本文提出Astra框架，使模型能通过与世界模拟器交互生成想象视觉证据。该框架结合了通过强化学习训练的Astra-VL策略与基于Bagel的Astra-WM模拟器，后者从上下文图像和语言引导的相机运动生成新视角观测，并通过视角一致性训练提升视觉一致性。框架采用两阶段强化学习课程，引导模型仅在想象视角优于直接推理时调用模拟器。实验表明，Astra-WM使Gemini-3-Flash在MMSI-Bench上从45.1提升至49.5，Astra-VL使Qwen3-VL在MMSI-Bench上从29.8提升至38.8，在MindCube上从36.8提升至42.7，证明想象观测可增强空间推理，但需学习何时、何地、如何想象。
RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models
Authors: Mykyta Ielanskyi, Kajetan Schweighofer, Lukas Aichberger, Sepp Hochreiter
First: 2026-06-04T17:56:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:56:31+00:00
Comments: Preprint, under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RREDCoT：面向推理模型的段级奖励重分配
近期推理语言模型的进步主要依赖强化学习（RL）微调，多数方法采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）或其变体，引导模型生成思维链（CoT）轨迹。由于最终答案需在CoT轨迹完成后才能验证并分配奖励，这构成了延迟奖励问题。GRPO及其变体对应标准RL中的蒙特卡洛方法，已知其方差较高。一种可能的解决方案是通过信用分配重分配奖励，即强调对达成理想解至关重要的CoT轨迹段落并赋予更高奖励。虽然蒙特卡洛采样可用于无偏估计中间状态值，但其计算开销使其不适合在长上下文和高粒度训练中进行信用分配。我们提出RREDCoT（思维链的奖励重分配），利用模型自身近似最优奖励重分配，无需额外生成。我们研究了该方法相较于蒙特卡洛采样及多种归因方法的优势，并进一步分析了构建重分配过程中的若干关键因素，如CoT轨迹分段与状态值估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high variance in Monte Carlo reward estimation during RL fine-tuning of reasoning models, this work proposes RREDCoT, a method that redistributes rewards at the segment level within Chain-of-Thought traces without requiring additional generations. Instead of relying on Monte Carlo sampling, RREDCoT uses the model itself to approximate optimal reward redistribution, enabling efficient credit assignment for intermediate steps. Experimental results show that RREDCoT outperforms Monte Carlo methods and several attribution-based baselines, particularly in long-context settings, while maintaining computational feasibility during training.
为了解决强化学习微调推理模型时蒙特卡洛采样带来的高方差问题，本文提出RREDCoT方法，通过在思维链的段落级别进行奖励重分配，无需额外生成。该方法利用模型自身近似最优奖励重分配，从而在训练时高效完成信用分配。实验表明，RREDCoT在长上下文场景下显著优于蒙特卡洛采样及其他归因方法，同时保持计算效率。
Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Paul Jünger, Justin Lovelace, Linxi Zhao, Dongyoung Go, Kilian Q. Weinberger
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-04T17:56:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:56:27+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion language models generate text by iteratively denoising an entire response in parallel. At each step, they predict tentative tokens for every masked position, committing the confident predictions to the output and discarding the unconfident ones. We show that the discarded tokens are in fact a useful lookahead signal for retrieval-augmented generation: even low-confidence tokens often surface salient entities early in the denoising trajectory, enabling retrieval of stronger evidence before the output is finalized. We exploit this through Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models (SARDI), a dynamic RAG framework that uses these lookahead tokens to guide retrieval during denoising. SARDI is training-free, retriever-agnostic, and applicable to any reasoning-capable discrete diffusion language model. Across five multi-hop QA benchmarks, SARDI outperforms current training-free diffusion and autoregressive retrieval baselines at up to $8\times$ higher throughput.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散语言模型的自增强检索
离散扩散语言模型通过并行去噪整个响应来生成文本。在每一步，它们为每个被遮蔽位置预测临时标记，将置信度高的预测提交到输出，而丢弃置信度低的预测。我们发现，这些被丢弃的标记实际上对检索增强生成具有有用的前瞻信号：即使置信度低的标记也常在去噪轨迹早期揭示重要实体，从而在输出最终确定前就能检索到更强的证据。我们通过扩散语言模型的自增强检索（SARDI）框架利用这一特性，SARDI是一种动态RAG框架，利用这些前瞻标记在去噪过程中引导检索。SARDI无需训练、与检索器无关，适用于任何具备推理能力的离散扩散语言模型。在五个多跳问答基准测试中，SARDI在吞吐量高达现有无训练扩散和自回归检索基线的8倍的情况下表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that low-confidence tokens discarded during the denoising process of discrete diffusion language models often contain early signals of salient entities, the authors propose Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models (SARDI), a training-free, retriever-agnostic framework that leverages these lookahead tokens to guide retrieval during generation. SARDI dynamically integrates retrieval evidence based on token confidence levels, enabling more informed generation without requiring retraining or specific retriever architectures. Experimental results across five multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that SARDI outperforms existing training-free diffusion and autoregressive retrieval baselines while achieving up to 8x higher throughput.
鉴于离散扩散语言模型在去噪过程中被舍弃的低置信度词元往往包含早期显著实体，作者提出了自增强检索框架SARDI，该框架无需训练、不依赖特定检索器，利用这些前瞻词元动态引导生成过程中的检索。SARDI在生成过程中根据不断演化的词元预测动态引入检索证据，从而提升响应质量。在五个多跳问答基准测试中，SARDI显著优于现有无训练扩散与自回归检索基线，最高吞吐量提升达8倍，同时保持或提升了准确率。
MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery
Authors: Shangheng Du, Xiangchao Yan, Jinxin Shi, Zongsheng Cao, Shiyang Feng, Zichen Liang, Boyuan Sun, Tianshuo Peng, Yifan Zhou, Xin Li, Jie Zhou, Liang He, Bo Zhang, Lei Bai
First: 2026-06-04T17:55:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:55:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MLEvolve：一种用于自动化机器学习算法发现的自演进框架
大语言模型（LLM）代理正越来越多地应用于科学发现和机器学习工程（MLE）等长周期任务，其中持续的自演进能力成为关键。然而，现有MLE代理存在分支间信息隔离、无记忆搜索及缺乏层次化控制等问题，共同阻碍了长周期优化。我们提出MLEvolve，这是一种基于LLM的自演进多代理框架，用于端到端机器学习算法发现。通过将树搜索扩展为渐进式MCGS，MLEvolve借助基于图的参考边实现跨分支信息流动，并通过受熵启发的渐进式调度，逐步从广泛探索转向聚焦利用。为使代理能随经验积累而演进，我们引入回顾性记忆，结合冷启动领域知识库与动态全局记忆，实现任务特定经验的检索与重用。为确保长周期迭代的稳定性，我们进一步将战略规划与代码生成解耦，并采用自适应编码模式。在MLE-Bench上的评估表明，MLEvolve在12小时预算（标准运行时间的一半）下，在平均奖牌率和有效提交率等多个维度均达到最先进水平。此外，MLEvolve在数学算法优化任务上也优于专用算法发现方法（如AlphaEvolve），展现出强大的跨领域泛化能力。代码开源于https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for sustained self-evolution in long-horizon machine learning engineering tasks, MLEvolve introduces a self-evolving multi-agent framework powered by large language models. It employs Progressive MCGS tree search with graph-based reference edges to enable cross-branch information flow and gradually transitions from exploration to exploitation via an entropy-inspired schedule. Retrospective Memory integrates domain knowledge with dynamic global memory to allow experience reuse, while decoupling strategic planning from code generation via adaptive coding modes ensures stable long-horizon iteration. Experiments on MLE-Bench show MLEvolve achieves top performance in average medal rate and valid submission rate within a 12-hour budget, and outperforms AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization.
为应对长周期机器学习工程任务中持续自演化的挑战，MLEvolve提出一种基于大语言模型的自演化多智能体框架。该框架采用渐进式MCGS树搜索，通过图结构参考边实现跨分支信息流动，并借助熵启发式调度逐步从探索转向利用。引入回顾记忆机制，结合领域知识库与动态全局记忆，实现任务经验的积累与复用；同时通过自适应编码模式解耦策略规划与代码生成，保障长周期迭代稳定性。在MLE-Bench评测中，MLEvolve在12小时预算内达成最优平均奖牌率与有效提交率，且在数学算法优化任务上超越AlphaEvolve，展现出强大的跨领域泛化能力。
PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training
Authors: Senmiao Wang, Tiantian Fang, Haoran Zhang, Yushun Zhang, Kunxiang Zhao, Alex Schwing, Ruoyu Sun
First: 2026-06-04T17:55:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:55:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PC层：通过多项式预条件改善大语言模型预训练
我们提出了一种预条件（PC）层，通过多项式预条件器对权重进行参数化，确保大语言模型训练过程中权重条件稳定。PC模块通过低阶多项式预条件重塑权重矩阵的奇异值谱。训练完成后，预条件权重可合并回原始架构，不增加推理开销。我们在Llama-1B预训练中展示了该PC层相较于标准Transformer在AdamW和Muon优化器下的优势。理论上，我们通过证明：对于某些深度线性网络，均匀限制每层奇异值可确保梯度下降在全局最小值处几何收敛，从而论证了该谱控制原则。代码见https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of weight conditioning during large language model pre-training, the authors introduce a PC layer that applies polynomial preconditioning to weight matrices to stabilize their singular-value spectrum. This method reshapes the spectrum via low-degree polynomials, ensuring consistent conditioning across training layers without altering the model’s inference efficiency. Experimental results on Llama-1B pre-training show improved convergence and performance under both AdamW and Muon optimizers, with theoretical support demonstrating that uniform singular-value bounds guarantee geometric convergence for certain deep linear networks.
为解决大语言模型预训练过程中权重条件数不稳定的问题，作者提出PC层，通过低次多项式预处理调整权重矩阵的奇异值谱，确保训练过程中的稳定条件数，且无需增加推理开销。在Llama-1B预训练中，使用AdamW和Muon优化器时，该方法相较标准Transformer显著提升收敛效率与性能。理论分析证明，对深层线性网络的每一层奇异值进行均匀约束，可保证梯度下降在特定条件下几何收敛至全局最小值。
How abundant are good interpolators?
Authors: August Y. Chen, Ahmed El Alaoui
First: 2026-06-04T17:55:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:55:10+00:00
Comments: 140 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Let $S$ be the set of unit norm linear classifiers $θ\in \mathbb{R}^d$ which correctly classify every point of a labeled dataset $(X_i,y_i)_{i=1}^n$, $X_i \in \mathbb{R}^d$, $y_i \in \{-1,+1\}$, with a possibly negative margin $κ$ fixed in advance. Under two natural data-generating distributions of the $(X,y)$ pairs -- a Gaussian mixture model and a logistic model with Gaussian features -- and in the proportional regime $n/d \to α$ with small enough $α$, we establish a large deviation principle on the event that a point $θ$ chosen uniformly at random from $S$ achieves a given generalization error, with high probability over the choice of the data. The associated large deviation rate function is deterministic and describes the proportion, at the exponential scale in $d$, of interpolating classifiers having a given desired performance. As a consequence, we establish the following concentration phenomenon: all but an exponentially small fraction of interpolating classifiers have approximately the same generalization performance given by the unique maximizer of this rate function. We numerically compare this maximizer to the performance of empirical risk minimization by gradient descent and to the performance of a natural linear program, both finding a point in $S$, and deduce that in the overparametrized regime of small $α$, these efficient procedures outperform the vast majority of interpolators, pointing to their nontrivial benign overfitting in this setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：好的插值器有多丰富？
设 $S$ 为在给定标签数据集 $(X_i,y_i)_{i=1}^n$（其中 $X_i \in \mathbb{R}^d$，$y_i \in \{-1,+1\}$）上能正确分类所有点且具有预设非负边距 $κ$ 的单位范数线性分类器 $θ\in \mathbb{R}^d$ 的集合。在两种自然的数据生成分布——高斯混合模型和具有高斯特征的逻辑回归模型——以及比例极限 $n/d \to α$（其中 $α$ 足够小）下，我们建立了一个大偏差原理：当从 $S$ 中均匀随机选取一个点 $θ$ 时，其达到给定泛化误差的概率很高，且该概率服从大偏差速率函数。该速率函数是确定性的，描述了在 $d$ 的指数尺度下，具有给定期望性能的插值分类器所占比例。由此我们得出以下集中现象：几乎所有插值分类器（除指数小比例外）都具有近似相同的泛化性能，该性能由该速率函数的唯一最大值点决定。我们通过数值比较该最大值点与梯度下降法实现的经验风险最小化及一个自然线性规划的性能（两者均在 $S$ 中找到点），发现当 $α$ 较小时，在过参数化情形下，这些高效方法优于绝大多数插值器，表明其在此设定下具有非平凡的良性过拟合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the abundance and performance distribution of interpolating classifiers in high-dimensional overparameterized settings, the paper analyzes the generalization behavior of linear classifiers that perfectly fit labeled data under two data models: Gaussian mixture and logistic with Gaussian features. In the regime where sample size n scales proportionally to dimension d with small α = n/d, it derives a large deviation principle quantifying the exponential proportion of interpolators achieving a given generalization error. The rate function reveals that almost all interpolators have nearly identical performance, determined by its unique maximizer. Numerical experiments show that gradient descent and a linear program, both yielding interpolating solutions, consistently outperform the vast majority of random interpolators, indicating benign overfitting in this setting.
为理解高维过参数化场景下插值分类器的丰富性与性能分布，该研究在高斯混合与逻辑-高斯数据模型下，分析了完美拟合标注数据的单位范数线性分类器的泛化行为。在样本量n与维度d满足比例关系n/d = α且α较小时，作者推导出大偏差原理，量化了随机插值分类器达到特定泛化误差的指数概率。核心发现是，几乎所有插值分类器的泛化性能几乎相同，由大偏差速率函数的唯一最大值决定。数值实验表明，梯度下降与线性规划所生成的插值解，其性能显著优于绝大多数随机插值分类器，揭示了该场景下非平凡的良性过拟合现象。
Goedel-Architect: Streamlining Formal Theorem Proving with Blueprint Generation and Refinement
Authors: Jui-Hui Chung, Ziyang Cai, Zihao Li, Qishuo Yin, Rohit Agarwal, Simon Park, Rodrigo Porto, Narutatsu Ri, Ziran Yang, Shange Tang, Xingyu Dang, Hongzhou Lin, Mengdi Wang, Danqi Chen, Chi Jin, Liam H Fowl, Sanjeev Arora
First: 2026-06-04T17:54:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:54:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Goedel-Architect, an agentic framework for formal theorem proving in Lean 4 centered on blueprint generation and refinement. A blueprint is a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas that builds up to the main theorem. First, Goedel-Architect generates a blueprint of formally stated definitions and lemmas, along with declared dependencies. This blueprint is optionally guided by a natural language proof. Then, a tool-equipped Lean prover component closes each open lemma node in parallel using relevant dependencies. Failed lemmas in turn drive refinement of the global blueprint. This strategy contrasts with other mainstream approaches which use recursive lemma decomposition, and can inefficiently loop on dead-end strategies. Using the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) as the backbone, Goedel-Architect attains 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% pass@1 on PutnamBench. With an optional natural-language proof seeding the initial blueprint on the harder problems, we additionally close the remaining two MiniF2F-test problems (reaching 100%), lift PutnamBench to 88.8% (597/672), and solve 4/6 on IMO 2025, 11/12 on Putnam 2025, and 3/6 on USAMO 2026. This represents state-of-the-art performance for an open-source pipeline at a price point up to 500x less than comparable open-source pipelines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：哥德尔建筑师：通过蓝图生成与优化简化形式定理证明
我们提出了Goedel-Architect，一个以蓝图生成与优化为核心的Lean 4形式定理证明代理框架。蓝图是构建主定理所需定义与引理的依赖图。首先，Goedel-Architect生成形式化表述的定义与引理蓝图，以及声明的依赖关系，该蓝图可选地由自然语言证明引导。随后，配备工具的Lean证明器组件并行关闭每个开放引理节点，利用相关依赖。失败的引理则驱动全局蓝图的优化。该策略与主流递归引理分解方法不同，后者易陷入低效的死循环。以开放权重的DeepSeek-V4-Flash（284B-A13B）为骨干，Goedel-Architect在MiniF2F-test上达到99.2%的pass@1，在PutnamBench上达到75.6%的pass@1。在较难问题中，若以自然语言证明引导初始蓝图，还可额外解决MiniF2F-test剩余两个问题（达到100%），将PutnamBench提升至88.8%（597/672），并在IMO 2025中解决4/6题、Putnam 2025中解决11/12题、USAMO 2026中解决3/6题。这代表了开源管道的最先进性能，且成本仅为同类开源管道的500倍以内。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of recursive lemma decomposition in existing theorem provers, Goedel-Architect introduces a blueprint-driven approach to formal theorem proving in Lean 4, where a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas is first generated, optionally guided by natural language proofs, and then refined iteratively as failed lemmas are resolved. The system employs a parallelized Lean prover component powered by the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) to close each lemma node, with global blueprint refinement triggered by failures. Experimental results show it achieves 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% on PutnamBench, and with natural language seeding, solves all MiniF2F-test problems (100%), lifts PutnamBench to 88.8%, and solves 4/6 IMO 2025, 11/12 Putnam 2025, and 3/6 USAMO 2026 problems, outperforming prior open-source pipelines at a fraction of their cost.
为解决传统递归引理分解方法在形式定理证明中的低效问题，Goedel-Architect提出基于蓝图生成与迭代优化的框架，通过自然语言引导生成定理依赖图，再由Lean证明器并行闭合各引理节点，失败引理触发全局蓝图修正。在MiniF2F-test和PutnamBench等基准测试中，该系统实现99.2%和75.6%的pass@1准确率；结合自然语言引导后，可解决全部MiniF2F-test问题，将PutnamBench提升至88.8%，并在IMO 2025、Putnam 2025、USAMO 2026等竞赛题中取得4/6、11/12、3/6的优异成绩，展现出开源系统中顶尖性能与极低成本优势。
You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing
Authors: Yutao Sun, Yanqi Zhang, Li Dong, Jianyong Wang, Furu Wei
First: 2026-06-04T17:54:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:54:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index. A single indexer computes token-level top-k selection once and reuses the resulting index across layers, thereby preserving the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing the routing overhead. The resulting architecture improves all major inference bottlenecks jointly, including pre-filling, KV-cache storage, and long-context decoding. Experiments across short-context and long-context benchmarks show that CLSA is both accurate and efficient, achieving up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context. These results suggest a more complete architectural solution for long-context LLMs that jointly advances model quality and inference efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你只需索引一次：基于共享路由的跨层稀疏注意力
现代大语言模型在长上下文推理中，解码效率日益成为瓶颈，尤其在需要大量推理的场景中，模型需生成长的中间思维链。现有稀疏注意力方法常面临效率与质量的权衡：结构化块稀疏方法虽加速效果显著，但会带来明显质量损失；而令牌稀疏方法通常更准确，但因需在完整缓存上执行top-k路由，端到端加速有限。本文提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），基于如YOCO等KV共享架构。其核心思想是不仅在跨解码层间共享KV缓存，还共享路由索引。单个索引器仅需一次计算令牌级top-k选择，并在各层复用该索引，从而保留令牌稀疏注意力的细粒度选择性，同时摊销路由开销。该架构协同优化推理瓶颈，包括预填充、KV缓存存储及长上下文解码。在短上下文与长上下文基准测试中，CLSA兼具准确性和效率，在128K上下文下实现最高7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍整体吞吐提升。结果表明，CLSA为长上下文LLM提供了一种更全面的架构方案，同时提升模型质量与推理效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of long-context inference in large language models, especially during reasoning tasks, this work introduces cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA) to jointly improve speed and quality. CLSA builds on KV-sharing architectures and extends them by sharing a single routing index computed once per token across all decoder layers, enabling token-level top-k selection without redundant computation. This design preserves the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing routing overhead. Experiments show CLSA achieves up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context length, with minimal quality loss, demonstrating a balanced architectural solution for efficient long-context inference.
为解决大语言模型在长上下文推理中的效率瓶颈，尤其是推理密集型任务，本文提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），在KV共享架构基础上进一步共享路由索引。该方法仅在每层首次计算每个token的top-k选择，之后在所有层复用该索引，既保留了token稀疏注意力的精细选择性，又摊薄了路由开销。实验表明，CLSA在128K上下文长度下可实现最高7.6倍的解码加速和17.1倍的整体吞吐提升，且不损失精度，展现出一种兼顾模型质量和推理效率的统一架构方案。
Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?
Authors: Mandana Samiei, Eunice Yiu, Anthony GX-Chen, Dongyan Lin, Jocelyn Shen, Blake A. Richards, Alison Gopnik, Doina Precup
First: 2026-06-04T17:53:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:53:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 48th Annual Conference of the Cognitive Science Society (CogSci 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人类成人与大语言模型作为科学家：谁从主动探索中获益？
因果学习领域长期发现，成人难以识别需要多个原因同时存在的联合因果规则，而在析取性设置中表现更好。然而，大多数“联合劣势”的实验证据依赖被动观察范式，学习者无法控制证据生成。本文探讨当成人获得主动探索的自主权时，这一偏差是否依然存在。通过修改版“blicket探测器”任务，成人参与者自由干预以识别联合或析取性规则结构下的因果对象。结果表明，主动探索显著提升了成人对联合因果规则的推理能力，尽管联合规则仍需更多测试才能推断。我们进一步将人类表现与多种大语言模型在相同设置下进行比较：部分前沿模型在假设推断准确性上接近人类水平，但其探索策略效率较低，且联合与析取规则表现差距相似。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether adults’ difficulty in inferring conjunctive causal rules—where an effect requires multiple simultaneous causes—persists when they are allowed to actively explore their environment. Using a modified blicket detector task, participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under either conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. Results show that active exploration significantly improves adults’ ability to learn conjunctive rules, though they still require more trials than disjunctive ones. When compared to large language models, some models matched human accuracy in hypothesis inference but performed less efficiently in exploration and retained similar performance gaps between conjunctive and disjunctive tasks.
本研究探讨成人是否在被允许主动探索（而非被动观察）时，仍会难以推断需要多个同时原因的联合作用因果规则。通过修改的blicket探测器任务，参与者可自由干预以识别在联合作用或析取作用规则结构下的因果对象。结果表明，主动探索显著提升成人对联合作用规则的学习能力，但仍需比析取规则更多试验。与大型语言模型对比发现，部分模型在假设推断准确率上达到人类水平，但探索策略效率较低，且在联合作用与析取作用规则间的性能差距与人类相似。
Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once
Authors: Shiyun Xiong, Dongming Wu, Peiwen Sun, Yuang Ai, Bokang Yang, Wencheng Han, Xiao-Hui Li, Xiangyu Yue
First: 2026-06-04T17:52:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:52:04+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://benchmarkagent.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Benchmarks are fundamental for evaluating and advancing LLMs and MLLMs by providing standardized and explicit measures of performance. However, their construction is labor-intensive and hard to reuse, raising concerns about sustainability and scalability. Moreover, existing benchmarks often quickly reach performance saturation after their release, resulting in insufficient discrimination among state-of-the-art models. To address these challenges, we introduce Benchmark Agent, a fully autonomous agentic system designed for benchmark building. Our framework orchestrates the complete benchmark construction pipeline, from user query analysis and subtask design to data annotation and quality control. To assess Benchmark Agent, we implement it to produce 15 representative benchmarks, spanning diverse evaluation scenarios, including text understanding, multimodal understanding, and domain-specific reasoning. Extensive experiments, including human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and consistency checks, demonstrate Benchmark Agent can generate high-quality benchmark samples with minimal human involvement. More importantly, through continual evaluation, we observe several insightful findings, including that current models struggle with certain domain-specific reasoning tasks. We believe that rapidly evolving benchmarks can contribute significantly to the research community. The preview and code will be publicly available at the demo page and code repository.

标题：全方位基准评测系统
基准评测是评估和推动大语言模型（LLMs）及多模态大模型（MLLMs）发展的基础，提供标准化、明确的性能度量。然而，基准构建过程耗时费力且难以复用，引发对可持续性和可扩展性的担忧。此外，现有基准在发布后往往迅速达到性能饱和，导致顶尖模型间区分度不足。为应对这些挑战，我们引入Benchmark Agent，一个完全自主的智能体系统，专用于基准构建。我们的框架统筹整个基准构建流程，涵盖用户查询分析、子任务设计、数据标注与质量控制。为评估Benchmark Agent，我们将其应用于生成15个代表性基准，覆盖文本理解、多模态理解及领域特定推理等多样化评估场景。大量实验，包括人工评估、LLM作为裁判评估及一致性检查，表明Benchmark Agent可生成高质量基准样本，且需极少人工参与。更重要的是，通过持续评估，我们观察到若干重要发现，如当前模型在某些领域特定推理任务上表现不佳。我们相信，快速演进的基准将显著推动研究社区的发展。预览与代码将在演示页面和代码仓库公开。
