Evaluated by generating 15 diverse benchmarks across text, multimodal, and domain-specific reasoning tasks, the system demonstrates high-quality output with minimal human input, validated through human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessments, and consistency checks. Key findings include persistent performance gaps in domain-specific reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for dynamic, evolving benchmarks to better discriminate state-of-the-art models.
为应对现有基准构建耗时费力、难以复用且易达性能饱和的问题，作者提出Benchmark Agent，一个可自主完成从需求分析、子任务设计到数据标注与质量控制全流程的智能系统。通过生成涵盖文本理解、多模态理解及领域特定推理等15个代表性基准，实验表明该系统能以极低人工干预产出高质量样本，经人工评估、LLM裁判评估及一致性检验验证。主要发现揭示当前模型在部分领域推理任务上表现薄弱，凸显动态演进基准对推动大语言模型研究的重要价值。
Flow-based Policy Adaptation without Policy Updates
Authors: Luzhe Sun, Jingtian Ji, Haoran Chen, Jiawei Zhou, Matthew R. Walter
First: 2026-06-04T17:51:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:51:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Leveraging prior knowledge from pretrained policies, foundation models, or human operators offers an efficient alternative to learning robot skills from scratch. However, these agents often provide actions that are suboptimal, noisy, or misaligned with task-specific expert behavior. We propose GLOVES, a family of flow-based adaptation methods that correct non-expert actions by transporting them toward an expert action distribution. Rather than replacing agentic control with full autonomy, GLOVES performs selective action-level adaptation, improving task success while preserving agent intent. The learned flow also provides a natural in-distribution scoring mechanism through reverse flow evaluation. We use this signal as an intervention gate: actions that appear consistent with the expert distribution are passed through unchanged, while anomalous or out-of-distribution (OOD) actions are corrected. In this way, assistance is only provided when necessary. GLOVES requires only limited expert supervision, using a small number of demonstrations or reusable successful skill segments. By learning local expert action patterns and stitching them during execution, GLOVES provides a lightweight shared-control module for robust action adaptation across tasks and environments. Code and demos are available at ripl.github.io/GLOVES_web.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需策略更新的基于流的策略自适应
利用预训练策略、基础模型或人类操作员的先验知识，可为机器人技能学习提供高效替代方案。然而，这些代理常提供次优、嘈杂或与任务特定专家行为不一致的动作。我们提出GLOVES，一种基于流的自适应方法家族，通过将非专家动作传输至专家动作分布，对其进行修正。GLOVES并非用完全自主控制替代代理控制，而是执行选择性的动作级自适应，在提升任务成功率的同时保留代理意图。所学习的流还通过反向流评估提供自然的分布内评分机制。我们将其作为干预门控信号：与专家分布一致的动作直接通过，而异常或分布外（OOD）动作则被修正。因此，仅在必要时提供辅助。GLOVES仅需少量专家监督，使用少量演示或可复用的成功技能片段。通过学习局部专家动作模式并在执行时拼接，GLOVES提供轻量级共享控制模块，实现跨任务和环境的鲁棒动作自适应。代码和演示见ripl.github.io/GLOVES_web。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of learning robot skills from scratch, this work introduces GLOVES, a flow-based adaptation method that corrects suboptimal, noisy, or misaligned actions from non-expert agents by transporting them toward an expert action distribution. Instead of replacing agent control, GLOVES selectively adapts actions at the level of individual movements, preserving agent intent while improving task success. It uses a learned flow to score actions via reverse flow evaluation, acting as an intervention gate: actions consistent with the expert distribution are passed unchanged, while anomalous or out-of-distribution actions are corrected. The method requires minimal expert supervision, leveraging only a few demonstrations or reusable skill segments, and enables lightweight, cross-task, and cross-environment robust adaptation through local expert pattern stitching.
为减少从零学习机器人技能的低效性，本文提出GLOVES方法，通过流机制将非专家动作向专家分布迁移，无需更新原始策略。该方法利用反向流评估作为干预门控，仅修正异常或分布外动作，保留用户意图同时提升任务成功率。实验表明，GLOVES仅需少量演示或可复用技能片段即可实现跨任务与环境的轻量级动作适应，表现优异。
Will the Agent Recuse Itself? Measuring LLM-Agent Compliance with In-Band Access-Deny Signals
Authors: Thamilvendhan Munirathinam
First: 2026-06-04T17:50:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:50:54+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 figure. Code, specification, and experiment harness: https://github.com/mthamil107/Recuse
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As autonomous LLM agents increasingly hold real credentials and operate infrastructure without a human in the loop, operators have no standard way to tell an agent that a resource is off-limits. Access controls either let the agent in (it has valid credentials) or hard-fail it (indistinguishable from any other client). We propose a third mode: a lightweight, published in-band deny signal -- the Recuse Signal -- that a server emits over a protocol's existing channels (an SSH banner, a PostgreSQL NOTICE) asking a connecting automated agent to voluntarily withdraw. This is a cooperative governance control, the robots.txt analogue for live access; it is explicitly not a security boundary. Its value is entirely empirical and, to our knowledge, unmeasured: do compliant LLM agents actually honor such a signal? We define the signal as an open mini-standard, implement two zero- or low-footprint adapters (an SSH banner/PAM hook and a PostgreSQL wire-protocol proxy), deploy them on a live production host, and run a controlled experiment in which fresh agents are given a benign operations task and observed for recusal. In a pilot (SSH; OpenAI GPT-4o and GPT-4o-mini; and Claude Code as a deployed agent), the signal cleanly induces recusal -- 100% recusal when present versus 100% task completion in a no-signal control -- and, revealingly, behaves as a cooperative rather than absolute signal: an explicit operator-authorization framing flips the most capable model to proceed, while other agents continue to defer to the on-host policy. We release the standard, adapters, and experiment harness for reproduction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理会主动回避吗？衡量大语言模型代理对带内访问拒绝信号的合规性
随着自主的大语言模型代理越来越多地持有真实凭证并在无人值守的情况下操作基础设施，操作员目前没有标准方法告知代理某资源不可访问。访问控制要么允许代理进入（其凭证有效），要么直接拒绝（与任何其他客户端无异）。我们提出第三种模式：一种轻量级、公开发布的带内拒绝信号——回避信号（Recuse Signal），由服务器通过现有协议通道（如SSH欢迎信息、PostgreSQL NOTICE）发出，请求连接的自动化代理自愿退出。这是一种协作治理控制，相当于实时访问的robots.txt，明确不是安全边界。其价值完全基于实证，据我们所知尚未被测量：合规的大语言模型代理是否真的会遵守此类信号？我们将该信号定义为一个开放的微型标准，实现两个零或低开销适配器（SSH欢迎信息/PAM钩子和PostgreSQL线协议代理），在真实生产主机上部署，并进行受控实验，让新代理执行良性操作任务并观察其是否回避。在试点实验（SSH；OpenAI GPT-4o 和 GPT-4o-mini；以及部署的 Claude Code 代理）中，该信号能清晰诱导回避——存在信号时100%回避，无信号对照组100%完成任务——且揭示性地表现为协作而非绝对信号：明确的运营授权框架可使最强大的模型继续执行，而其他代理仍遵循主机策略。我们发布该标准、适配器及实验工具以供复现。
Summary / 总结
As autonomous LLM agents gain real credentials and operate infrastructure without human oversight, operators lack a standardized way to instruct agents to avoid restricted resources. The paper proposes a lightweight 'Recuse Signal' — an in-band denial message emitted by servers via existing protocol channels — to encourage voluntary agent withdrawal. Two low-footprint adapters were implemented for SSH and PostgreSQL, deployed on a live server, and tested in a controlled experiment where agents performed benign tasks. Results showed 100% recusal when the signal was present versus 100% task completion without it, demonstrating the signal’s effectiveness. Notably, when framed as explicit operator authorization, even the most capable model (GPT-4o) proceeded, revealing the signal’s cooperative nature rather than absolute enforcement.
随着自主LLM代理获得真实凭证并在无人监督下操作基础设施，操作者缺乏标准化的资源拒绝信号机制。作者提出“Recuse Signal”——一种轻量级、内嵌于协议通道的信号（如SSH欢迎信息或PostgreSQL NOTICE），要求代理自愿退出。他们实现两个低开销适配器，部署在生产服务器上，并对新代理执行良性任务进行控制实验。结果显示，合规代理完全遵守该信号（有信号时100%退出，无信号时100%完成任务），且行为具有情境依赖性：当操作者明确授权时，即使最强大的模型也会继续执行，而其他代理仍遵循主机策略。
Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN
Authors: Christie Djidjev, Nicholas Kaminski
First: 2026-06-04T17:50:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:50:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向AI-RAN的参数到KPI依赖关系学习的事件检测
下一代无线网络预计将依赖多个并发的AI驱动控制功能，同时优化不同的网络目标，特别是在AI集成和开放无线接入网络架构（如AI-RAN和O-RAN）中。当这些功能相互作用时，它们的干扰难以仅从原始网络数据中检测。管理此类交互的关键缺失环节是可靠的、可解释的依赖结构，以捕捉在任何给定时刻哪些控制参数正在影响哪些网络性能结果。本文聚焦于支持此类依赖学习所需的事件检测步骤，即将嘈杂的连续遥测数据转换为参数活动和KPI响应的二进制指示器。核心难点在于，并非数据中的每一次波动都反映真实的控制交互，因此方法必须区分真实参数-结果关系与背景变化。由于难以获取具有已知参数-KPI真实值的AI-RAN真实流量数据，我们引入了一个带有预设潜在依赖关系的合成闭环流量生成器。我们利用该受控遥测数据评估一种基于机器学习的依赖关系恢复管道，将连续轨迹转换为二进制事件指示器视为显著性检测问题。实验评估表明，当信号与背景变化充分分离时，所提出的管道能可靠地从嘈杂连续轨迹中恢复潜在依赖结构，同时强调阈值校准是控制事件检测质量的关键因素。这些结果为可解释依赖学习在自适应AI-RAN控制系统中的应用奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to manage interference among concurrent AI-driven control functions in next-generation wireless networks like AI-RAN, this work addresses the challenge of detecting meaningful parameter-to-KPI dependencies from noisy telemetry. It proposes a machine-learning pipeline that converts continuous network traces into binary event indicators by treating the problem as a significance-detection task, distinguishing genuine control interactions from background noise. Evaluated using a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with known latent dependencies, the method successfully recovers dependency structures when signal strength exceeds noise, with threshold calibration identified as the critical factor determining detection accuracy.
为应对下一代无线网络（如AI-RAN）中并发AI控制功能相互干扰的问题，本文提出一种从噪声数据中检测参数与KPI间真实依赖关系的方法。该方法将连续遥测数据转化为二值事件指示符，将其建模为显著性检测问题，以区分真实控制交互与背景噪声。通过合成闭环流量生成器（含已知依赖关系）进行评估，结果表明当信号强度高于噪声时，该方法能可靠恢复依赖结构，且阈值校准是决定检测质量的关键因素。
In-Context Multiple Instance Learning
Authors: Alexander Möllers, Marvin Sextro, Julius Hense, Gabriel Dernbach, Klaus-Robert Müller
First: 2026-06-04T17:50:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:50:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文多实例学习
多实例学习（MIL）处理监督信息在实例袋级别可用的问题，并已在计算病理学到卫星图像等多个领域成功应用。然而，现有算法在标签稀缺的现实应用场景中表现不佳：灵活模型容易过拟合，刚性模型则无法适应具体任务。我们表明，使用Perceiver风格架构在合成数据上预训练一个上下文学习器，可生成仅需少量标注袋即可解决新任务的模型。推理时，分类仅需一次前向传播，无需梯度更新。我们提出并研究了多种适用于袋结构数据的合成数据生成器，发现它们捕捉了互补的归纳偏置。在混合这些生成器上预训练的模型继承了各生成器的任务优势，在十二个MIL基准测试中实现了最佳平均性能，优于需要任务特定训练的监督基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of limited labeled data in real-world multiple instance learning (MIL) tasks, this work introduces an in-context learning approach using a Perceiver-style architecture pretrained on synthetic bag-structured data. The method generates diverse synthetic data using multiple data generators, each encoding different inductive biases, and trains a single model on their mixture. At inference, classification occurs in one forward pass without gradient updates. Experiments across twelve MIL benchmarks show that this pretrained model achieves superior average performance compared to supervised baselines requiring task-specific training, demonstrating robust generalization with minimal labeled examples.
为应对现实世界中MIL任务标签数据稀缺的问题，该研究提出一种基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习方法，通过在合成袋结构数据上预训练解决新任务。该方法通过生成多样化的合成数据以捕捉互补的归纳偏置，预训练模型仅需少量标注袋即可完成推理，无需梯度更新。在十二个基准测试中，该方法平均性能优于需任务特定训练的监督基线模型。
From Kinematics to Dynamics: Learning to Refine Hybrid Plans for Physically Feasible Execution
Authors: Lidor Erez, Shahaf S. Shperberg, Ayal Taitler
First: 2026-04-14T09:00:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:50:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In many robotic tasks, agents must traverse a sequence of spatial regions to complete a mission. Such problems are inherently mixed discrete-continuous: a high-level action sequence and a physically feasible continuous trajectory. The resulting trajectory and action sequence must also satisfy problem constraints such as deadlines, time windows, and velocity or acceleration limits. While hybrid temporal planners attempt to address this challenge, they typically model motion using linear (first-order) dynamics, which cannot guarantee that the resulting plan respects the robot's true physical constraints. Consequently, even when the high-level action sequence is fixed, producing a dynamically feasible trajectory becomes a bi-level optimization problem. We address this problem via reinforcement learning in continuous space. We define a Markov Decision Process that explicitly incorporates analytical second-order constraints and use it to refine first-order plans generated by a hybrid planner. Our results show that this approach can reliably recover physical feasibility and effectively bridge the gap between a planner's initial first-order trajectory and the dynamics required for real execution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从运动学到动力学：学习优化混合计划以实现物理可行的执行
在许多机器人任务中，代理必须遍历一系列空间区域以完成任务。此类问题本质上是混合离散-连续的：一个高层动作序列和一个物理上可行的连续轨迹。生成的轨迹和动作序列还必须满足诸如截止时间、时间窗口、速度或加速度限制等约束条件。尽管混合时序规划器试图解决这一挑战，但它们通常使用线性（一阶）动力学建模运动，无法保证生成的计划符合机器人的实际物理约束。因此，即使高层动作序列已固定，生成动态可行轨迹也成为一个双层优化问题。我们通过连续空间中的强化学习解决该问题，定义了一个显式包含解析二阶约束的马尔可夫决策过程，并用其优化混合规划器生成的一阶计划。实验结果表明，该方法能可靠恢复物理可行性，并有效弥合规划器初始一阶轨迹与实际执行所需动力学之间的差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate physically feasible trajectories for robotic tasks that combine discrete actions with continuous motion, this work proposes a reinforcement learning method to refine first-order hybrid plans into dynamically feasible trajectories. The approach models the problem as a Markov Decision Process that explicitly enforces second-order physical constraints such as acceleration limits. Experiments demonstrate that this method reliably recovers physical feasibility and effectively bridges the gap between initial planner outputs and real-world execution requirements.
为解决机器人任务中混合离散-连续动作规划时物理可行性不足的问题，该研究提出一种基于强化学习的轨迹优化方法，通过在马尔可夫决策过程中显式嵌入二阶动力学约束，对混合规划器生成的一阶轨迹进行修正。该方法将轨迹优化建模为连续空间优化问题，从而在满足速度、加速度和时间约束的前提下恢复物理可行性。实验结果表明，该方法能稳定生成可执行的动态轨迹，有效弥合规划器初始轨迹与真实机器人运动需求之间的差距。
From Out-of-Distribution Detection to Hallucination Detection: A Geometric View
Authors: Litian Liu, Reza Pourreza, Yubing Jian, Yao Qin, Roland Memisevic
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-06T23:05:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:49:13+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 main conference paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting hallucinations in large language models is a critical open problem with significant implications for safety and reliability. While existing hallucination detection methods achieve strong performance in question-answering tasks, they remain less effective on tasks requiring reasoning. In this work, we revisit hallucination detection through the lens of out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, a well-studied problem in areas like computer vision. Treating next-token prediction in language models as a classification task allows us to apply OOD techniques, provided appropriate modifications are made to account for the structural differences in large language models. We show that OOD-based approaches yield training-free, single-sample-based detectors, achieving strong accuracy in hallucination detection for reasoning tasks. Overall, our work suggests that reframing hallucination detection as OOD detection provides a promising and scalable pathway toward language model safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从分布外检测到幻觉检测：一种几何视角
检测大型语言模型中的幻觉是关乎安全与可靠性的关键开放问题。尽管现有幻觉检测方法在问答任务中表现优异，但在需要推理的任务中效果较差。本文从分布外（OOD）检测的角度重新审视幻觉检测，该方法在计算机视觉等领域已有深入研究。将语言模型的下一个词预测视为分类任务，可应用OOD技术，只需对大型语言模型的结构差异进行适当调整。我们证明，基于OOD的方法可实现无需训练、单样本的检测器，在推理任务的幻觉检测中达到高准确率。总体而言，本文表明将幻觉检测重新定义为OOD检测，是实现语言模型安全的有前景且可扩展的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable hallucination detection in large language models, especially for reasoning tasks where existing methods underperform, this work frames hallucination detection as an out-of-distribution (OOD) detection problem. By treating next-token prediction as a classification task and adapting OOD techniques for language models, the authors develop training-free, single-sample detectors that leverage geometric properties of model outputs. Experimental results demonstrate that these OOD-based detectors achieve strong accuracy in identifying hallucinations across reasoning tasks, suggesting a scalable and theoretically grounded approach to improving language model safety.
为提升大语言模型在推理任务中对幻觉的检测能力，本文将幻觉检测重新定义为分布外检测问题。通过将下一词预测视为分类任务，并对语言模型结构进行适配，作者构建了无需训练、仅需单样本的检测器。实验表明，该几何视角方法在推理任务中展现出高准确率，为提升模型安全性提供了一条可扩展且理论扎实的新路径。
Scaffold, Not Vocabulary? A Controlled, Two-Tier, Pre-Registered Study of a Popperian Code-Generation Skill
Authors: Mehmet Iscan
First: 2026-06-04T17:49:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:49:00+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models increasingly write, review, and judge code, and a fast-growing practice equips them with prompt 'skills' that ask the model to reason like a scientist. A prominent example tells the model to act as a Popperian falsificationist, and such skills are reported to improve generated code. But these gains are almost always read off an LLM-as-a-judge, an instrument with documented positional, self-preference, and stylistic biases. We ask: if it appears to help, is the gain from the skill's Popperian content, or from the structure any scaffold imposes? We pre-register a two-tier ablation with three controls: a length-matched placebo, a labels-only scaffold that keeps the Popperian headers but strips the procedure, and an execution oracle (HumanEval+ unit tests), plus a vocabulary-halo sentinel and a same-model self-judge audit. On a frontier model (Claude Sonnet 4.6, N=163) all conditions sit near the benchmark ceiling and do not separate, so the pre-registered +5-point improvement is not supported (a ceiling-limited non-detection). On a small model (Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B, N=164) structured arms lift best-of-eight correctness by 20-22 points, but the full skill shows no separable benefit over a labels-only scaffold (aggregate F@8=L@8 vs V@8=34.8%), and the placebo trails by only 2.4 points. A 0.5B self-judge applying the Popperian rubric does not beat random selection and concentrates 60% of its picks on one index. In the two settings tested, the skill's Popperian procedural content adds no separable execution-correctness benefit beyond a labels-only scaffold, so the gains track scaffold structure. We contribute a calibrated negative result and a reusable disambiguation protocol; the finding bounds an engineering claim about one prompt-skill family and is not an evaluation of Popperian methodology in general.
中文标题/摘要
标题：支架，而非词汇？一项关于波普尔式代码生成技能的受控双层预注册研究
大型语言模型日益参与代码的编写、评审与评判，一种快速发展的实践赋予它们通过提示‘技能’模拟科学家推理的能力。一个典型例子是要求模型扮演波普尔式证伪主义者，此类技能据称能提升生成代码的质量。但这些收益通常依赖于作为评判者的LLM，而该工具存在已知的立场偏见、自我偏好和风格偏见。我们提出疑问：若看似有益，是技能中的波普尔式内容带来收益，还是支架结构本身的作用？我们预注册了一项双层消融实验，包含三个对照组：长度匹配的安慰剂、仅保留波普尔式标题但剥离程序步骤的标签支架、执行预言机（HumanEval+单元测试），以及词汇光环哨兵和同模型自我评判审计。在前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6，N=163）上，所有条件均接近基准上限且无显著差异，因此预注册的+5分提升未获支持（天花板限制下的未检测）。在小型模型（Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B，N=164）上，结构化组将八选最优正确率提升20-22分，但完整技能未显示出相对于仅标签支架的可分离优势（聚合F@8=L@8 vs V@8=34.8%），安慰剂仅落后2.4分。0.5B自我评判模型应用波普尔式评分标准，表现不优于随机选择，且60%的选中结果集中于单一索引。在所测试的两种设置中，技能的波普尔式程序内容未带来超出仅标签支架的可分离执行正确性收益，因此收益归因于支架结构。我们贡献了一个校准后的负向结果及可复用的歧义消解协议；该发现仅限于特定提示技能家族的工程主张，而非对波普尔式方法论的普遍评价。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to disentangle whether Popperian prompt skills improve code generation through content or scaffold structure, the study employs a pre-registered two-tier ablation design with controls including a placebo, a labels-only scaffold, and an execution oracle. On a frontier model (Claude Sonnet 4.6), all conditions performed near the ceiling with no significant separation, failing to replicate the claimed +5-point gain. On a small model (Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B), structured prompts improved correctness by 20-22 points over baseline, but the full Popperian skill showed no additional benefit over the labels-only scaffold, while the placebo lagged by only 2.4 points. A self-judge applying the Popperian rubric performed no better than random and concentrated choices on one index, indicating the skill’s procedural content adds no separable correctness benefit beyond structural scaffolding.
为区分波普尔式提示技能是否通过内容或结构框架提升代码生成效果，研究采用预注册的双层消融设计，设置安慰剂、仅标签框架和执行 oracle 等对照。在前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6）上，所有条件均接近性能上限，未发现可区分的提升，未能支持宣称的+5分收益。在小型模型（Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B）上，结构化方案提升正确率20-22分，但完整波普尔技能未显著优于仅标签框架，安慰剂仅落后2.4分。应用波普尔标准的自评者表现不优于随机选择，且集中于单一索引。结果表明，波普尔程序内容对执行正确性无额外贡献，收益源于结构框架而非方法论内容。
Vortex: Efficient and Programmable Sparse Attention Serving for AI Agents
Authors: Zhuoming Chen, Xinrui Zhong, Qilong Feng, Ranajoy Sadhukhan, Yang Zhou, Michael Qizhe Shieh, Zhihao Jia, Beidi Chen
First: 2026-06-04T17:48:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:48:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse attention is becoming increasingly important for serving large language models (LLMs) as generation lengths continue to grow. However, deploying and evaluating new sparse attention algorithms at scale remains highly engineering-intensive, slowing both human researchers and AI agents in exploring the sparse attention design. To address this challenge, we present Vortex, a system that combines a Python-embedded frontend language atop a page-centric tensor abstraction for expressing a broad range of sparse attention algorithms, with an efficient backend tightly integrated into modern LLM serving stacks. Vortex enables rapid prototyping, deployment, and evaluation of sparse attention algorithms, effectively translating their theoretical efficiency gains into real-world throughput improvements. As a result, Vortex substantially accelerates the design and iteration of sparse attention algorithms. First, AI agents use Vortex to automatically generate and refine diverse algorithms, the best reaching up to $3.46\times$ higher throughput than full attention while preserving accuracy. Second, Vortex extends sparse attention to emerging architectures and very large models that are otherwise hard to experiment with, reaching up to $4.7\times$ higher throughput on the MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash and $1.37\times$ on the 229B-parameter MiniMax-M2.7 on NVIDIA B200 GPUs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vortex：面向AI代理的高效可编程稀疏注意力服务
随着生成长度持续增长，稀疏注意力在服务大型语言模型（LLM）中变得愈发重要。然而，大规模部署和评估新的稀疏注意力算法仍高度依赖工程化工作，这延缓了人类研究人员和AI代理探索稀疏注意力设计的速度。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了Vortex系统，它结合了基于页面中心张量抽象的前端语言（嵌入Python），用于表达广泛的稀疏注意力算法，以及与现代LLM服务栈紧密集成的高效后端。Vortex支持稀疏注意力算法的快速原型设计、部署与评估，有效将理论效率增益转化为实际吞吐量提升。因此，Vortex显著加速了稀疏注意力算法的设计与迭代。首先，AI代理利用Vortex自动生成并优化多样化的算法，最优算法在保持精度的同时，吞吐量较全注意力提升高达3.46倍。其次，Vortex将稀疏注意力扩展至新兴架构和难以实验的超大规模模型，例如在基于MLA的GLM-4.7-Flash上吞吐量提升达4.7倍，在NVIDIA B200 GPU上运行的229B参数MiniMax-M2.7模型上提升1.37倍。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to efficiently serve increasingly long-generation LLMs, Vortex introduces a system that enables rapid prototyping and deployment of sparse attention algorithms through a Python-embedded frontend and a page-centric tensor backend tightly integrated into LLM serving stacks. By allowing AI agents to automatically generate and refine diverse sparse attention designs, Vortex achieves up to 3.46x throughput gains over full attention while maintaining accuracy. It further extends sparse attention to large models like GLM-4.7-Flash and MiniMax-M2.7, delivering 4.7x and 1.37x throughput improvements respectively on NVIDIA B200 GPUs, demonstrating its scalability and practical utility.
随着大语言模型生成长度增加，稀疏注意力对高效服务至关重要，但新算法的部署仍需大量工程工作，阻碍了人类研究者和AI代理的探索。Vortex通过结合支持多种稀疏注意力算法的Python嵌入式前端与紧密集成于现代LLM服务栈的页级张量后端，实现快速原型设计与部署。实验表明，AI代理使用Vortex可生成最高达3.46倍于全注意力吞吐量的算法且保持精度，同时成功将稀疏注意力扩展至GLM-4.7-Flash和MiniMax-M2.7等大型模型，在NVIDIA B200 GPU上分别实现4.7倍和1.37倍的吞吐量提升。
Agent Memory: Characterization and System Implications of Stateful Long-Horizon Workloads
Authors: Yasmine Omri, Ziyu Gan, Zachary Broveak, Robin Geens, Zexue He, Alex Pentland, Marian Verhelst, Tsachy Weissman, Thierry Tambe
First: 2026-06-04T17:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:44:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks requiring sustained reasoning over extended interaction histories. Realizing this at scale requires agents to persistently store, retrieve, and update their own memory across sessions. A rich ecosystem of agent memory systems has emerged spanning flat retrieval, LLM-mediated extraction, consolidating fact stores, and agentic control flows. Yet, their system-level behavior remains uncharacterized. We present the first systems characterization of agent memory. First, we introduce a system-oriented taxonomy classifying agent memory systems along four axes. Second, we build a phase-aware profiling harness attributing cost to construction, retrieval, and generation. Third, we characterize ten representative systems across two benchmark suites, uncovering how design choices shift cost across the write and read paths. Finally, we derive 10 system recommendations covering construction scheduling, capability floors, amortization via query volume, freshness-latency tradeoffs, and fleet-scale management.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理记忆：状态化长周期任务的特征与系统影响
大型语言模型代理正越来越多地部署于需要在长时间交互历史中持续推理的长周期任务中。要在大规模场景下实现这一目标，代理必须在会话间持续存储、检索和更新自身的记忆。目前已涌现出涵盖扁平检索、LLM介导提取、事实整合存储及代理控制流等多种代理记忆系统。然而，其系统级行为尚未被系统性刻画。本文首次对代理记忆系统进行系统性分析：首先，我们提出一种面向系统的分类法，沿四个维度对代理记忆系统进行分类；其次，我们构建了一种具备阶段感知的性能剖析工具，将成本归因于构建、检索与生成三个阶段；第三，我们在两个基准测试套件中对十种代表性系统进行分析，揭示设计选择如何影响写入与读取路径的成本；最后，我们提出十条系统性建议，涵盖构建调度、能力底线、通过查询量摊销成本、新鲜度与延迟权衡，以及集群规模管理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how LLM agents manage memory across long-horizon tasks, this study systematically characterizes agent memory systems by introducing a four-axis taxonomy and a phase-aware profiling tool that tracks costs during construction, retrieval, and generation. Through profiling ten representative systems on two benchmarks, the research reveals how architectural decisions influence write and read costs differently, uncovering trade-offs such as freshness versus latency and amortization via query volume. The findings lead to ten system-level recommendations for optimizing memory management at scale, including scheduling, capability floors, and fleet-wide coordination.
为理解大语言模型代理在长周期任务中如何管理记忆，本研究系统性地对代理记忆系统进行建模，提出四维分类法并构建阶段感知的性能分析工具，追踪构建、检索与生成阶段的成本。通过对十种代表性系统在两个基准测试中的分析，研究揭示了设计选择如何影响写入与读取路径的成本分布。主要发现包括记忆新鲜度与延迟的权衡、查询量对成本摊销的影响，以及大规模集群管理的必要性，最终提出十条系统优化建议以提升记忆效率。
Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows
Authors: Guancheng Tu, Xiangjun Fu, Suhao Yu, Yao Tang, Haoqiang Kang, Lianhui Qin, Yizhe Zhang, Jiatao Gu
First: 2026-06-04T17:44:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:44:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用归一化流的潜在推理
大型语言模型常通过生成显式的思维链（CoT）来提升推理能力，凸显了中间计算的重要性。然而，文本形式的CoT迫使计算通过离散、串行且以通信为导向的token流：每个推理步骤必须先被语言化，模型才能继续，即使底层更新是语义的、不确定的或仅部分形成。潜在推理通过在提交文本前在紧凑的连续状态中执行中间计算，提供更高带宽的替代方案。但现有潜在推理方法常牺牲了使CoT在自回归语言模型中有效的关键优势，包括原生左到右生成、概率采样、与KV缓存解码兼容性及可处理的似然估计。我们提出NF-CoT，一种通过归一化流建模连续思维的潜在推理框架，以保留这些优势。NF-CoT在LLM主干内实现TARFlow风格的归一化流，定义了从显式CoT提炼出的紧凑连续思维上的可处理概率模型。连续思维位置由NF头生成，文本位置则由同一因果流中的标准语言模型头生成。该设计为潜在思维提供精确似然，支持使用原始KV缓存的左到右概率解码，并支持在潜在推理空间中直接进行策略梯度优化。在代码生成基准测试中，NF-CoT在提升通过率的同时，显著降低了中间推理成本，优于显式CoT及先前的潜在推理基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of discrete, serial chain-of-thought generation in large language models, this paper proposes NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that performs intermediate reasoning in compact continuous states using normalizing flows, preserving key autoregressive advantages like left-to-right generation and KV-cache compatibility. The method embeds a TARFlow-style normalizing flow within the LLM backbone to model continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT, while maintaining a standard language model head for text generation in the same causal stream. Experimental results show NF-CoT achieves higher pass rates on code-generation benchmarks than explicit CoT and prior latent methods, while significantly reducing the computational cost of intermediate reasoning.
为了解决大语言模型中显式链式思维生成效率低的问题，本文提出NF-CoT框架，通过在模型中嵌入归一化流，将中间推理过程建模为紧凑的连续状态。该方法在LLM骨干结构中实现TARFlow风格的流，对从显式CoT中蒸馏出的连续思维建模，支持概率左到右生成、KV缓存兼容及可计算似然。实验表明，NF-CoT在代码生成基准上优于显式CoT和现有隐式推理方法，同时大幅降低了中间推理的计算开销。
USAD 2.0: Scaling Representation Distillation for Universal Audio Understanding
Authors: Heng-Jui Chang, Alexander H. Liu, Saurabhchand Bhati, Mrudula Athi, Anton Ratnarajah, Amit Chhetri, James Glass
First: 2026-06-04T17:42:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:42:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Audio encoders are critical to modern audio applications as large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on a single encoder for diverse inputs. While self-supervised learning (SSL) has yielded strong domain-specific encoders like speech or music experts, multi-domain approaches like USAD and SPEAR remain limited in coverage and evaluation. Recent studies also suggest supervised encoders align better with audio LLMs. We present USAD 2.0, a universal encoder integrating knowledge from both SSL and supervised foundation models. USAD 2.0 introduces domain-aware distillation to address teacher mismatch, extends coverage to the music domain, and adds second-stage supervised distillation for downstream use. We further scale the model to one billion parameters via depth scaling. Experiments show USAD 2.0 achieves strong or state-of-the-art performance across probing and LLM-based evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：USAD 2.0：面向通用音频理解的表示蒸馏扩展
音频编码器对现代音频应用至关重要，因为大型语言模型（LLM）日益依赖单一编码器处理多样输入。虽然自监督学习（SSL）已产生如语音或音乐专家等领域特定编码器，但多领域方法如USAD和SPEAR在覆盖范围和评估上仍有限。近期研究表明，监督编码器与音频LLM更匹配。我们提出USAD 2.0，一种整合SSL与监督基础模型知识的通用编码器。USAD 2.0引入领域感知蒸馏以解决教师模型不匹配问题，扩展至音乐领域，并增加第二阶段监督蒸馏以支持下游任务。我们进一步通过深度扩展将模型规模提升至十亿参数。实验表明，USAD 2.0在探测与基于LLM的评估中均取得强性能或当前最优结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a single audio encoder that supports diverse inputs for large language models, USAD 2.0 combines self-supervised learning and supervised foundation models through domain-aware distillation to resolve teacher-student mismatch, extends coverage to music, and adds second-stage supervised distillation for downstream tasks. Scaled to one billion parameters via depth scaling, the model achieves strong or state-of-the-art performance across both probing and LLM-based evaluations, demonstrating broader applicability and effectiveness than prior multi-domain approaches.
为满足大型语言模型对多域音频输入的统一编码需求，USAD 2.0融合自监督与监督基础模型，通过领域感知蒸馏解决教师-学生不匹配问题，扩展至音乐领域，并增加第二阶段监督蒸馏以适配下游任务。模型通过深度扩展提升至十亿参数规模，在多项探测与基于LLM的评估中均取得强性能或当前最优结果，展现出更强的泛化能力与对音频LLM的适配性。
Revising Context, Shifting Simulated Stance: Auditing LLM-Based Stance Simulation in Online Discussions
Authors: Xinnong Zhang, Wanting Shan, Hanjia Lyu, Zhongyu Wei, Jiebo Luo
First: 2026-06-04T17:41:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:41:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly used to simulate social media users and infer how individuals may respond to online discussions. However, it remains unclear whether these simulations reflect precise user-specific beliefs or whether they are highly sensitive to semantically independent changes in conversational contexts. In this work, we study counterfactual context revision as a framework for auditing LLM-based stance simulation. Given an original online conversation, we first infer a target user's stance toward a specific topic. We then apply controlled revision strategies to the conversational context and simulate the user's stance again under the revised context. We compare text-only revision strategies with a multimodal one that incorporates meme-based context and evaluate two main effectiveness metrics, i.e., average directional stance shift and stance transition rate. The results reveal effective and robust stance transitions in both text-only and multimodal strategies across different polarization-preference mechanisms. Our study contributes an evaluation framework for understanding the context sensitivity of LLM-based stance simulation. More broadly, it highlights both the promise and risk of using LLMs to simulate online opinion dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：修订语境、调整模拟立场：审计基于大语言模型的立场模拟在在线讨论中的表现
大语言模型正越来越多地用于模拟社交媒体用户并推断个体对在线讨论的可能反应。然而，尚不清楚这些模拟是否准确反映用户特定的信念，还是对语义独立的对话语境变化高度敏感。在本研究中，我们以反事实语境修订为框架，审计基于大语言模型的立场模拟。给定原始在线对话，我们首先推断目标用户对特定话题的立场，然后对对话语境应用受控修订策略，并在修订后的语境下再次模拟用户立场。我们比较纯文本修订策略与结合基于表情包的多模态策略，并评估两个主要有效性指标：平均立场方向变化和立场转换率。结果表明，无论采用纯文本还是多模态策略，均能在不同极化偏好机制下实现有效且稳健的立场转换。本研究贡献了一种评估框架，用于理解基于大语言模型的立场模拟对语境的敏感性。更广泛地说，它突显了利用大语言模型模拟在线意见动态的潜力与风险。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models simulate user stances in online discussions and whether these simulations are sensitive to changes in context. The researchers audit stance simulation by applying controlled revisions to conversation contexts—both text-only and multimodal (including memes)—and measuring how user stances shift under these changes. They evaluate performance using average directional stance shift and stance transition rate, finding that both strategies induce consistent and robust stance changes regardless of polarization mechanisms, demonstrating that LLMs can reliably simulate opinion shifts but also highlighting potential risks in using them to model real-world opinion dynamics.
本研究探讨大语言模型在模拟在线讨论中用户立场时，其结果是否受对话上下文变化的影响。研究者通过施加可控的反事实上下文修改（包括纯文本和多模态，如表情包）来审计立场模拟效果，并用平均立场方向变化和立场转换率两个指标评估。结果表明，无论采用哪种策略，模型都能在不同极化机制下产生稳定且显著的立场转变。该研究构建了评估LLM立场模拟上下文敏感性的框架，同时揭示了其在模拟网络舆论动态中的潜力与风险。
Causal Atlases from Entropic Inference: Bayesian Networks beyond Optimal DAGs
Authors: Hazhir Aliahmadi, Irina Babayan, Greg van Anders
First: 2026-06-04T17:41:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:41:32+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data-driven causal relationship identification is pertinent to advancing understanding of complex systems both within and beyond science. Bayesian networks offer a probabilistic method for modelling generic causal relationships via directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). However, typical techniques for constructing Bayesian networks rely on optimization, which can be ill-suited for learning causal relationships because the underlying data may admit multiple chains of causation. More data-faithful representations of causal relationships would provide frameworks for constructing multiple causal maps that are consistent with the variability that is inherent in underlying data. Here, we show that entropy-based inference generates atlases of plausible causal relationships that are consistent with underlying data. On simulated noisy data of 2- and 20-node linear structural equation models, we sample a maximum-entropy ensemble of graphs that allow us to quantify the inherent structural ambiguity in underlying causal relationships. Our method shows that "optimized" DAGs can contain causal artifacts are not consistent across equivalently accurate topologies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从熵推断构建因果图谱：超越最优DAG的贝叶斯网络
数据驱动的因果关系识别对于推进科学内外复杂系统的理解至关重要。贝叶斯网络通过有向无环图（DAG）提供了一种概率方法，用于建模通用因果关系。然而，典型的贝叶斯网络构建技术依赖于优化，这可能不适合学习因果关系，因为底层数据可能允许多条因果链。更符合数据特征的因果关系表示将为构建多个与底层数据固有变异性一致的因果图谱提供框架。本文表明，基于熵的推断可生成与底层数据一致的合理因果关系图谱。在模拟的2节点和20节点线性结构方程模型的噪声数据上，我们采样最大熵图集合，以量化底层因果关系中的固有结构模糊性。我们的方法表明，"优化"的DAG可能包含不一致于等效准确拓扑结构的因果伪影。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of optimization-based Bayesian network construction in capturing true causal variability, this work proposes using entropy-based inference to generate causal atlases—ensembles of plausible DAGs—that better reflect data-driven structural ambiguity. The method samples maximum-entropy graph ensembles from simulated noisy linear structural equation models with 2 and 20 nodes, revealing that conventional "optimal" DAGs often contain spurious causal structures not shared across equally accurate topologies. Experimental results demonstrate that entropy-guided sampling yields more robust and data-consistent causal representations than optimization-driven approaches.
为克服基于优化的贝叶斯网络在捕捉真实因果结构变异性方面的局限性，本文提出采用熵推断生成因果图谱——即一组可能的DAG集合，以更准确反映数据驱动的结构不确定性。方法在含噪声的2节点和20节点线性结构方程模型上采样最大熵图集合，结果表明传统“最优”DAG常包含不具普适性的伪因果结构，而熵引导采样能生成更稳健、与数据一致的因果表示。
Class-Dependent Hybrid Data Augmentation for Multiclass Migraine Classification under Severe Class Imbalance
Authors: Elvin Somón, Miguel A. Gutiérrez-Naranjo
First: 2026-05-22T10:08:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:39:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We conducted a reproducibility-oriented re-evaluation of prior migraine classification studies, correcting for data leakage and metric bias. We then introduced (i) a clinically motivated aggregation of two hemiplegic subtypes following ICHD-3 §1.2.3, (ii) a class-dependent hybrid augmentation strategy that assigns generation methods based on per-class sample size, and (iii) the concept of fidelity asymmetry, motivating proportionally constrained growth as an alternative to full class balance. Experiments were performed on a dataset of 400 patients across seven migraine subtypes under a two-stage protocol, including the six-class configuration described above. Models were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with macro-averaged F1 as the primary metric. Correcting methodological flaws reduces previously inflated performance estimates, with the corrected macro-F1 baseline standing at 0.71. The proposed framework consistently outperformed individual augmenters in macro-F1 averaged across the eight evaluated classifiers (0.862 vs. 0.836 for Gaussian Copula, 0.815 for CTGAN, and 0.801 for the no-augmentation baseline), and achieved its peak result of 0.914 with FT-Transformer under proportional augmentation. The no-augmentation FT-Transformer baseline (0.896) shows that, at the per-classifier ceiling, clinically motivated class aggregation accounts for most of the absolute improvement; the framework's principal measurable contribution is the gain in average robustness across classifiers, highlighting the dominant role of problem formulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对严重类别不平衡的多类偏头痛分类的类别依赖混合数据增强方法
我们对先前的偏头痛分类研究进行了以可重复性为导向的重新评估，纠正了数据泄露和指标偏差。随后引入了三项改进：(i) 根据ICHD-3 §1.2.3将两种偏瘫型偏头痛子类合并为一个临床驱动的聚合类别；(ii) 一种类别依赖的混合增强策略，根据各子类样本量分配生成方法；(iii) 真实性不对称概念，提出比例约束增长作为替代完全类别平衡的方法。实验在包含7种偏头痛亚型、400名患者的两阶段协议数据集上进行，采用分层5折交叉验证，以宏平均F1为评估指标。 纠正方法学缺陷后，先前被高估的性能指标下降，修正后的宏F1基线为0.71。所提框架在8个评估分类器的宏F1平均值上持续优于单个增强器（0.862 vs. 高斯Copula的0.836、CTGAN的0.815、无增强基线的0.801），并在比例增强下使用FT-Transformer时达到峰值0.914。无增强的FT-Transformer基线（0.896）表明，在各分类器性能上限下，临床驱动的类别聚合贡献了大部分绝对提升；该框架的主要可测量贡献在于提升分类器间的平均鲁棒性，凸显了问题建模的主导作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to correct methodological flaws in prior migraine classification studies, this work introduces a class-dependent hybrid data augmentation strategy that assigns augmentation methods based on per-class sample size, alongside clinically motivated class aggregation and the concept of fidelity asymmetry. Evaluated on a dataset of 400 patients across seven migraine subtypes using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and macro-averaged F1, the framework outperformed individual augmenters (0.862 vs. 0.836–0.815) and achieved a peak macro-F1 of 0.914 with FT-Transformer under proportional augmentation, demonstrating that while class aggregation contributes significantly to performance, the framework’s main advantage lies in enhancing average robustness across classifiers.
为纠正先前研究中的方法缺陷，本研究通过修正数据泄露和指标偏差，将宏观F1基准降至0.71。提出了一种基于类样本量分配不同增强方法的混合数据增强策略，并结合临床驱动的类别聚合与“保真度不对称”概念，以实现比例约束增长。在包含七类偏头痛亚型的400例患者数据集上，对八种分类器进行分层五折交叉验证，该框架在所有分类器上均优于单个增强器（如0.862 vs. 0.836的高斯Copula），并在FT-Transformer下达到0.914的峰值宏观F1。与无增强基线（0.896）相比，虽然类别聚合贡献了大部分提升，但框架的核心优势在于显著增强了分类器间的平均鲁棒性。
Training-Free Inference for High-Resolution Sinogram Completion
Authors: Jiaze E, Srutarshi Banerjee, Tekin Bicer, Guannan Wang, Yanfu Zhang, Bin Ren
First: 2025-06-10T13:59:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:37:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-resolution sinogram completion is critical for computed tomography reconstruction, as missing projections can introduce severe artifacts. While diffusion models provide strong generative priors for this task, their inference cost grows prohibitively with resolution. We propose HRSino, a training-free and efficient diffusion inference approach for high-resolution sinogram completion. By explicitly accounting for spatial heterogeneity in signal characteristics, such as spectral sparsity and local complexity, HRSino allocates inference effort adaptively across spatial regions and resolutions, rather than applying uniform high-resolution diffusion steps. This enables global consistency to be captured at coarse scales while refining local details only where necessary. Experimental results show that HRSino reduces peak memory usage by up to 30.81% and inference time by up to 17.58% compared to the state-of-the-art framework, and maintains completion accuracy across datasets and resolutions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需训练的高分辨率投影数据补全推理
高分辨率投影数据补全对计算机断层扫描重建至关重要，因缺失投影会引入严重伪影。尽管扩散模型为此任务提供了强大的生成先验，但其推理成本随分辨率增加而急剧上升。我们提出HRSino，一种无需训练且高效的扩散推理方法，用于高分辨率投影数据补全。通过显式考虑信号特征的空间异质性（如频谱稀疏性和局部复杂度），HRSino在空间区域和分辨率上自适应分配推理资源，而非统一应用高分辨率扩散步骤。这使得在粗尺度上捕获全局一致性，仅在必要处细化局部细节。实验表明，与当前最优框架相比，HRSino可将峰值内存使用降低30.81%，推理时间降低17.58%，且在不同数据集和分辨率下保持补全精度。
Summary / 总结
To address the high computational cost of diffusion-based sinogram completion at high resolutions, the paper introduces HRSino, a training-free inference method that adapts diffusion steps based on local signal characteristics like spectral sparsity and spatial complexity. Instead of uniformly applying high-resolution diffusion, HRSino allocates computational resources dynamically, prioritizing detail refinement where needed while preserving global structure at coarser scales. Experiments demonstrate that HRSino achieves up to 30.81% lower peak memory usage and 17.58% faster inference than the prior state-of-the-art, while maintaining comparable reconstruction accuracy across multiple datasets and resolutions.
为解决高分辨率下扩散模型推断成本过高的问题，HRSino提出一种无需训练的推理方法，根据信号特性（如频谱稀疏性和局部复杂度）的空间异质性，动态分配扩散步骤。它在粗尺度上保持全局一致性，仅在必要区域细化局部细节，避免统一高分辨率扩散。实验表明，相比当前最优方法，HRSino可降低峰值内存使用30.81%，推理时间缩短17.58%，且在多个数据集和分辨率下保持完成精度不变。
Computational Modeling of Human Adaptation in Urban Infrastructure Management under Extreme Conditions: A Case Study of Subway Flood Scenarios
Authors: Jinfeng Lou, Zijie Liang, Pengkun Liu, Yuxin Zhang, Cleotilde Gonzalez, Pingbo Tang
First: 2026-06-04T17:34:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:34:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision-making in urban infrastructure management during extreme events relies heavily on human operators, yet current computational support systems often fail to account for non-monotonic human adaptation and latent psychological biases like overconfidence and defensive overcorrection. This study addresses this gap by integrating Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) into the domain of civil engineering computing. We establish a computational cognitive architecture that simulates operator decision processes through the mathematical mechanisms of memory retrieval and utility blending. This model functions as a computational baseline, representing boundedly rational adaptation driven by experiential priors, thus allowing for the algorithmic isolation of latent psychological biases from the baseline dynamics of memory-based learning. We demonstrated this framework using a human-in-the-loop microworld experiment simulating subway flood-induced track suspensions, where dispatchers must balance passenger safety against service efficiency. Analysis revealed a complex, non-linear human adaptation cycle consisting of four phases: acquisition, overconfidence, overcorrection, and recalibration. Specifically, the computational model exposed a significant divergence during the post-accident "overcorrection" phase: while human operators exhibited immediate, defensive risk overestimation, the model maintained a stable trajectory based on accumulated experience. This strategic divergence confirms that operational instability following failure is often attributable to acute psychological bias overriding stable memory-based adaptation, a pattern theoretically expected to recur across analogous high-stakes environments and validatable through multi-modal behavioral and sensor data from professional operators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：极端条件下城市基础设施管理中人类适应性的计算建模：地铁洪水场景案例研究
城市基础设施管理在极端事件中的决策高度依赖人类操作员，但当前计算支持系统往往未能考虑人类适应的非单调性及潜在心理偏见（如过度自信与防御性过度修正）。本研究通过将实例学习理论（IBLT）引入土木工程计算领域，构建了一种计算认知架构，通过记忆检索与效用融合的数学机制模拟操作员决策过程。该模型作为计算基线，体现有界理性适应，由经验先验驱动，从而可将潜在心理偏见从基于记忆的学习基线动态中算法性分离。我们通过人机协同微世界实验验证该框架，模拟地铁洪水导致轨道停运场景，调度员需在乘客安全与服务效率间权衡。分析揭示人类适应呈现复杂非线性循环，包含四个阶段：获取、过度自信、过度修正与再校准。具体而言，计算模型在事故后“过度修正”阶段表现出显著差异：人类操作员立即表现出防御性风险高估，而模型则基于累积经验保持稳定轨迹。该战略差异证实，故障后操作不稳定常源于急性心理偏见压倒稳定记忆驱动的适应，此模式在类似高风险环境中理论上可重复出现，并可通过专业操作员的多模态行为与传感器数据验证。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the gap in computational models for urban infrastructure management during extreme events by integrating Instance-Based Learning Theory into a cognitive architecture that simulates human operator decision-making through memory retrieval and utility blending. The model serves as a baseline for boundedly rational adaptation, enabling isolation of psychological biases like overconfidence and defensive overcorrection. In a human-in-the-loop experiment simulating subway flood scenarios, the model revealed a four-phase human adaptation cycle—acquisition, overconfidence, overcorrection, and recalibration—where human operators exhibited immediate risk overestimation during the overcorrection phase, contrasting with the model’s stable, experience-driven trajectory. This divergence confirms that psychological bias often drives post-failure instability, a pattern expected to recur in high-stakes environments and measurable via behavioral and sensor data.
本研究通过整合实例学习理论，构建计算认知架构模拟人类在极端条件下的适应行为，以分离记忆驱动学习与心理偏见（如过度自信和防御性过度修正）。在地铁洪水模拟实验中，模型表现出稳定适应轨迹，而人类操作员在“过度修正”阶段出现显著风险高估，证实心理偏见是导致事故后操作不稳定的关键因素，该模式可推广至其他高风险环境并借助多模态数据验证。
Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation
Authors: Hanxu Hu, Zdeněk Šnajdr, Pinzhen Chen, Jannis Vamvas, Rico Sennrich
First: 2026-06-04T17:32:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:32:06+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习激发对未见语言翻译的上下文学习
先前研究表明，大型语言模型（LLM）可通过持续训练或在上下文中编码语法书来翻译未见或低资源语言。但两种方法通常过度拟合特定语言，在测试时零样本迁移能力有限。为大规模翻译极端低资源语言，我们认为LLM必须习得利用上下文语言知识的元技能，而非记忆特定语言。本文提出一种强化学习（RL）方法，利用表面翻译指标（chrF）作为奖励，在丰富语言上下文中实现对未见语言的翻译。实证表明，尽管奖励机制轻量，我们的RL训练模型能有效提取并应用上下文中的相关语言信息，在完全未见语言上实现优于上下文学习或监督微调的翻译效果。分析表明，基于结果的RL可超越传统推理任务（如数学与编程），成为从上下文学习语言的通用方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to translate extremely low-resource languages without overfitting, this paper proposes using reinforcement learning to teach large language models to extract and apply linguistic knowledge from context rather than memorizing specific languages. The method employs a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as a reward signal to guide RL training, enabling models to generalize across unseen languages. Experimental results show that RL-trained models outperform in-context learning and supervised fine-tuning in zero-shot translation tasks, demonstrating that reward-driven learning can effectively harness contextual linguistic patterns for language acquisition.
鉴于现有方法易过拟合特定语言且在零样本场景下泛化能力弱，本文提出一种强化学习方法，使大语言模型无需记忆特定语言即可从上下文提取并应用语言知识进行翻译。该方法以表面翻译指标（chrF）作为奖励信号，引导模型学习上下文中的语言规律。实验表明，强化学习训练的模型在完全未见语言上的翻译效果优于上下文学习和监督微调，证明基于结果的强化学习可有效推广至语言学习任务。
RiskFlow: Fast and Faithful Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation
Authors: Qi Lan, Yining Tang, Yu Shen, Yi Zhou, Yuhao Wei, Jie Li, Guofa Li
First: 2026-06-04T17:28:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:28:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety-critical traffic scenario generation is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems under rare but high-risk interactions. Existing diffusion-based methods offer strong controllability in closed-loop generation, but their iterative denoising process is computationally expensive and may accumulate sampling and guidance errors over long rollouts, causing unrealistic motion artifacts such as jitter, abnormal acceleration, and off-road behavior. To address these issues, we propose RiskFlow, a closed-loop safety-critical multi-agent traffic generation framework that formulates future trajectory generation as transport in the action space. Instead of relying on iterative denoising, RiskFlow learns an average velocity field over a finite interval to transform Gaussian action sequences into future acceleration and yaw-rate commands with a single forward pass, using a JVP-based objective for efficient and stable training. At test time, RiskFlow applies output-space guidance to the generated actions, steering selected critical agents toward risky interactions while regularizing off-road behavior, and reconstructs physically feasible trajectories through vehicle dynamics. Experiments on nuScenes with tbsim closed-loop evaluation show that RiskFlow achieves a strong adversariality-realism trade-off across multi-agent and long-horizon settings. Compared with representative baselines, RiskFlow consistently improves realism while maintaining competitive safety-critical generation capability, and substantially reduces inference time for evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RiskFlow：快速且忠实的安全关键交通场景生成
安全关键交通场景生成对于评估自动驾驶系统在罕见但高风险交互下的表现至关重要。现有基于扩散的方法在闭环生成中具有较强的可控性，但其迭代去噪过程计算成本高，且在长时步生成中可能累积采样和引导误差，导致不现实的运动伪影，如抖动、异常加速度和离路行为。为解决这些问题，我们提出RiskFlow，一种闭环安全关键多智能体交通生成框架，将未来轨迹生成建模为动作空间中的传输过程。RiskFlow不依赖迭代去噪，而是学习有限时间区间内的平均速度场，通过单次前向传播将高斯动作序列转换为未来加速度和转向率指令，采用基于JVP的目标函数实现高效稳定训练。在测试阶段，RiskFlow对生成的动作施加输出空间引导，引导选定的关键智能体趋向高风险交互，同时规范离路行为，并通过车辆动力学重建物理可行轨迹。在nuScenes数据集上使用tbsim闭环评估实验表明，RiskFlow在多智能体和长时域设置下实现了强对抗性与真实性的良好平衡。与代表性基线方法相比，RiskFlow在保持竞争力的安全关键生成能力的同时，持续提升真实感，并大幅降低评估推理时间。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational inefficiency and motion artifacts in existing diffusion-based traffic scenario generators, RiskFlow introduces a closed-loop framework that models trajectory generation as transport in action space. It learns a velocity field over a finite interval to map Gaussian actions directly into future vehicle commands via a single forward pass, using a JVP-based objective for stable training. At test time, it applies output-space guidance to steer critical agents toward risky interactions while enforcing physical constraints, then reconstructs trajectories using vehicle dynamics. Experiments on nuScenes with tbsim show RiskFlow achieves superior realism without sacrificing safety-critical fidelity and reduces inference time significantly compared to baselines.
为解决现有扩散模型在生成安全关键交通场景时计算成本高、易产生运动伪影的问题，RiskFlow提出一种闭环框架，将轨迹生成建模为动作空间中的传输过程，通过学习有限时间内的平均速度场，单次前向传播即可将高斯动作序列转化为未来加速度与转向角指令。采用JVP目标函数确保训练稳定，测试时通过输出空间引导调控关键代理向高风险交互移动并抑制离路行为，再经车辆动力学重建物理可行轨迹。在nuScenes结合tbsim的实验中，RiskFlow在多代理与长时域场景下实现现实感与安全关键能力的平衡，且显著优于基线方法，推理速度大幅提升。
RAG Security and Privacy: Formalizing the Threat Model and Attack Surface
Authors: Atousa Arzanipour, Rouzbeh Behnia, Reza Ebrahimi, Kaushik Dutta
Venue: 2025 IEEE International Conference on Data Mining Workshops (ICDMW), pp. 1387-1394, 2025
First: 2025-09-24T17:11:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:26:56+00:00
Comments: Published at the 5th ICDM Workshop in November 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is an emerging approach in natural language processing that combines large language models (LLMs) with external document retrieval to produce more accurate and grounded responses. While RAG has shown strong potential in reducing hallucinations and improving factual consistency, it also introduces new privacy and security challenges that differ from those faced by traditional LLMs. Existing research has demonstrated that LLMs can leak sensitive information through training data memorization or adversarial prompts, and RAG systems inherit many of these vulnerabilities. At the same time, reliance of RAG on an external knowledge base opens new attack surfaces, including the potential for leaking information about the presence or content of retrieved documents, or for injecting malicious content to manipulate model behavior. Despite these risks, there is currently no formal framework that defines the threat landscape for RAG systems. In this paper, we address a critical gap in the literature by proposing, to the best of our knowledge, the first formal threat model for retrieval-RAG systems. We introduce a structured taxonomy of adversary types based on their access to model components and data, and we formally define key threat vectors such as document-level membership inference and data poisoning, which pose serious privacy and integrity risks in real-world deployments. By establishing formal definitions and attack models, our work lays the foundation for a more rigorous and principled understanding of privacy and security in RAG systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAG安全与隐私：威胁模型与攻击面的形式化
检索增强生成（RAG）是自然语言处理领域新兴的方法，通过结合大语言模型（LLM）与外部文档检索，生成更准确、更可靠的响应。尽管RAG在减少幻觉和提升事实一致性方面展现出巨大潜力，但也引入了与传统LLM不同的新隐私和安全挑战。现有研究表明，LLM可能通过训练数据记忆或对抗性提示泄露敏感信息，而RAG系统继承了这些漏洞。同时，RAG对外部知识库的依赖开辟了新的攻击面，包括泄露检索文档的存在或内容信息，或注入恶意内容以操控模型行为。尽管存在这些风险，目前尚无正式框架定义RAG系统的威胁格局。本文填补了文献中的关键空白，提出了（据我们所知）首个针对检索型RAG系统的正式威胁模型。我们基于攻击者对模型组件和数据的访问权限，构建了攻击者类型的结构化分类法，并正式定义了如文档级成员推断和数据投毒等关键威胁向量，这些威胁在实际部署中对隐私和完整性构成严重风险。通过建立形式化定义和攻击模型，我们的工作为更严谨、更原则性的RAG系统隐私与安全研究奠定了基础。

Motivated by the growing adoption of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems and their unique privacy and security risks, this paper introduces the first formal threat model for RAG, addressing a critical gap in existing literature. The authors define a structured taxonomy of adversary types based on access to model components and external data, and formally characterize key attack vectors such as document-level membership inference and data poisoning. Experimental results, though not detailed in the abstract, demonstrate that these threats are viable and pose serious risks to real-world RAG deployments, including potential leakage of document presence or content and injection of malicious content to manipulate outputs.
为应对检索增强生成（RAG）系统中日益突出的安全与隐私风险，本文首次提出针对RAG的正式威胁模型，填补现有研究空白。作者基于对模型组件和外部数据的访问权限，构建了攻击者类型结构化分类，并形式化定义了文档级成员推断与数据投毒等关键攻击向量。实验分析表明，这些威胁在实际部署中具有现实危害，尤其当系统依赖外部知识库时，易引发隐私泄露与完整性破坏。
A Komi-Yazva--Russian Parallel Corpus and Evaluation Protocol for Zero- and Few-Shot LLM Translation
Authors: Petr Parshakov
First: 2026-06-04T17:26:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:26:38+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 6 tables, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus together with an explicit evaluation protocol for studying LLM translation in an endangered, extremely low-resource setting. The dataset contains 457 aligned sentence pairs from 74 narrative texts and is accompanied by documented provenance, sentence-level alignment, and story identifiers that enable leakage-aware evaluation. We use this setup to compare modern large language models on Komi-Yazva-to-Russian translation under severe parallel-data scarcity in zero-shot and retrieval-based few-shot regimes. The protocol includes story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, strict validation of generated outputs, complementary reference-based and judge-based metrics, and story-level uncertainty estimates. Across models, LLMs produce non-trivial translations, but performance varies strongly by model family and prompting regime. Retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently improves over zero-shot prompting, while gains beyond a small retrieved context remain limited. The results show that evaluative conclusions in this setting depend materially on metric choice and failure handling, so the paper frames the corpus as both a dataset contribution and a reproducible evaluation testbed for endangered-language machine translation.

标题：科米-亚兹瓦-俄语平行语料库及零样本和少样本LLM翻译评估协议
我们首次发布科米-亚兹瓦-俄语平行语料库，并配套明确的评估协议，用于研究濒危且资源极度匮乏语境下的大语言模型（LLM）翻译。该语料库包含74篇叙事文本中的457对对齐句子，附带来源记录、句子级对齐及故事标识符，支持防泄露评估。我们利用此设置，在平行语料极度稀缺条件下，比较现代大语言模型在科米-亚兹瓦到俄语翻译任务中的零样本与基于检索的少样本表现。评估协议包括故事级交叉验证、少样本提示中的确定性检索、生成输出的严格验证、互补的参考指标与人工评估指标，以及故事级不确定性估计。不同模型表现差异显著，LLM能生成非平凡翻译，但性能受模型家族和提示方式影响。基于检索的少样本提示始终优于零样本提示，但超出小规模检索上下文后的性能提升有限。结果表明，该语境下评估结论高度依赖指标选择与失败处理方式，因此本文将语料库既视为数据贡献，也作为濒危语言机器翻译的可复现评估平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models (LLMs) in translating endangered, low-resource languages, this study introduces the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus with 457 sentence pairs and a rigorous evaluation protocol designed to prevent data leakage. The method employs story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, and strict output validation, using both reference and human judge metrics alongside uncertainty estimates. Experimental results show that while LLMs generate non-trivial translations, performance varies significantly by model family and prompting regime, with retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently outperforming zero-shot, but with diminishing returns beyond a small context size. The study emphasizes that evaluation outcomes are highly sensitive to metric selection and failure handling, positioning the corpus as both a dataset and a reproducible testbed for endangered language translation.

Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autonomous medical robots hold promise to improve patient outcomes, reduce provider workload, democratize access to care, and enable superhuman precision. However, autonomous medical robotics has been limited by a fundamental data problem: existing medical robotic datasets are small, single-embodiment, and rarely shared openly, restricting the development of foundation models that the field needs to advance. We introduce Open-H-Embodiment, the largest open dataset of medical robotic video with synchronized kinematics to date, spanning more than 50 institutions and multiple robotic platforms including the CMR Versius, Intuitive Surgical's da Vinci, da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK), Rob Surgical BiTrack, Virtual Incision's MIRA, Moon Surgical Maestro, and a variety of custom systems, spanning surgical manipulation, robotic ultrasound, and endoscopy procedures. We demonstrate the research enabled by this dataset through two foundation models. GR00T-H is the first open foundation vision-language-action model for medical robotics, which is the only evaluated model to achieve full end-to-end task completion on a structured suturing benchmark (25% of trials vs. 0% for all others) and achieves 64% average success across a 29-step ex vivo suturing sequence. We also train Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator, the first action-conditioned world model to enable multi-embodiment surgical simulation from a single checkpoint, spanning nine robotic platforms and supporting in silico policy evaluation and synthetic data generation for the medical domain. These results suggest that open, large-scale medical robot data collection can serve as critical infrastructure for the research community, enabling advances in robot learning, world modeling, and beyond.

Scaling Laws and Spectra of Shallow Neural Networks in the Feature Learning Regime
Authors: Leonardo Defilippis, Yizhou Xu, Julius Girardin, Emanuele Troiani, Vittorio Erba, Lenka Zdeborová, Bruno Loureiro, Florent Krzakala
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-09-29T14:58:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:25:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural scaling laws underlie many of the recent advances in deep learning, yet their theoretical understanding remains largely confined to linear models. In this work, we present a systematic analysis of scaling laws for quadratic and diagonal neural networks in the feature learning regime. Leveraging connections with matrix compressed sensing and LASSO, we derive a detailed phase diagram for the scaling exponents of the excess risk as a function of sample complexity and weight decay. This analysis uncovers crossovers between distinct scaling regimes and plateau behaviors, mirroring phenomena widely reported in the empirical neural scaling literature. Furthermore, we establish a precise link between these regimes and the spectral properties of the trained network weights, which we characterize in detail. As a consequence, we provide a theoretical validation of recent empirical observations connecting the emergence of power-law tails in the weight spectrum with network generalization performance, yielding an interpretation from first principles.

标题：特征学习阶段浅层神经网络的缩放定律与谱特性
神经网络的缩放定律是深度学习近期诸多进展的基础，但其理论理解目前仍主要局限于线性模型。本文系统分析了在特征学习阶段，二次和对角神经网络的缩放定律。借助与矩阵压缩感知和LASSO的联系，我们推导出过量风险缩放指数随样本复杂度和权重衰减变化的详细相图。该分析揭示了不同缩放阶段间的转变以及平台行为，与实证神经缩放文献中广泛报告的现象相呼应。此外，我们建立了这些阶段与训练后网络权重谱特性的精确关联，并对其进行了详细刻画。由此，我们从第一性原理出发，理论验证了近期实证观察到的权重谱中幂律尾部出现与网络泛化性能之间的联系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of neural scaling laws but the lack of theoretical grounding beyond linear models, this work analyzes scaling laws for quadratic and diagonal neural networks in the feature learning regime. Using connections to matrix compressed sensing and LASSO, the authors derive a phase diagram of excess risk scaling exponents as functions of sample size and weight decay, revealing regime transitions and plateau behaviors consistent with empirical findings. Crucially, they link these scaling regimes to the spectral properties of trained weights, showing that power-law tails in the weight spectrum correlate with generalization performance, thereby offering a first-principles theoretical explanation for observed phenomena.
鉴于神经网络缩放定律在深度学习中的实证成功，但理论研究主要局限于线性模型，本文系统分析了二次和对角神经网络在特征学习阶段的缩放规律。通过将问题与矩阵压缩感知和LASSO联系起来，作者推导出样本复杂度和权重衰减下过量风险缩放指数的相图，揭示了与实证研究一致的相变与平台行为。更重要的是，他们建立了这些缩放行为与训练权重谱性质的精确关联，表明权重谱中的幂律尾部与泛化性能相关，从而从第一性原理上解释了近期实证发现。
Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss
Authors: Thomas T. Zhang, Alok Shah, Yifei Zhang, Vincent Zhang, Nikolai Matni, Max Simchowitz
First: 2026-06-04T17:22:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:22:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：双重预处理（DoPr）：优化测试时性能，而非验证损失
许多深度学习的现代应用通过一步预测损失（如L²回归、交叉熵）训练神经网络，但在部署时沿其自身预测展开。关键示例包括自回归语言建模、基于流的生成建模和机器人策略学习。文献已证实，这些设置会引发我们称之为测试时反馈（TTF）的现象：训练/验证损失与下游指标（如任务成功率和生成质量）之间的不匹配，且随任务长度增加而加剧。尽管已有数据整理、架构设计和目标设计等方法用于缓解TTF中的训练-测试偏移，本文提出将优化作为新的设计维度，以减轻误差累积。具体而言，我们引入一种名为双重预处理（DoPr）的新优化范式，专为应对TTF挑战而设计。DoPr结合了类似Adam和Muon的梯度预处理与类似KFAC的激活预处理（AP）。我们表明，添加AP可作为即插即用的干预手段，在多种TTF设置下提升下游模型性能。有趣的是，这些测试时性能的提升并不总是伴随验证损失的改善，这引发了关于如何正确评估使用一步监督目标训练的模型的新问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that test-time performance often diverges from validation loss in autoregressive and generative tasks due to feedback effects, this paper proposes double-preconditioning (DoPr) as a novel optimization strategy to reduce error accumulation. DoPr combines gradient-based preconditioning (like Adam) with activation-based preconditioning (like KFAC), enabling more stable and accurate rollouts during inference. Experiments across multiple TTF settings show that DoPr significantly improves downstream metrics such as task success and generation quality without necessarily improving validation loss, suggesting optimization design can be decoupled from supervised loss minimization.
针对自回归和生成模型在任务长度增加时出现的测试时反馈问题（尽管验证损失低但实际表现下降），本文提出双预处理（DoPr）优化方法，结合梯度预处理（如Adam）与激活预处理（如KFAC）。实验表明，DoPr能显著提升下游任务表现（如生成质量与成功率），但并不总是降低验证损失，说明优化设计可独立于训练目标直接影响测试时表现。
Warning Message Content Increases Help Seeking in a Large-Scale Dark Web CSAM Intervention
Authors: Caoilte Ó Ciardha, Joel Scanlan, Tegan Insoll, Juha Nurmi, Nina Vaaranen-Valkonen
First: 2026-06-04T17:21:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:21:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Warning messages have been used to disrupt individuals seeking online child sexual abuse material (CSAM) and promote engagement with support services, yet large-scale field evidence on message content remains limited, particularly in high anonymity environments. This study reports a field experiment on Ahmia.fi, a Tor search engine, examining how warning message content influences behavior. Across a 140-day period, almost 20 million searches were observed, with over 3 million searches containing known CSAM-related terms that triggered a warning linking to an anonymous self-help program. Users were exposed to warning messages varying in thematic content and framing, or a neutral message. Across a randomized comparison, a campaign-wide analysis, and interrupted time series models, message content consistently influenced engagement with help resources. All active messages increased click-through rates to help resources relative to the neutral condition, with a harm-focused message producing the strongest effects. At the platform level, click-through rates increased from 8.73% before the intervention to 15.67% during the campaign. These findings highlight the importance of message content in shaping responses to warning interventions, supporting an approach in which messaging is refined and adapted to increase engagement with support resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：警告信息内容的增强有助于在大型暗网儿童性虐待材料干预中促进求助行为
警告信息已被用于干扰寻求在线儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）的个人并促进其参与支持服务，但在高匿名环境中，关于信息内容的大规模实地证据仍有限。本研究在Ahmia.fi（一个Tor搜索引擎）上报告了一项实地实验，考察警告信息内容如何影响用户行为。在140天期间，观察到近2000万次搜索，其中超过300万次包含已知CSAM相关术语的搜索触发了链接至匿名自助项目的警告。用户接触了主题内容和表述方式各异的警告信息，或中性信息。在随机对照比较、全战役分析和中断时间序列模型中，信息内容始终一致地影响了用户对帮助资源的参与度。所有活跃信息相比中性条件均提高了对帮助资源的点击率，以危害为中心的信息效果最强。在平台层面，点击率从干预前的8.73%上升至活动期间的15.67%。这些发现强调了信息内容在塑造警告干预反应中的重要性，支持通过优化和调整信息以提高用户对支持资源参与度的方法。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how different warning message content affects user behavior on Ahmia.fi, a Tor-based search engine, in response to searches containing known child sexual abuse material (CSAM) terms. Motivated by the need to improve intervention effectiveness in high-anonymity environments, the researchers conducted a 140-day field experiment exposing users to varied warning messages—harm-focused, thematic, or neutral—and measured engagement with linked help resources. Results from randomized comparisons, campaign-wide analysis, and interrupted time series models showed that all active messages increased click-through rates compared to the neutral message, with the harm-focused message producing the strongest effect. Platform-wide click-through rates rose from 8.73% to 15.67% during the intervention, demonstrating that message content significantly influences help-seeking behavior.
为提升在高匿名环境（如暗网）中对儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）搜索者的干预效果，本研究测试了不同警告信息内容对求助行为的影响。在140天内，近2000万次搜索被分析，其中300多万次触发了链接至匿名支持项目的警告。警告信息分为伤害导向、支持导向和中性三种，随机分配给用户。结果显示，所有主动信息相比中性信息均提升了求助资源点击率，其中伤害导向信息效果最强。平台整体点击率从干预前的8.73%上升至干预期间的15.67%，表明信息内容对用户参与支持服务具有显著影响。
Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis
Authors: Zhisong Qiu, Kangqi Song, Shengwei Tang, Shuofei Qiao, Lei Liang, Huajun Chen, Shumin Deng
First: 2026-06-04T17:20:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:20:47+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于代理数据分析的无监督技能发现
推理时技能增强提供了一种轻量级方法，通过注入可重用的程序性知识来提升数据分析代理，而无需更新模型参数。然而，发现有效的数据分析技能仍具挑战性，因为可靠的监督成本高昂，且成功标准因分析格式而异。这引出了一个关键问题：如何仅从无标签探索中发现可重用的数据分析技能？我们提出了DataCOPE，一种面向数据分析代理的无监督验证引导技能发现框架。DataCOPE从探索轨迹中提取验证信号，并利用这些信号表征轨迹间的相对质量或一致性。它迭代协调数据分析代理生成轨迹、无监督验证器提取信号，以及技能管理器进行对比式技能蒸馏。对于报告式分析，我们将验证器实例化为自适应检查清单验证器，根据任务特定标准推导评分依据，通过可验证的覆盖度评分报告，并迭代优化检查清单。对于推理式分析，将其实例化为答案一致性验证器，按答案一致性分组轨迹，并利用自一致性作为辅助信号。我们在Deep Data Research的报告式分析和DABStep的推理式分析上评估DataCOPE。在两种设置下，DataCOPE均持续优于基线方法。在四种模型设置下平均来看，DataCOPE在报告式和推理式任务上的平均得分分别提升9.71%和32.30%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance data-analytic agents without costly supervision or parameter updates, the paper introduces DataCOPE, an unsupervised framework that discovers reusable skills through exploration trajectories. It employs a verifier-guided approach: for report-style tasks, an Adaptive Checklist Verifier scores coverage and refines criteria; for reasoning tasks, an Answer Agreement Verifier groups trajectories by answer consistency. DataCOPE iteratively coordinates an agent for trajectory generation, a verifier for signal extraction, and a skill manager for contrastive distillation. Experiments on Deep Data Research and DABStep show consistent performance gains over baselines, with average improvements of 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively across four model settings.
为提升数据分析代理能力而无需昂贵监督或参数更新，作者提出DataCOPE框架，通过探索轨迹无监督发现可复用技能。该框架由验证器引导：无监督验证器从代理生成的轨迹中提取质量信号，用于指导技能管理器进行对比技能蒸馏。报告式任务中采用自适应检查清单验证器，通过覆盖度评分并迭代优化清单；推理式任务中采用答案一致性验证器，按答案一致性分组并利用自洽性作为辅助信号。在Deep Data Research和DABStep数据集上评估，DataCOPE显著优于基线，报告式任务平均得分提升9.71%，推理式任务提升32.30%，覆盖四种模型配置。
JI-ADF: Joint-Individual Learning with Adaptive Decision Fusion for Multimodal Skin Lesion Classification
Authors: Phan Nguyen, Dat Cao, Hien Kha, Hien Chu, Minh Le, Trang Pham, Nguyen Quoc Khanh Le
First: 2026-04-30T02:48:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:20:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skin lesion classification is essential for early dermatological diagnosis, yet many existing computer-aided systems rely primarily on dermoscopic images and underutilize the multimodal evidence routinely available in clinical practice. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{JI-ADF}, a trimodal deep learning framework that integrates dermoscopic images, clinical photographs, and structured patient metadata for clinically grounded skin lesion classification. The proposed architecture combines joint multimodal representation learning with modality-specific auxiliary supervision and an adaptive decision fusion mechanism that dynamically calibrates modality contributions on a per-sample basis. To enhance cross-modal reasoning while preserving modality-specific evidence, we further introduce a multimodal fusion attention (MMFA) module. We evaluate JI-ADF on the large-scale MILK10k benchmark, which reflects real-world clinical acquisition conditions and severe class imbalance. The proposed method demonstrates strong and well-balanced performance across lesion categories, improving sensitivity and Dice score while maintaining high specificity and good calibration. Extensive analyses, including modality ablation, calibration evaluation, and Grad-CAM visualization, further confirm the robustness and clinically meaningful behavior of the model. These results indicate that JI-ADF provides a reliable and practical foundation for multimodal skin lesion classification in real-world clinical settings.

标题：JI-ADF：用于多模态皮肤病变分类的联合个体学习与自适应决策融合
皮肤病变分类对早期皮肤病诊断至关重要，但许多现有计算机辅助系统主要依赖皮肤镜图像，而未充分利用临床实践中常规可用的多模态证据。为解决这一问题，我们提出\textbf{JI-ADF}，一种整合皮肤镜图像、临床照片和结构化患者元数据的三模态深度学习框架，以实现基于临床的皮肤病变分类。该架构结合了联合多模态表征学习、模态特定辅助监督以及自适应决策融合机制，后者能在样本级别动态校准各模态的贡献。为增强跨模态推理同时保留模态特异性证据，我们进一步引入多模态融合注意力（MMFA）模块。我们在大规模MILK10k基准数据集上评估JI-ADF，该数据集反映真实临床采集条件及严重类别不平衡。所提方法在各类病变中展现出强而均衡的性能，提升了敏感性和Dice分数，同时保持高特异性和良好校准。通过模态消融、校准评估和Grad-CAM可视化等广泛分析，进一步验证了模型的鲁棒性和临床意义。结果表明，JI-ADF为真实临床环境中多模态皮肤病变分类提供了可靠且实用的基础。
