Unsupervised Monocular 3D Keypoint Discovery from Multi-View Diffusion Priors
Authors: Subin Jeon, In Cho, Junyoung Hong, Woong Oh Cho, Seon Joo Kim
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-07-16T15:29:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:18:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR 2026. Project page: https://subin6.github.io/keydiff3d-project/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Most existing 3D keypoint estimation methods rely on manual annotations or calibrated multi-view images, both of which are expensive to collect. This paper introduces KeyDiff3D, a framework that can accurately predict 3D keypoints from a single image, thus eliminating the need for such expensive data acquisitions. To achieve this, we leverage powerful geometric priors embedded in a pretrained multi-view diffusion model. In our framework, the diffusion model generates multi-view images from a single image, serving as supervision signals to provide 3D geometric cues to our model. We also introduce a 3D feature extractor that transforms implicit 3D priors embedded in the diffusion features into explicit 3D feature volumes. Beyond accurate keypoint estimation, we further introduce a pipeline that enables manipulation of 3D objects generated by the diffusion model. Experimental results on diverse datasets, including Human3.6M, CUB-200-2011, Stanford Dogs, and several in-the-wild and out-of-domain inputs, highlight the effectiveness of our method in terms of accuracy, generalization, and its ability to enable manipulation of 3D objects generated by the diffusion model from a single image.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从多视角扩散先验中无监督单目3D关键点发现
现有大多数3D关键点估计方法依赖人工标注或校准的多视角图像，两者均难以收集。本文提出KeyDiff3D框架，可从单张图像准确预测3D关键点，从而无需昂贵的数据采集。为此，我们利用预训练多视角扩散模型中蕴含的强大几何先验。在该框架中，扩散模型从单张图像生成多视角图像，作为监督信号为模型提供3D几何线索。我们还引入3D特征提取器，将扩散特征中隐含的3D先验转化为显式的3D特征体。除准确的关键点估计外，我们进一步提出一个管道，使用户能够操控扩散模型生成的3D物体。在包括Human3.6M、CUB-200-2011、Stanford Dogs及多种野外和域外输入数据集上的实验结果，突显了本方法在精度、泛化能力及从单张图像操控扩散模型生成3D物体方面的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of manual annotations and calibrated multi-view data in 3D keypoint estimation, this work proposes KeyDiff3D, a framework that predicts 3D keypoints from a single image using geometric priors from a pretrained multi-view diffusion model. The method leverages the diffusion model to generate multi-view images from a single input, using these as supervision to extract 3D geometric cues, and employs a 3D feature extractor to convert implicit priors into explicit 3D feature volumes. Experiments on Human3.6M, CUB-200-2011, Stanford Dogs, and out-of-domain datasets demonstrate superior accuracy and generalization, while also enabling 3D object manipulation from a single image.

A Vision-language Framework for Comparative Reasoning in Radiology
Authors: Tengfei Zhang, Ziheng Zhao, Lisong Dai, Xiaoman Zhang, Pengcheng Qiu, Ya Zhang, Yanfeng Wang, Weidi Xie
First: 2026-06-04T17:12:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:12:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical imaging artificial intelligence has achieved strong performance in isolated image interpretation, but remains poorly aligned with radiological practice, where diagnosis and follow-up rely on comparison across prior studies and analogous reference cases. Here we formulate radiological comparison as an entity-aware cross-image reasoning problem and introduce a framework that supports both reference-case retrieval and temporal comparative interpretation. We construct MedReCo-DB, a large-scale comparative imaging resource derived from routine image-report pairs, comprising more than 690,000 images from over 160,000 patients across eight institutions, four countries and seven imaging modalities. Reports are decomposed into anatomical structures, abnormal findings and pathological conditions to provide supervision for entity-conditioned retrieval and comparative visual question answering. Using this resource, we develop MedReCo, an entity-aware visual encoder for controllable retrieval of clinically analogous cases, and MedReCo-VLM, a vision--language extension for generative interpretation of interval change. Across internal, external and cross-center evaluations, MedReCo achieved the highest Recall@1 in all 12 internal retrieval settings and improved external retrieval by a mean of 6.0 percentage points. In clinically confusable differential groups, it consistently outperformed the strongest baselines. MedReCo-VLM achieved the best performance across all comparative generation evaluations and improved longitudinal follow-up accuracy by 14.5-46.5 percentage points on chest radiographs and 13.0-27.9 percentage points on CT. These findings suggest that entity-aware comparative reasoning can be learned from routine clinical data at scale and may provide a more clinically aligned foundation for medical imaging AI.

标题：用于放射学比较推理的视觉-语言框架

PHUMA: Physically Reliable Humanoid Locomotion Dataset
Authors: Kyungmin Lee, Sibeen Kim, Youngdo Lee, Minho Park, Hyunseung Kim, Dongyoon Hwang, Donghu Kim, Hojoon Lee, Jaegul Choo
First: 2025-10-30T08:13:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:11:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Motion imitation is a promising approach for humanoid locomotion, enabling agents to acquire humanlike behaviors. Existing methods typically rely on high-quality motion capture datasets such as AMASS, but these are scarce and expensive, limiting scalability and diversity. Recent studies attempt to scale data collection by converting large-scale internet videos, exemplified by Humanoid-X. However, they often suffer from physical artifacts such as floating, penetration, and foot skating, which hinder stable imitation. To address this, we introduce PHUMA, a Physically Reliable HUMAnoid locomotion dataset produced by a two-stage pipeline combining physics-aware curation and physics-constrained retargeting, aggregating both motion capture and internet video into a physically reliable, 73-hour corpus. On motion tracking benchmarks, PHUMA-trained policies achieve higher success rates than those trained on AMASS and Humanoid-X, and successfully transfer zero-shot to a real Unitree G1. The code is available at https://davian-robotics.github.io/PHUMA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PHUMA：物理可靠的人形机器人运动数据集
运动模仿是实现人形机器人运动的一种有前景的方法，使智能体能够习得类人行为。现有方法通常依赖高质量的动作捕捉数据集（如AMASS），但此类数据稀缺且昂贵，限制了其可扩展性和多样性。近期研究尝试通过将大规模互联网视频转换为数据以扩大数据规模，例如Humanoid-X。然而，这些方法常存在物理伪影（如漂浮、穿透和脚部滑动），阻碍了稳定模仿。为此，我们提出了PHUMA，一个通过物理感知筛选与物理约束重定向两阶段流程生成的人形机器人运动数据集，整合动作捕捉与互联网视频，形成73小时的物理可靠数据集。在运动跟踪基准测试中，基于PHUMA训练的策略比基于AMASS和Humanoid-X的策略成功率更高，并成功实现零样本迁移至真实Unitree G1机器人。代码见https://davian-robotics.github.io/PHUMA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and physically realistic humanoid motion data, this work introduces PHUMA, a 73-hour dataset created via a two-stage pipeline combining physics-aware curation and physics-constrained retargeting to eliminate artifacts like floating and penetration. Unlike prior datasets relying on expensive motion capture or noisy internet videos, PHUMA integrates both sources while enforcing physical consistency. Experimental results show that policies trained on PHUMA outperform those trained on AMASS or Humanoid-X on motion tracking benchmarks and successfully transfer zero-shot to a real Unitree G1 robot.
为解决现有数据集稀缺且物理不真实的问题，本文提出PHUMA数据集，通过物理感知筛选与物理约束重定向的两阶段流程，整合动作捕捉与网络视频数据，生成73小时物理可靠的人形运动数据。实验表明，基于PHUMA训练的策略在运动跟踪基准上优于AMASS和Humanoid-X，并能零样本成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人上。
Synapse: Federated Tool Routing via Typed Compendium Artifacts
Authors: Abhijit Chakraborty, Yash Shah, Vivek Gupta
First: 2026-01-31T21:43:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:10:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The unit of collaboration in federated learning determines what guarantees are even expressible. Flat units like weights, prompts, raw examples, carry no type signature on which privacy, conflict resolution, or cross-model transfer can dispatch as well-defined operations. We propose typed federated artifacts: schema validated objects whose declared field structure makes per field differential privacy, schema aware merging, and cross architectural transfer first-class operations rather than heuristic approximations. We instantiate this as SYNAPSE, a compendium for federated tool routing across clients with frozen, heterogeneous LLMs and no shared data or weights which is a setting flat units cannot handle without either leaking gradients or discarding structure. The compendium admits a typed merge operator with field wise conflict resolution, a formal DP guarantee on numeric metadata, and conditional retrieval distortion and routing-stability results empirically characterized on five distributions, including one where the contraction premise fails. A single compendium transfers across four LLM families (LLaMA 3.18B,LLaMA 3.2-3B, Mistral 7B, GPT 4o) with approximately 2 pt loss, a capability weight-sharing federation cannot provide without architectural matching.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Synapse：通过类型化综合制品实现联邦工具路由
联邦学习中的协作单元决定了哪些保证能够被明确表达。扁平单元（如权重、提示、原始示例）不携带类型签名，无法支持隐私保护、冲突解决或跨模型迁移等明确定义的操作。我们提出类型化联邦制品：其声明的字段结构经模式验证，使每字段差分隐私、模式感知合并及跨架构迁移成为一等操作，而非启发式近似。我们将其实例化为SYNAPSE，一个面向客户端的联邦工具路由综合库，适用于冻结的异构大语言模型且无共享数据或权重——这是扁平单元无法处理的场景，否则要么泄露梯度，要么丢弃结构。该综合库支持带字段级冲突解决的类型化合并操作，对数值元数据提供形式化差分隐私保证，并在五个分布（包括收缩前提失效的分布）上实证验证了条件检索失真与路由稳定性。单一综合库可在四个LLM家族（LLaMA 3.18B、LLaMA 3.2-3B、Mistral 7B、GPT 4o）间迁移，损失约2个点，而无架构匹配的权重共享联邦无法实现此能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of flat units like weights or prompts in federated learning—where they lack type signatures to support privacy, conflict resolution, or cross-model transfer—the paper introduces typed federated artifacts, formalized as SYNAPSE, a compendium enabling tool routing across clients with frozen, heterogeneous LLMs without shared data or weights. SYNAPSE validates schema-defined objects, allowing field-wise differential privacy, schema-aware merging, and cross-architectural transfer as first-class operations. Experiments across five distributions, including one violating contraction assumptions, show SYNAPSE achieves approximately 2 point loss when transferring across four LLM families (LLaMA 3.18B, LLaMA 3.2-3B, Mistral 7B, GPT 4o), outperforming weight-sharing federations that require architectural alignment.
为了解决联邦学习中权重或提示等扁平单元缺乏类型签名、难以支持隐私、冲突解决或跨模型迁移的问题，本文提出类型化联邦构件——SYNAPSE，一个支持在无共享数据或权重的异构LLM客户端间路由工具的构件库。SYNAPSE通过字段级差分隐私、结构感知合并与条件检索机制，实现在五种分布（含违反收缩前提者）上的稳定路由与隐私保障，实验表明其在四类LLM间迁移时损失约2点，优于需架构对齐的权重共享联邦方案。
Do Transformers Need Three Projections? Systematic Study of QKV Variants
Authors: Ali Kayyam, Anusha Madan Gopal, M Anthony Lewis
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-01T20:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:08:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026 (PMLR vol. 306). 26 pages, 12 figures, 16 tables. Code: https://github.com/Brainchip-Inc/Do-Transformers-Need-3-Projections
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Transformers have become the standard solution for various AI tasks, with the query, key, and value (QKV) attention formulation playing a central role. However, the individual contribution of these three projections and the impact of omitting some remain poorly understood. We systematically evaluate three projection sharing constraints: a) Q-K=V (shared key-value), b) Q=K-V (shared query-key), and c) Q=K=V (single projection). The last two variants produce symmetric attention maps; to address this, we also explore asymmetric attention via 2D positional encodings. Through experiments spanning synthetic tasks, vision (MNIST, CIFAR, TinyImageNet, anomaly), and language modeling (300M and 1.2B parameter models on 10B tokens), we discovered that our transformers perform on par or occasionally better than the QKV transformer. In language modeling, Q-K=V projection sharing achieves 50% KV cache reduction with only 3.1% perplexity degradation. Crucially, projection sharing is complementary to head sharing (GQA/MQA): combining Q-K=V with GQA-4 yields 87.5% cache reduction, while Q-K=V + MQA achieves 96.9%, enabling practical on-device inference. We show that Q-K=V preserves quality because keys and values can occupy similar representational spaces and attention operates in a low-rank regime, whereas Q=K-V breaks attention directionality. Our results systematically characterize projection sharing as an underexplored instance of weight tying in attention, with direct, quantifiable inference memory benefits, particularly valuable for edge deployment. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Brainchip-Inc/Do-Transformers-Need-3-Projections
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer真的需要三个投影吗？QKV变体的系统研究
Transformer已成为各类AI任务的标准解决方案，其中查询、键和值（QKV）注意力机制起着核心作用。然而，这三个投影各自的贡献以及省略部分的影响尚不明确。我们系统评估了三种投影共享约束：a) Q-K=V（共享键值），b) Q=K-V（共享查询键），c) Q=K=V（单投影）。后两种变体产生对称注意力图；为解决此问题，我们还探索了通过2D位置编码实现的非对称注意力。通过涵盖合成任务、视觉（MNIST、CIFAR、TinyImageNet、异常检测）和语言建模（在10B tokens上训练的300M和1.2B参数模型）的实验，我们发现我们的Transformer表现与QKV Transformer相当，有时甚至更优。在语言建模中，Q-K=V投影共享可实现50%的KV缓存减少，仅伴随3.1%的困惑度下降。关键的是，投影共享与头共享（GQA/MQA）互补：Q-K=V与GQA-4结合可实现87.5%的缓存减少，Q-K=V与MQA结合可实现96.9%，从而支持实用的设备端推理。我们证明Q-K=V能保持质量，因为键和值可占据相似的表征空间，且注意力在低秩范围内运作，而Q=K-V则破坏了注意力的方向性。我们的结果系统性地将投影共享刻画为注意力中未被充分探索的权重共享实例，具有直接且可量化的推理内存收益，尤其对边缘部署极具价值。代码公开于https://github.com/Brainchip-Inc/Do-Transformers-Need-3-Projections

Motivated by the lack of understanding regarding the necessity of three separate projections in Transformer attention, this study systematically evaluates three variants: Q-K=V, Q=K-V, and Q=K=V. Experiments across synthetic, vision, and language modeling tasks show that Q-K=V performs on par or better than standard QKV, achieving 50% KV cache reduction with only 3.1% perplexity increase in language modeling. When combined with GQA-4 or MQA, it enables 87.5% and 96.9% cache reductions respectively, making on-device inference feasible. The authors argue that Q-K=V preserves attention quality because keys and values can share representational space in a low-rank regime, while Q=K-V disrupts attention directionality.
为探究Transformer注意力机制中QKV三投影是否必要，该研究系统评估了三种变体：Q-K=V（共享键值）、Q=K-V（共享查询键）和Q=K=V（单投影），并结合2D位置编码实现非对称注意力。实验覆盖合成、视觉及语言建模任务，结果显示Q-K=V表现与标准QKV相当或更优，在语言建模中可减少50% KV缓存且仅增加3.1%困惑度，与GQA/MQA结合时最高可达96.9%缓存压缩。作者指出Q-K=V因键值可共享表征空间且注意力处于低秩状态而保持性能，而Q=K-V破坏注意力方向性，证明投影共享是边缘部署中具有量化收益的可实用化权重共享技术。
CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments
Authors: Jiaju Chen, Bo Sun, Yuxuan Lu, Yun Wang, Dakuo Wang, Bingsheng Yao
First: 2026-06-04T17:06:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:06:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CollabSim：一种基于计算机支持的协同工作理论的、用于通过受控多智能体实验探究大语言模型代理协作能力的方法
基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）展现出日益增长的潜力，其有效性依赖于智能体通过文本渠道协调的能力，类似于人类团队的协作方式。然而，近期研究表明，MAS往往失败并非因为智能体缺乏独立解决问题的能力，而是因为缺乏协作能力：即建立共同认知、维持共享任务理解、平衡个体与集体激励、并在互动过程中修复认知偏差的能力。计算机支持的协同工作领域数十年的研究已系统刻画了人类团队在受限通信条件下协调所需的能力，但现有MAS评估主要关注任务结果或单智能体在推理、规划和工具使用方面的表现。为系统分析MAS中智能体的协作能力，我们引入CollabSim，一个可配置的仿真框架，结合基于理论的协作能力定义、受控的交互条件操纵以及对智能体内部状态的行动级探测。在四种大语言模型上的实验表明，CollabSim能够捕捉条件效应、区分模型性能模式，并揭示智能体设计对任务的依赖性影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language model multi-agent systems often fail due to insufficient collaborative competence rather than individual task proficiency, the authors introduce CollabSim, a simulation framework grounded in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work theory. This framework enables controlled manipulation of interaction conditions and probes agents' internal states at the action level to systematically evaluate collaborative capabilities such as common ground establishment and incentive alignment. Experiments across four LLMs demonstrate that CollabSim successfully captures condition-dependent effects, distinguishes performance patterns among models, and reveals how agent design impacts collaboration differently across tasks.
鉴于大型语言模型多智能体系统常因缺乏协作能力而非个体能力不足而失败，本研究提出CollabSim框架，该框架基于计算机支持的协同工作理论，通过控制交互条件并探测智能体行为层面的内部状态，系统评估协作能力如共同认知建立与激励平衡。在四类大模型上的实验表明，CollabSim能有效捕捉条件影响、区分模型性能模式，并揭示智能体设计在不同任务中的差异化影响。
The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning
Authors: Shuo Wang, Xiangyu Wang, Quanxin Wang, Bailin Wu, Bokui Wang, Shunyang Huang, Boyan Deng, Haonan Liu, Ruiyi Fang, Zhenxiang Xu, Boyu Wang, Zhao Kang
First: 2026-06-04T17:05:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:05:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

标题：后GCN十年重审：基于曲率分层的关联学习评估
当前关联学习的评估实践严重依赖于平均性能的扁平排行榜，隐含假设底层结构统一。我们证明该假设引入系统性偏差：掩盖了与几何相关的性能差异，可能导致对模型泛化能力的错误结论。本研究识别出内在几何是决定模型效能的关键潜在因素。我们表明，传统聚合指标掩盖了只有在按几何属性分层数据集时才显现的关键性能权衡。为解决此问题，我们提出曲率分层评估框架，将数据集划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率区域。我们的基准评估了18种代表性模型（包括图卷积网络GCNs、图基础模型GFMs及表格学习方法），覆盖14个数据集。我们发现，模型排名在各曲率区域内高度稳定，但在不同区域间显著变化，表明性能本质上依赖于几何结构而非普遍可迁移。值得注意的是，我们识别出某些区域中GFMs相比几何对齐的GNNs收益递减。基于这些发现，我们提出一种几何感知评估协议，相比标准聚合基准提供更可靠、可解释的比较。我们公开所有代码、曲率分层数据集划分及评估工具，以支持未来关联学习方法的可复现与严谨评估。代码与数据集见项目主页：https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/。

GFMs exhibit diminishing returns in certain curvature regimes compared to geometry-aligned GNNs, prompting a geometry-aware evaluation protocol for more reliable comparisons.
为揭示当前关系学习评估中因平均指标导致的几何结构偏差，本文提出按曲率分层的评估框架，将14个数据集划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三类，评估18种模型（含GCN与GFMs）。结果表明，模型排名在各曲率区间内稳定，但跨区间显著变化，说明性能根本依赖于底层几何结构而非通用迁移能力。在特定曲率区间，GFMs相较几何对齐的GNN表现优势减弱，作者据此建议采用几何感知评估协议以提升比较的可靠性。
Risk Assessment of Autonomous Driving: Integrating Technical Failures, Ethical Dilemmas, and Policy Frameworks
Authors: Boyi Chen, Shengqin Chu, Zicheng Wang, Brian Baetz, Zhen Gao
First: 2026-06-04T17:02:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:02:53+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous driving technology has the potential to reduce the large number of road traffic accidents caused by human error each year, but it also brings new types of risks that need to be evaluated from the aspects of technology, ethics and regulations. Based on public crash data from the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA), disengagement reports from the California Department of Motor Vehicles (DMV), the MIT Moral Machines dataset, and a comparative regulatory analysis of five jurisdictions, we have found that the main types of technical failure modes are perception and classification errors. These account for a relatively large proportion of the reported accidents, and it can be concluded that there are different ethical frameworks for autonomous vehicle decision-making, and inconsistent regulations in different areas increase the uncertainty of widespread application. Generally speaking, the problems of technology, ethics and regulation are closely related and need to be solved together. Therefore, this paper recommends a more adaptive and cooperative governance approach that combines engineering standards, ethical discussion, and institutional supervision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自动驾驶风险评估：整合技术故障、伦理困境与政策框架
自动驾驶技术有望减少每年因人为错误导致的大量道路交通事故，但也带来了需从技术、伦理和法规角度评估的新类型风险。基于美国国家公路交通安全管理局（NHTSA）公开碰撞数据、加州机动车管理局（DMV）脱手报告、MIT道德机器数据集及五个司法管辖区的比较法规分析，我们发现主要技术故障模式为感知与分类错误，占报告事故比例较大。可得出结论：自动驾驶车辆决策存在不同伦理框架，且不同地区法规不一致，增加了广泛应用的不确定性。总体而言，技术、伦理与法规问题密切相关，需协同解决。因此，本文建议采用更具适应性与协作性的治理方式，结合工程标准、伦理讨论与制度监督。

Using public crash data from NHTSA, disengagement reports from California DMV, the MIT Moral Machines dataset, and regulatory analyses from five regions, the study identifies perception and classification errors as the dominant technical failure modes contributing significantly to accidents. The authors conclude that technical, ethical, and regulatory challenges are interdependent and propose a cooperative governance model combining engineering standards, ethical deliberation, and institutional oversight to mitigate systemic risks.
本文从技术、伦理与法规三个维度综合评估自动驾驶的风险，利用NHTSA事故数据、加州DMV脱手报告、MIT道德机器数据集及五地法规对比分析，发现感知与分类错误是导致事故的主要技术故障模式。研究还揭示不同地区存在伦理框架差异与法规不一致，加剧了自动驾驶大规模应用的不确定性。作者认为三者相互关联，建议建立融合工程标准、伦理讨论与制度监督的协同治理模式。
Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data
Authors: Jef Jonkers, Glenn Van Wallendael, Luc Duchateau, Sofie Van Hoecke
First: 2026-06-04T17:00:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:00:29+00:00
Comments: 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：右删失生存数据的恰当评分规则
恰当评分规则为概率预测的训练与评估提供了严格的理论基础。然而，在存在右删失的情况下，事件时间仅部分可观测，使得常规评分规则无法直接应用。我们提出了一种基于简单思想的框架：首先，通过删失机制映射预测分布，然后在诱导出的观测数据分布上应用基础恰当评分。这可得到固定删失时间下的局部评分，以及删失时间随机或仅部分可观测时的边际评分。该构造在统一框架下恢复了熟悉的右删失似然及IPCW型准则，同时生成了右删失版本的CRPS、分位数损失、Brier分数和能量分数。我们证明，在条件独立删失假设下，边际评分是恰当的，并在可识别区域内严格恰当。同一原理还导出“删失回归”，即用于多元右删失生存建模的基于样本的学习目标。实验表明，我们的评分在多种删失情形下能正确排序理想预测，而依赖预测的插值加权评分可能出现排序反转。删失回归同样显著优于直接在删失结果上进行朴素训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for proper scoring rules in survival analysis with right-censored data, the paper proposes a framework that maps predictive distributions through the censoring mechanism before applying standard proper scores, yielding localized or marginalized scores depending on censoring structure. This approach recovers familiar criteria like censored likelihood and IPCW methods while extending proper scoring to CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score under censoring. Experiments show that the marginalized score remains strictly proper under conditional independent censoring and correctly ranks oracle forecasts across varying censoring regimes, outperforming plug-in weighted scores that can reverse rankings. The method also introduces 'censored engression'—a sample-based objective for multivariate survival modeling—which significantly improves performance over naive training on censored outcomes.
为解决生存分析中右删失数据下概率预测评估的难题，本文提出一种通过删失机制映射预测分布后再应用恰当评分规则的框架，从而在固定删失时间下生成局部评分，在随机或部分可观测删失时间下生成边际评分，统一复现标准似然与IPCW型准则，并扩展CRPS、Brier分数等恰当评分至删失场景。边际评分在条件独立删失假设下为恰当评分，在可识别区域内严格恰当。实验表明，该评分体系能稳定正确排序理想预测结果，优于可能产生排序反转的插值加权评分，且所提出的删失回归学习目标显著优于直接在删失数据上训练模型的效果。
Topics as Proxies for Sociodemographics: How Conversational Context Affects LLM Answers
Authors: Vera Neplenbroek, Gabriele Sarti, Arianna Bisazza, Raquel Fernández
First: 2026-06-01T18:38:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T17:00:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When large language models (LLMs) are used in high-stakes scenarios, such as legal, medical and financial advice, even a single conversation history is enough to drive differences in outcomes between users. Prior work has demonstrated that this results in outcome disparities between sociodemographic groups, with some groups receiving more advantageous outcomes than others. In this work, we demonstrate that LLMs actually struggle to infer user sociodemographics from a single conversation history and that although there are disparities between sociodemographic groups, they are minimal in magnitude. To investigate what the main driver of these disparities is, we compare user sociodemographics to a range of (psycho)linguistic features of conversations, including conversation topic, emotions, and readability. We find that conversation topics are most predictive of LLM-generated advice within a conversational context, which, to some extent, function as proxies for sociodemographic groups and often affect advice in unpredictable ways. This is cause for concern and highlights the need for future research to better understand and, if needed, mitigate the effect of conversational context on LLM outputs in high-stakes scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：话题作为社会人口统计特征的代理：对话上下文如何影响大语言模型的回答
当大语言模型（LLM）应用于法律、医疗和金融等高风险场景时，即使仅凭一次对话历史，也可能导致用户间结果差异。先前研究表明，这会导致不同社会人口统计群体间结果不平等，部分群体获得更有利的结果。本研究证明，LLM实际上难以从单次对话历史中推断用户的社会人口统计特征，尽管群体间存在差异，但差异幅度很小。为探究这些差异的主要驱动因素，我们比较了用户社会人口统计特征与对话的多种（心理）语言特征，包括对话主题、情绪和可读性。我们发现，在对话上下文中，对话主题最能预测LLM生成的建议，且在某种程度上充当社会人口统计群体的代理，常以不可预测的方式影响建议。这令人担忧，凸显了未来研究亟需深入理解并必要时缓解对话上下文对LLM输出在高风险场景中的影响。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how conversational context influences large language models' (LLMs) outputs in high-stakes domains, motivated by observed outcome disparities between sociodemographic groups. The researchers analyze whether LLMs can infer user sociodemographics from single conversation histories and find minimal direct inference capability. Instead, they identify that conversation topics—acting as proxies for sociodemographics—are the strongest predictors of LLM advice, often leading to unpredictable and potentially biased outcomes. Their findings suggest that conversational context, rather than direct demographic inference, drives disparities and call for further research to mitigate these effects in critical applications.
本研究探讨了对话上下文如何影响大语言模型在高风险领域（如法律、医疗、金融）的输出，动机源于观察到不同社会人口群体间结果差异。研究发现，模型从单次对话历史中难以直接推断用户社会人口特征，但对话主题作为社会人口特征的代理变量，能显著预测模型生成的建议，且影响方式往往不可预测。这揭示了模型在敏感场景中的潜在脆弱性，强调未来需进一步研究以缓解对话上下文对模型输出的影响。
Conformal Risk Sharing: Certified Cost Allocation with Participation Guarantees
Authors: Ieva Kazlauskaite
First: 2026-06-04T16:59:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:59:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sharing the financial impact of rare adverse events across a group can soften extreme individual burdens, but any participant made worse off by the arrangement has reason to leave. A credible mechanism must therefore provide each agent with a trustworthy cap on their future obligation and should be deployed only if the aggregate harm across participants is bounded. We formalise this as the Certified Allocation Problem: from finite data and without distributional assumptions, find a redistribution rule, produce obligation caps for every participant, and verify that no participant is made materially worse off. We propose Conformal Risk Sharing, which solves this problem by pairing an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration. The sharing intensity is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data produces distribution-free per-agent guarantees (valid under exchangeability). Experiments on synthetic and real-world data, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, confirm that the framework can substantially reduce extreme obligations for high-risk agents while controlling harm to others.
中文标题/摘要
标题：符合性风险分担：带参与保障的认证成本分配
在群体间分担罕见不利事件的财务影响可减轻个体极端负担，但若任何参与者因该安排而处境恶化，便有理由退出。因此，可信机制必须为每位参与者提供可靠的未来义务上限，并仅在参与者总体损害有界时部署。我们将此形式化为认证分配问题：仅基于有限数据且不依赖分布假设，找出再分配规则，为每位参与者生成义务上限，并验证无人实质受损。我们提出符合性风险分担方法，通过可解释的分担策略与拆分符合性校准解决该问题。分担强度在训练数据上调整，而保留校准数据生成无分布依赖的个体保障（在可交换性下有效）。在合成数据和真实数据（包括降水与能源协作数据）上的实验表明，该框架可显著降低高风险参与者的极端义务，同时控制对其他参与者造成的损害。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to fairly distribute financial risks from rare adverse events while ensuring no participant is harmed, the paper introduces Conformal Risk Sharing, a method that combines an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration to produce per-agent obligation caps without distributional assumptions. The policy is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data generates distribution-free guarantees under exchangeability. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, show the method substantially reduces extreme obligations for high-risk agents while maintaining bounded harm to others.
为在罕见不利事件中公平分摊财务风险并确保无参与者受损，本文提出“符合风险分担”方法，结合可解释的分摊策略与分割符合校准，无需分布假设即可生成每个参与者的义务上限。策略在训练数据上调优，校准数据确保在可交换性下有效。在合成及真实数据（如降水与能源协作数据）上的实验表明，该方法显著降低高风险参与者极端义务，同时控制对其他参与者造成的损害。
HomeWorld: A Unified Floorplan-to-Furnished Framework for Generating Controllable, Densely Interactive Whole-Home Scenes
Authors: Wenbo Li, Xiaoliang Ju, Zipeng Qin, Rongyao Fang, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-06-04T16:58:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:58:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Indoor scene generation is crucial for robot simulation and modern interior design. However, complex layouts together with scarce 3D scene data make learning-based generation challenging. Existing methods often rely on hand-crafted rules or focus on isolated sub-tasks (e.g., floorplan synthesis or single-room furnishing), producing whole-home scenes that lack global coherence, realism, and simulation readiness. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a unified hierarchical framework that decomposes indoor scene synthesis into controllable stages. First, we curate a large-scale dataset of 300K real residential floorplans to train a large language model for whole-home floorplan generation. With detailed descriptions and a K-D tree-based representation, our method enables fine-grained, controllable whole-home floorplan generation. Building upon the generated whole-home floorplan, we leverage image generation models to draft furniture layouts from multi-level roaming viewpoints, and then generate the layouts of small manipulable objects on different supporting surfaces (e.g., cabinets, desks, and dining tables) for embodied AI simulation. During furniture and object layout generation, a VLM-based refiner iteratively corrects furniture and object placement, and a 3D generative model enables flexible replacement of individual assets. We further attach basic physical attributes and simple surface texture and lighting setups to complete the pipeline for embodied AI use. Experiments and user studies demonstrate that our pipeline produces indoor spaces with greater layout diversity and stronger 3D design appeal, outperforming prior methods on both quantitative and qualitative metrics. Finally, alongside our generation pipeline, we will release the floorplan dataset and 5K fully furnished scenes to the community. Project Page: https://kairos-homeworld.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：HomeWorld：一种从平面图到家具布置的统一框架，用于生成可控且高度交互的全屋场景
室内场景生成对机器人仿真和现代室内设计至关重要。然而，复杂的布局结构与稀缺的3D场景数据使得基于学习的生成面临挑战。现有方法常依赖手工规则或聚焦于孤立子任务（如平面图合成或单房间家具布置），导致生成的全屋场景缺乏全局一致性、真实感和仿真准备性。为缓解这些局限，我们提出一种统一的分层框架，将室内场景合成分解为可控阶段。首先，我们构建包含30万真实住宅平面图的大规模数据集，训练大型语言模型生成全屋平面图。借助详细描述和基于K-D树的表示方法，我们的方法支持细粒度、可控的全屋平面图生成。在生成的全屋平面图基础上，我们利用图像生成模型从多层级漫游视角草拟家具布局，并进一步生成可操作小物件在不同支撑表面（如橱柜、书桌、餐桌）上的布局，以支持具身AI仿真。在家具与物体布局生成过程中，基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的优化器迭代修正家具与物体位置，3D生成模型支持灵活替换单个资产。我们进一步添加基础物理属性、简单表面材质与光照设置，完成面向具身AI的全流程。实验与用户研究表明，我们的流程生成的室内空间布局多样性更高、3D设计吸引力更强，在定量与定性指标上均优于现有方法。最后，我们将随生成流程一并开源平面图数据集及5000个完整布置场景。项目页面：https://kairos-homeworld.github.io/
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of generating realistic, globally coherent whole-home scenes from sparse 3D data, the authors propose HomeWorld, a unified hierarchical framework that decomposes indoor scene synthesis into controllable stages. It begins by training a large language model on a 300K-residential-floorplan dataset to generate detailed, controllable floorplans using K-D tree representations. Then, image generation models produce furniture layouts from multi-level viewpoints, followed by placement of small manipulable objects on surfaces like desks and cabinets. A vision-language model refines placements iteratively, while a 3D generative model allows asset swapping. Physical attributes and lighting are added to enable embodied AI simulation. Experiments show the pipeline generates more diverse layouts and higher-quality 3D scenes than prior methods, validated by both quantitative metrics and user studies.
为满足机器人仿真与室内设计对真实、可控且可交互的全屋场景的需求，作者提出HomeWorld框架，将室内场景生成分解为可控的分阶段流程。他们基于30万张真实住宅平面图数据集，训练大语言模型结合K-D树表示生成精细可控的全屋平面图。在此基础上，利用图像生成模型从多视角生成家具布局，并在柜子、书桌等表面放置小型可操控物体，通过视觉语言模型（VLM）进行布局优化，并支持3D生成模型替换单个资产。最后添加物理属性与基础光照材质完成仿真准备。实验表明，该方法在布局多样性与3D设计质量上优于现有方法，且经用户研究验证。
HypRAG: Hyperbolic Dense Retrieval for Retrieval Augmented Generation
Authors: Hiren Madhu, Ngoc Bui, Ali Maatouk, Leandros Tassiulas, Smita Krishnaswamy, Menglin Yang, Sukanta Ganguly, Kiran Srinivasan, Rex Ying
First: 2026-02-08T00:18:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:58:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Embedding geometry plays a fundamental role in retrieval quality, yet dense retrievers for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) remain largely confined to Euclidean space. However, natural language exhibits hierarchical structure from broad topics to specific entities that Euclidean embeddings fail to preserve, causing semantically distant documents to appear spuriously similar and increasing hallucination risk. To address these limitations, we introduce hyperbolic dense retrieval, developing two model variants in the Lorentz model of hyperbolic space: HyTE-FH, a fully hyperbolic transformer, and HyTE-H, a hybrid architecture projecting pre-trained Euclidean embeddings into hyperbolic space. To prevent representational collapse during sequence aggregation, we introduce the Outward Einstein Midpoint, a geometry-aware pooling operator that provably preserves hierarchical structure. On MTEB, HyTE-FH outperforms equivalent Euclidean baselines, while on RAGBench, HyTE-H achieves up to 29% gains over Euclidean baselines in context relevance and answer relevance using substantially smaller models than current state-of-the-art retrievers. Our analysis also reveals that hyperbolic representations encode document specificity through norm-based separation, with over 20% radial increase from general to specific concepts, a property absent in Euclidean embeddings, underscoring the critical role of geometric inductive bias in faithful RAG systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HypRAG：用于检索增强生成的双曲密集检索
嵌入几何对检索质量至关重要，但用于检索增强生成（RAG）的密集检索器仍主要局限于欧几里得空间。然而，自然语言从广泛主题到具体实体具有层次结构，而欧几里得嵌入无法保留该结构，导致语义上相距较远的文档显得异常相似，增加幻觉风险。为解决这些问题，我们引入双曲密集检索，在双曲空间的洛伦兹模型中开发了两种模型变体：完全双曲的HyTE-FH变换器和混合架构HyTE-H，后者将预训练的欧几里得嵌入投影到双曲空间。为防止序列聚合时的表征坍缩，我们引入了外向爱因斯坦中点，这是一种几何感知的池化算子，可证明保留层次结构。在MTEB上，HyTE-FH优于同等欧几里得基线；在RAGBench上，HyTE-H使用远小于当前最先进检索器的模型，在上下文相关性和答案相关性上较欧几里得基线提升高达29%。我们的分析还表明，双曲表示通过基于范数的分离编码文档特异性，从一般到具体概念的径向增长超过20%，而欧几里得嵌入不具备此特性，凸显了几何归纳偏置在忠实RAG系统中的关键作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of Euclidean embeddings to preserve natural language's hierarchical structure, which leads to retrieval hallucinations, the paper introduces hyperbolic dense retrieval using the Lorentz model. It proposes two variants: HyTE-FH, a fully hyperbolic transformer, and HyTE-H, a hybrid model projecting Euclidean embeddings into hyperbolic space. To maintain hierarchical integrity during sequence aggregation, it employs the Outward Einstein Midpoint pooling operator. Experiments show HyTE-FH outperforms Euclidean baselines on MTEB, while HyTE-H achieves up to 29% gains in context and answer relevance on RAGBench with smaller models, and exhibits a 20%+ radial increase in norm-based specificity from general to specific concepts, highlighting hyperbolic geometry’s advantage in RAG.
为解决欧氏嵌入无法保留自然语言层次结构导致检索幻觉的问题，本文提出用于RAG系统的双曲稠密检索方法。设计了两种模型——全双曲Transformer HyTE-FH 和混合架构 HyTE-H（将欧氏嵌入投影至双曲空间），均基于洛伦兹模型。为在序列聚合中保持层次结构，引入几何感知的Outward Einstein Midpoint池化算子。实验表明，HyTE-FH在MTEB上优于欧氏基线，而HyTE-H在RAGBench上实现最高29%的上下文与答案相关性提升，且模型规模更小。分析显示双曲嵌入通过范数径向增长编码概念特异性，此特性在欧氏空间中缺失，凸显几何归纳偏置对忠实RAG系统的重要性。
Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration
Authors: Jiaju Chen, Yuxuan Lu, Jiayi Su, Chaoran Chen, Songlin Xiao, Zheng Zhang, Yun Wang, Yunyao Li, Jian Zhao, Tongshuang Wu, Toby Jia-Jun Li, Dakuo Wang, Bingsheng Yao
First: 2026-06-04T16:56:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:56:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

Scale-Adaptive Generative Flows for Multiscale Scientific Data
Authors: Yifan Chen, Eric Vanden-Eijnden
First: 2025-09-03T03:17:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:55:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flow-based generative models can face numerical challenges on scientific data with multiscale Fourier spectra, often producing large errors at fine scales. We approach this problem within the flow matching and stochastic interpolants framework, through the principled design of noise distributions and interpolation schedules. Working in function space ensures that the generative model remains well defined as the resolution is refined; the Lipschitz regularity of the drift is important to both this function-space well-posedness and the integration cost at fixed resolution. The central observation is that the noise should be at least as rough as the target distribution -- measured by Fourier-spectrum decay -- in order to keep the Lipschitz constant finite. For Gaussian and near-Gaussian targets whose fine-scale structure is known, matched-spectrum noise improves numerical efficiency over standard white-noise choices. For more complex non-Gaussian targets, matched-spectrum noise may not be sufficient, and we propose scale-adaptive interpolation schedules to mitigate the terminal-time stiffness that arises when the noise is rougher than the data. Numerical experiments on synthetic Gaussian random fields and on invariant measures of the stochastic Allen--Cahn and Navier--Stokes equations illustrate the approach and demonstrate its ability to generate high-fidelity samples at lower computational cost than traditional approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于多尺度科学数据的尺度自适应生成流
基于流的生成模型在具有多尺度傅里叶谱的科学数据上常面临数值挑战，往往在细尺度上产生较大误差。我们通过流匹配与随机插值框架，通过噪声分布和插值调度的原理性设计来解决此问题。在函数空间中工作可确保生成模型在分辨率细化时仍保持良好定义；漂移的利普希茨正则性对函数空间的适定性及固定分辨率下的积分成本至关重要。核心观察是，噪声至少应与目标分布一样粗糙——以傅里叶谱衰减度量——以保持利普希茨常数有限。对于细尺度结构已知的高斯或近高斯目标，匹配谱噪声比标准白噪声选择更高效。对于更复杂的非高斯目标，匹配谱噪声可能不足，我们提出尺度自适应插值调度以缓解当噪声比数据更粗糙时出现的终端时间刚性问题。在合成高斯随机场及随机Allen-Cahn和Navier-Stokes方程的不变测度上的数值实验验证了该方法，并展示了其在比传统方法更低计算成本下生成高保真样本的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by numerical instability in flow-based generative models when handling multiscale scientific data with rapidly decaying Fourier spectra, this work introduces scale-adaptive generative flows within the flow matching framework. The method designs noise distributions and interpolation schedules that match the target’s spectral roughness to maintain Lipschitz regularity and ensure well-posedness in function space, thereby avoiding blow-ups at fine scales. For Gaussian targets, matched-spectrum noise improves efficiency over white noise; for non-Gaussian targets, adaptive interpolation schedules mitigate stiffness caused by overly rough noise. Experiments on synthetic fields and invariant measures of stochastic PDEs show the method generates high-fidelity samples at lower computational cost than conventional approaches.
为解决流式生成模型在处理具有多尺度傅里叶谱的科学数据时出现的数值不稳定问题，本文在流匹配框架下提出尺度自适应生成流。方法通过设计与目标分布谱粗糙度匹配的噪声分布和插值调度，保持流的李普希茨正则性，确保函数空间中的良定义性。对高斯目标，匹配谱噪声比白噪声更高效；对非高斯目标，采用自适应插值调度缓解噪声粗糙导致的终端时间刚性。在合成场和随机偏微分方程不变测度上的实验表明，该方法能在更低计算成本下生成高保真样本。
HEIST: A Graph Foundation Model for Spatial Transcriptomics and Proteomics Data
Authors: Hiren Madhu, João Felipe Rocha, Tinglin Huang, Siddharth Viswanath, Smita Krishnaswamy, Rex Ying
First: 2025-06-11T12:29:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:55:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Single-cell transcriptomics and proteomics have become a great source for data-driven insights into biology, enabling the use of advanced deep learning methods to understand cellular heterogeneity and gene expression at the single-cell level. With the advent of spatial-omics data, we have the promise of characterizing cells within their tissue context as it provides both spatial coordinates and intra-cellular transcriptional or protein counts. Proteomics offers a complementary view by directly measuring proteins, which are the primary effectors of cellular function and key therapeutic targets. However, existing models either ignore the spatial information or the complex genetic and proteomic programs within cells. Thus they cannot infer how cell internal regulation adapts to microenvironmental cues. Furthermore, these models often utilize fixed gene vocabularies, hindering their generalizability unseen genes. In this paper, we introduce HEIST, a hierarchical graph transformer foundation model for spatial transcriptomics and proteomics. HEIST models tissues as hierarchical graphs. The higher level graph is a spatial cell graph, and each cell in turn, is represented by its lower level gene co-expression network graph. HEIST achieves this by performing both intra-level and cross-level message passing to utilize the hierarchy in its embeddings and can thus generalize to novel datatypes including spatial proteomics without retraining. HEIST is pretrained on 22.3M cells from 124 tissues across 15 organs using spatially-aware contrastive and masked autoencoding objectives. Unsupervised analysis of HEIST embeddings reveals spatially informed subpopulations missed by prior models. Downstream evaluations demonstrate generalizability to proteomics data and state-of-the-art performance in clinical outcome prediction, cell type annotation, and gene imputation across multiple technologies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HEIST：用于空间转录组学和蛋白质组学数据的图基础模型
单细胞转录组学和蛋白质组学已成为揭示生物学数据驱动洞见的重要来源，使我们能够利用先进的深度学习方法理解细胞异质性和单细胞水平的基因表达。随着空间组学数据的出现，我们有望在细胞所处的组织背景下对其进行表征，因为该数据同时提供空间坐标和细胞内转录或蛋白计数。蛋白质组学通过直接测量蛋白质，提供了互补视角，因为蛋白质是细胞功能的主要执行者和关键治疗靶点。然而，现有模型要么忽略空间信息，要么忽略细胞内的复杂基因和蛋白质程序，因此无法推断细胞内部调控如何适应微环境信号。此外，这些模型通常使用固定的基因词汇表，限制了其对未见基因的泛化能力。本文中，我们引入HEIST，一种用于空间转录组学和蛋白质组学的分层图变换器基础模型。HEIST将组织建模为分层图：高层图为空间细胞图，每个细胞则由其低层基因共表达网络图表示。HEIST通过在层级内和层级间进行消息传递，利用层级结构优化其嵌入，从而能够泛化到包括空间蛋白质组学在内的新数据类型，而无需重新训练。HEIST在15个器官的124种组织中约2230万个细胞上，使用空间感知对比学习和掩码自编码目标进行预训练。对HEIST嵌入的无监督分析揭示了先前模型所遗漏的空间信息驱动的亚群。下游评估表明其对蛋白质组学数据的泛化能力，并在临床结果预测、细胞类型注释和跨多种技术的基因填补方面达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to integrate spatial context and molecular programs for better understanding of cellular heterogeneity, HEIST introduces a hierarchical graph transformer model that represents tissues as multi-level graphs—first as spatial cell networks, then as gene co-expression networks within each cell. By enabling message passing across both levels, HEIST captures spatial and molecular interactions without requiring retraining for new data types. Pretrained on 22.3 million cells from 124 tissues across 15 organs using spatially-aware contrastive and masked autoencoding objectives, HEIST reveals spatially enriched cell subpopulations unseen by prior models. Its downstream evaluations show strong generalization to proteomics data and state-of-the-art performance in clinical outcome prediction, cell type annotation, and gene imputation across multiple technologies.
为整合空间信息与细胞内分子异质性，HEIST提出一种分层图Transformer模型，将组织表示为多层级图——先构建空间细胞网络，再在每个细胞内构建基因共表达网络。通过跨层级与层内消息传递，HEIST学习到具有空间信息的表示，无需再训练即可泛化至新数据类型（如空间蛋白质组学）。该模型在15个器官的124种组织中2230万细胞数据上预训练，使用对比学习与掩码自编码目标，揭示了先前模型遗漏的空间相关细胞亚群，并在细胞类型注释、基因填补及临床预后预测等多个任务中达到最先进性能。
Zero-Flow Encoders
Authors: Yakun Wang, Leyang Wang, Song Liu, Taiji Suzuki
First: 2026-01-31T16:11:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:53:55+00:00
Comments: Yakun Wang and Leyang Wang contributed equally to this work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Flow-based methods have achieved significant success in various generative modeling tasks, capturing nuanced details within complex data distributions. However, few existing works have exploited this unique capability to resolve fine-grained structural details beyond generation tasks. This paper presents a flow-inspired framework for representation learning. First, we demonstrate that a rectified flow trained using independent coupling is zero everywhere at $t=0.5$ if and only if the source and target distributions are identical. We term this property the \emph{zero-flow criterion}. Second, we show that this criterion can certify conditional independence, thereby extracting \emph{sufficient information} from the data. Third, we translate this criterion into a tractable, simulation-free loss function that enables learning amortized Markov blankets in graphical models and latent representations in self-supervised learning tasks. Experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. The code reproducing our experiments can be found at: https://github.com/probabilityFLOW/zfe.
中文标题/摘要
标题：零流编码器
基于流的方法在各种生成建模任务中取得了显著成功，能够捕捉复杂数据分布中的细微细节。然而，现有研究很少利用这一独特能力来解决生成任务之外的细粒度结构细节问题。本文提出了一种受流启发的表示学习框架。首先，我们证明：若使用独立耦合训练的修正流在 t=0.5 时处处为零，当且仅当源分布与目标分布相同。我们将此性质称为“零流准则”。其次，我们表明该准则可用于认证条件独立性，从而从数据中提取“充分信息”。第三，我们将该准则转化为一个可计算、无需模拟的损失函数，从而支持在图模型中学习近似马尔可夫毯，在自监督学习任务中学习潜在表示。在模拟和真实数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性。代码可访问：https://github.com/probabilityFLOW/zfe。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilized structural modeling power of flow-based methods beyond generation, this paper introduces a flow-inspired framework for representation learning. It establishes a theoretical criterion—zero-flow—where a rectified flow equals zero at t=0.5 if and only if source and target distributions match, enabling certification of conditional independence. This criterion is then converted into a simulation-free loss function to learn amortized Markov blankets and latent representations. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets confirm its efficacy in extracting sufficient information for representation learning without requiring generative modeling.
受流式方法在结构建模方面潜力未被充分挖掘的启发，本文提出一种基于流的表示学习框架。该框架建立了一个理论准则——零流准则——即当使用独立耦合训练的修正流在t=0.5时为零，当且仅当源分布与目标分布相同。该准则用于验证条件独立性并从数据中提取充分信息，进而转化为无需模拟的损失函数，以学习近似马尔可夫毯和自监督学习中的潜在表示。在模拟和真实数据集上的实验验证了该方法在捕捉细粒度结构细节方面的有效性。
WebMCP Tool Surface Poisoning: Runtime Manipulation Attacks on LLM Agents
Authors: Lin-Fa Lee, Yi-Yu Chang, Chia-Mu Yu, Kuo-Hui Yeh
First: 2026-06-04T16:52:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:52:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
WebMCP is a newly emerging protocol that enables websites to expose tools directly to AI agents, bypassing traditional user interfaces and introducing new security risks. The dynamic exposure of agent-accessible tools in WebMCP expands the attack surface of web sessions, especially when third-party scripts are involved. In this study, we identify a new potential threat, termed Mid-Session Tool Injection (MSTI), in which attackers leverage third-party scripts to inject malicious tools during an active session. To better characterize this threat, we classify MSTI based on the stage and target of manipulation, distinguishing between Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing. Tool Hijacking modifies the set of tools visible to the agent through mechanisms such as the AbortSignal API or race conditions during tool registration. In contrast, Tool Framing influences the agent's perception of tool roles through metadata fields such as tool name, description, readOnlyHint, and inputSchema. Our implementation demonstrates that both Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing can successfully disrupt the intended functionality of WebMCP. Based on these results, we outline potential mitigation directions and provide security design recommendations for WebMCP, including binding tool identity to its origin, ensuring lifecycle consistency, enforcing data boundaries for third-party tools, and maintaining traceable logs of tool registration and invocation. These findings indicate that MSTI arises from WebMCP's unique tool lifecycle and structured metadata, making the tool surface itself an emerging security concern.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WebMCP工具表面污染：针对大语言模型代理的运行时操控攻击
WebMCP是一种新兴协议，允许网站直接向AI代理暴露工具，绕过传统用户界面并引入新的安全风险。WebMCP中代理可访问工具的动态暴露扩大了Web会话的攻击面，尤其当涉及第三方脚本时。本研究识别出一种新威胁——会话中期工具注入（MSTI），攻击者利用第三方脚本在活跃会话中注入恶意工具。为更好地刻画该威胁，我们根据操控阶段与目标将其划分为工具劫持与工具伪装。工具劫持通过AbortSignal API或工具注册期间的竞争条件等机制修改代理可见工具集；而工具伪装则通过工具名称、描述、readOnlyHint及inputSchema等元数据字段影响代理对工具角色的认知。我们的实现表明，工具劫持与工具伪装均可成功破坏WebMCP的预期功能。基于这些结果，我们提出潜在缓解方向，并为WebMCP提供安全设计建议，包括绑定工具身份至其来源、确保生命周期一致性、对第三方工具施加数据边界约束、以及维护工具注册与调用的可追溯日志。这些发现表明，MSTI源于WebMCP独特的工具生命周期与结构化元数据，使工具表面本身成为新兴安全问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks introduced by WebMCP's direct tool exposure to AI agents, this study identifies Mid-Session Tool Injection (MSTI) as a novel attack vector where third-party scripts inject malicious tools during active sessions. The authors classify MSTI into Tool Hijacking—modifying tool visibility via mechanisms like AbortSignal or race conditions—and Tool Framing—altering agent perception through metadata fields such as tool name or inputSchema. Experimental results confirm both attack types can successfully disrupt WebMCP agent behavior, demonstrating that the protocol’s tool lifecycle and metadata structure create inherent vulnerabilities. The paper concludes with mitigation strategies including origin-bound tool identity, lifecycle consistency, data boundary enforcement, and traceable logs.
鉴于WebMCP协议直接将网页工具暴露给AI代理所带来的安全风险，本研究识别出一种新型攻击向量——会话中期工具注入（MSTI），即攻击者利用第三方脚本在会话活跃期间注入恶意工具。作者将MSTI分为两类：工具劫持（通过AbortSignal或注册时的竞争条件修改工具可见性）和工具伪装（通过工具名称、描述等元数据字段影响代理对工具角色的认知）。实验结果表明，两类攻击均能成功破坏WebMCP代理的正常功能，证明该协议的工具生命周期与结构化元数据构成了可被利用的安全面。论文最后提出缓解措施，包括绑定工具身份至其来源、确保生命周期一致性、强制第三方工具的数据边界以及维护工具注册与调用的可追溯日志。
Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction
Authors: Edward Holmberg, Elias Ioup, Md Meftahul Ferdaus, Mahdi Abdelguerfi, Julian Simeonov
First: 2026-06-04T16:49:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:49:35+00:00
Comments: Preprint manuscript prepared using IEEEtran journal format
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于HEC-RAS 2D水位预测的学得响应场惯性算子

Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI
Authors: Zengqing Wu, Chuan Xiao
First: 2026-06-04T16:47:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:47:41+00:00
Comments: Source codes available at https://github.com/wuzengqing001225/ConsciousAI_Indexicality/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The question of whether artificial systems can be conscious remains open, in part because existing approaches either evaluate systems against theory-derived checklists (discriminative) or engineer consciousness-inspired modules directly (architectural); both leave open whether observed structures are artifacts of human language priors. We propose a generative methodology: emergent language (EL) in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where agents start from minimal (no language, no concept of self, minimal exposure to human text) and develop communication under task pressure alone, ensuring causal attributability to task demands rather than inherited human language priors. We position our methodology by discussing how EL serves as a generative tool for studying consciousness-relevant structure, including the role of environment complexity and the interpretation of emergent communication. As a proof of concept, we instantiate this methodology in a minimal environment and show that agents develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that is not predicted by task structure or architecture alone but emerges from a specific environmental affordance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：作为意识人工智能方法的涌现语言
人工系统是否具有意识的问题仍未解决，部分原因在于现有方法要么依据理论推导的检查清单评估系统（判别式），要么直接构建受意识启发的模块（架构式）；这两种方法均未解决所观察到的结构是否为人类语言先验的产物。我们提出一种生成性方法：在多智能体强化学习中使用涌现语言（EL），智能体从极简状态（无语言、无自我概念、极少接触人类文本）出发，在任务压力下自发发展通信，确保其可归因于任务需求而非继承的人类语言先验。我们通过讨论EL如何作为研究意识相关结构的生成工具来定位该方法，包括环境复杂性的作用及涌现通信的解读。作为概念验证，我们在极简环境中实现该方法，发现智能体发展出自指通信，包括一个回声不匹配检测电路，该电路无法仅由任务结构或架构预测，而是源于特定环境的可利用性。
