Experimental results in a minimal environment demonstrate that agents spontaneously develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that emerges from environmental affordances and cannot be explained by task structure or architecture alone.
为了解决人工意识定义模糊及现有判别式或架构式方法的局限性，本研究提出在多智能体强化学习中使用“涌现语言”（EL）方法，即智能体从无语言、无自我概念的初始状态出发，在任务压力下自发发展沟通能力，避免人类语言先验的干扰。在最小化环境中实验表明，智能体自发形成自指性沟通，包括一种“回声不匹配检测电路”，该机制并非由任务结构或系统架构决定，而是源于特定环境的可操作性。
EasyLens: A Training-Free Plug-and-Play Subtle-Lesion Representation Amplifier for Medical Vision-Language Models
Authors: Qiwei Zeng, Hao Wang, Jinghao Lin, Shuchang Ye, Yuezhe Yang, Yige Peng, Haoyuan Che, Jinman Kim, Lei Bi
First: 2026-06-04T16:47:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:47:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical vision-language models (VLMs) have shown increasing potential for clinical image interpretation, including lesion detection and report generation. However, their practical utility remains limited by insufficient sensitivity to subtle lesions, whose visual evidence is often sparse, low-contrast, and embedded within complex anatomical context. As local visual tokens are aggregated, these weak lesion cues can become underrepresented in global image representations, making them difficult for medical VLMs to recognize. Existing efforts to improve lesion sensitivity mainly rely on medical-domain vision-encoder pre-training, clinical-term-guided alignment, or trainable pathological representation enhancement. Although effective, these approaches usually require additional training or model-specific adaptation and may overfit to particular disease morphologies, limiting their applicability to frozen medical VLMs. To address these limitations, we propose EasyLens, a training-free plug-and-play subtle-lesion representation amplifier for medical VLMs. EasyLens first constructs EasyBank, a pathology-anatomy prototype space that provides lesion-related prototypes and anatomy-aware normal references for comparing suspicious patches against both pathological and normal anatomical patterns. To avoid blindly amplifying normal tissues, EasyTag selects lesion-relevant patches through counterfactual prototype reasoning. To counteract the dilution of subtle lesion cues in global image representations, EasyAmplifier strengthens the selected lesion-relevant patch representations through morphology-guided residual enhancement, thereby increasing their contribution to the global image embedding. Experiments on multiple medical image datasets and frozen medical VLM backbones show that EasyLens improves subtle-lesion detection and outperforms existing encoder-enhancement baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EasyLens：一种无需训练的即插即用型细微病变表示增强器，用于医学视觉-语言模型
医学视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在临床图像解读（如病灶检测和报告生成）方面展现出日益增长的潜力。然而，其实际应用受限于对细微病变的敏感度不足，因为这些病变的视觉证据往往稀疏、低对比度，并嵌入复杂解剖背景中。当局部视觉标记被聚合时，这些微弱的病变线索可能在全局图像表示中被弱化，导致医学VLMs难以识别。现有提升病灶敏感度的方法主要依赖医学领域视觉编码器预训练、临床术语引导对齐或可训练的病理表示增强。尽管有效，这些方法通常需要额外训练或模型特定适配，且可能过拟合特定疾病形态，限制其在冻结的医学VLMs上的适用性。为解决上述局限，我们提出EasyLens，一种无需训练的即插即用型细微病变表示增强器。EasyLens首先构建EasyBank，一个病理-解剖原型空间，提供与病变相关的原型及解剖感知的正常参考，用于将可疑区域与病理和正常解剖模式进行比较。为避免盲目放大正常组织，EasyTag通过反事实原型推理选择与病变相关的区域。为对抗全局图像表示中细微病变线索的稀释，EasyAmplifier通过形态引导的残差增强强化所选病变相关区域的表示，从而提升其对全局图像嵌入的贡献。在多个医学图像数据集和冻结的医学VLMs主干网络上的实验表明，EasyLens提升了细微病变检测性能，并优于现有编码器增强基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited sensitivity of medical vision-language models to subtle lesions due to sparse and low-contrast visual cues, EasyLens introduces a training-free, plug-and-play method to amplify lesion representations without modifying the model. It first builds EasyBank, a pathology-anatomy prototype space for comparing suspicious patches against pathological and normal anatomical patterns. Then, EasyTag selects lesion-relevant patches via counterfactual prototype reasoning to avoid amplifying normal tissues. Finally, EasyAmplifier enhances selected patch representations using morphology-guided residual enhancement to boost their influence in global image embeddings. Experiments on multiple medical datasets and frozen VLM backbones demonstrate improved subtle-lesion detection performance, outperforming existing encoder-enhancement baselines.
为了解决医学视觉-语言模型对细微病灶敏感度不足的问题，EasyLens提出一种无需训练、即插即用的方法，通过增强病灶表征而不修改模型。其首先构建EasyBank，即病理-解剖原型空间，用于将可疑区域与病理和正常解剖模式进行对比。接着，EasyTag通过反事实原型推理选择与病灶相关的区域，避免误增强正常组织。最后，EasyAmplifier利用形态引导的残差增强方法强化选定区域的表征，提升其在全局图像嵌入中的贡献。在多个医学数据集和冻结的医学VLM主干网络上的实验表明，EasyLens显著提升了细微病灶检测性能，并优于现有编码器增强基线方法。
Variational Entropic Optimal Transport
Authors: Roman Dyachenko, Nikita Gushchin, Kirill Sokolov, Petr Mokrov, Evgeny Burnaev, Alexander Korotin
First: 2026-02-02T15:48:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:45:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Entropic optimal transport (EOT) in continuous spaces with quadratic cost is a classical tool for solving the domain translation problem. In practice, recent approaches optimize a weak dual EOT objective depending on a single potential, but doing so is computationally not efficient due to the intractable log-partition term. Existing methods typically resolve this obstacle in one of two ways: by significantly restricting the transport family to obtain closed-form normalization (via Gaussian-mixture parameterizations), or by using general neural parameterizations that require simulation-based training procedures. We propose Variational Entropic Optimal Transport (VarEOT), based on an exact variational reformulation of the log-partition $\log \mathbb{E}[\exp(\cdot)]$ as a tractable minimization over an auxiliary log-normalizer. This yields a differentiable learning objective optimized with stochastic gradients and avoids the necessity of MCMC simulations during the training. We provide theoretical guarantees, including finite-sample generalization bounds and approximation results under universal function approximation. Experiments on synthetic data and unpaired image-to-image translation demonstrate competitive or improved translation quality, while comparisons within the solvers that use the same weak dual EOT objective support the benefit of the proposed optimization principle. The code for our solver can be found at https://github.com/DrEternity/VarEOT .
中文标题/摘要
标题：变分熵最优传输
连续空间中具有二次成本的熵最优传输（EOT）是解决域迁移问题的经典工具。在实践中，近期方法优化依赖于单个势函数的弱对偶EOT目标，但由于不可计算的对数分区项，该方法计算效率低下。现有方法通常通过两种方式解决此问题：一是显著限制传输族以获得闭式归一化（如高斯混合参数化）；二是使用通用神经参数化，需依赖仿真训练。我们提出变分熵最优传输（VarEOT），基于对数分区项 $\log \mathbb{E}[\exp(\cdot)]$ 的精确变分重写，将其转化为关于辅助对数归一化器的可计算最小化问题。这产生了一个可微分的学习目标，通过随机梯度优化，且无需训练期间进行MCMC仿真。我们提供了理论保证，包括有限样本泛化界和通用函数逼近下的近似结果。在合成数据和无配对图像到图像翻译实验中，我们的方法展现出竞争力或更优的翻译质量；与使用相同弱对偶EOT目标的求解器比较，支持所提优化原则的优势。代码见 https://github.com/DrEternity/VarEOT 。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of optimizing weak dual entropic optimal transport (EOT) due to intractable log-partition terms, this paper introduces Variational Entropic Optimal Transport (VarEOT), which reformulates the log-partition as a tractable minimization over an auxiliary log-normalizer, enabling differentiable learning via stochastic gradients without MCMC. The method leverages universal function approximation and provides theoretical guarantees including finite-sample generalization bounds. Experiments on synthetic data and unpaired image translation show VarEOT achieves competitive or superior translation quality compared to existing methods using the same weak dual EOT objective.
为解决弱对偶EOT目标中对数分区项计算不高效的问题，本文提出变分熵最优传输（VarEOT），通过将对数分区项转化为可微分的辅助对数归一化器最小化问题，避免了MCMC采样，同时无需限制传输族为高斯混合或依赖模拟训练。实验在合成数据和无配对图像翻译任务上验证了VarEOT在性能上可与现有方法媲美甚至更优，理论方面提供了有限样本泛化界和通用逼近性保证。
Rethinking Infrastructure Inspection as Image Difference Classification: A Traffic Sign Case Study
Authors: Ching Yau Fergus Mok, Lavindra de Silva, Varun Kumar Reja, Ioannis Brilakis
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-06-04T16:43:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:43:30+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Computer Vision for the Built World Workshop (CV4AEC @ CVPR)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Digital twins (DTs) allow the digitalization of road infrastructure inspection, though this is hindered by limited annotated data. This work exploits the relational nature of continuous asset condition monitoring to reformulate image-based defect detection as image difference classification (IDC) to reduce data reliance. This was evaluated in a case study on low-resource traffic sign inspection with different IDC classifiers using a newly-curated, high quality dataset. Results indicate that the instruction-based classifier outperforms encoder-based ones and gains from comparison with reference images. This shows that IDC can be an effective task modeling for tackling data constraints in infrastructure inspection and DT asset condition updating.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考基础设施检测作为图像差异分类：交通标志案例研究
数字孪生（DT）可实现道路基础设施检测的数字化，但受限于标注数据不足。本研究利用连续资产状态监测的关联特性，将基于图像的缺陷检测重新建模为图像差异分类（IDC），以减少对数据的依赖。该方法在低资源交通标志检测案例中通过新编纂的高质量数据集，使用不同IDC分类器进行评估。结果表明，基于指令的分类器优于编码器类分类器，并受益于与参考图像的对比。这表明IDC可作为应对基础设施检测和DT资产状态更新中数据约束的有效任务建模方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of annotated data in road infrastructure inspection, this study redefines defect detection as image difference classification to reduce reliance on labeled examples. Using a newly curated dataset for traffic sign inspection, it evaluates multiple IDC classifiers and finds that instruction-based models outperform encoder-based ones, especially when compared against reference images. This demonstrates that IDC is a viable and effective approach for handling data constraints in digital twin asset monitoring.
为应对基础设施检测中标注数据稀缺的问题，该研究将缺陷检测重新建模为图像差异分类任务，以降低对标注数据的依赖。在新构建的交通标志检测数据集上，实验表明基于指令的分类器优于编码器类模型，且在与参考图像对比时表现更优，证明图像差异分类在数据受限场景下可有效支持数字孪生系统中的资产状态更新。
Query-efficient model evaluation using cached responses
Authors: Hayden Helm, Ben Johnson, Carey Priebe
First: 2026-05-08T01:24:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:41:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating a new model on an existing benchmark is often necessary to understand its behavior before deployment. For modern evaluation frameworks, generating and evaluating a response for all queries can be prohibitively expensive. In practice, responses from previously-evaluated models are often cached -- creating a potential opportunity to use this additional information to decrease the number of queries required to accurately evaluate a new model. In this paper, we introduce an approach for predicting benchmark performance that leverages cached model responses based on the Data Kernel Perspective Space (DKPS), a method for quantifying the relationship between models in the black-box setting. Theoretically, we show that DKPS-based methods are query-efficient under certain conditions. Empirically, we demonstrate that DKPS-based methods achieve the same mean absolute error as baselines with a substantially decreased query budget. We conclude by proposing an offline method for selecting a set of queries that maximizes the goodness-of-fit on reference models, improving prediction accuracy over random query selection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用缓存响应进行高效模型评估
在部署前，通常需要在现有基准上评估新模型以了解其行为。对于现代评估框架，为所有查询生成并评估响应可能成本过高。实践中，先前评估模型的响应常被缓存，这为利用该额外信息减少评估新模型所需查询数量提供了潜在机会。本文提出一种基于数据核视角空间（DKPS）的基准性能预测方法，DKPS是一种用于黑盒环境下量化模型间关系的方法。理论上，我们证明在特定条件下DKPS方法具有查询效率。实证表明，DKPS方法在保持与基线相同平均绝对误差的同时，显著降低了查询预算。最后，我们提出一种离线方法，通过选择最大化参考模型拟合度的查询集，提升预测准确性，优于随机查询选择。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of evaluating new models on existing benchmarks, this paper proposes a query-efficient method that leverages cached responses from previously evaluated models to reduce the number of queries needed. It employs the Data Kernel Perspective Space (DKPS), a black-box model comparison technique, to predict benchmark performance by quantifying model relationships. Theoretically, DKPS methods are shown to be query-efficient under specific conditions, and empirically, they match baseline accuracy in mean absolute error while using significantly fewer queries. The paper further introduces an offline strategy to select queries that maximize goodness-of-fit with reference models, enhancing prediction accuracy compared to random selection.
为降低新模型在现有基准上的评估成本，本文提出一种利用缓存响应的高效查询评估方法。该方法基于数据核视角空间（DKPS），一种黑盒模型比较技术，以预测模型性能并减少查询次数。实验表明，DKPS方法在均方绝对误差上与基线相当，但查询量大幅减少，且通过离线选择最优查询集，其预测精度优于随机采样。
A Horizon-Aware Decision-Support Framework for Demand Forecasting Model Selection in Resilient Production Planning
Authors: Adolfo González, Víctor Parada
First: 2026-02-15T00:24:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:41:13+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 12 figures and Appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Demand forecasting is a critical input for resilient production planning, inventory replenishment, procurement, and capacity decisions under demand intermittency, high variability, and operational uncertainty. In these contexts, selecting forecasting models solely on the basis of fixed test-horizon performance may lead to decisions misaligned with the future planning horizons in which forecasts are used. This study proposes the Metric Degradation by Forecast Horizon (MDFH) procedure as a horizon-aware decision-support framework for selecting demand forecasting models. MDFH projects eligible out-of-sample error metrics, specifically MAE, RMSE, and RMSSE, from an observed test horizon toward future operational horizons under explicit structural-stability conditions. Based on this layer, RMSSEh is derived as a parsimonious horizon-aware selector, while the Adaptive Hybrid Selector for Intermittency and Variability (AHSIV) is proposed as an adaptive extension for structurally heterogeneous demand series. ERA, a multivariate ranking-aggregation selector, is included as a comparator. The empirical evaluation uses the Walmart, M3, M4, and M5 datasets, three training-testing partitions, 22 forecasting models, and 12-step future horizons. Results show that RMSSEh and AHSIV provide more consistent downstream volumetric alignment than ERA when assessed through ex post Global Relative Accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向韧性生产计划需求预测模型选择的时程感知决策支持框架
需求预测是韧性生产计划、库存补货、采购及产能决策的关键输入，尤其在需求间歇性、高变异性及运营不确定性背景下。在此类情境中，仅依据固定测试时程性能选择预测模型可能导致决策与实际应用的未来规划时程不匹配。本研究提出“预测时程导致的指标退化（MDFH）”程序，作为时程感知的决策支持框架，用于选择需求预测模型。MDFH在明确结构稳定性条件下，将观测到的测试时程外样本误差指标（MAE、RMSE、RMSSE）投影至未来运营时程。在此基础上，推导出简明的时程感知选择器RMSSEh，并提出适应性混合选择器AHSIV，用于结构异质性需求序列。ERA（多变量排名聚合选择器）作为比较基准。实证评估使用Walmart、M3、M4、M5数据集，三种训练-测试划分，22个预测模型及12步未来时程。结果表明，RMSSEh与AHSIV在通过事后全局相对准确性评估时，比ERA提供更一致的下游体积对齐。
Summary / 总结
To address the mismatch between fixed-horizon model selection and actual operational planning horizons, this study introduces the MDFH framework, which projects error metrics (MAE, RMSE, RMSSE) from observed test horizons to future operational horizons under structural stability. It derives RMSSEh as a horizon-aware selector and proposes AHSIV for heterogeneous demand series. Evaluated across four datasets, 22 models, and 12-step horizons, RMSSEh and AHSIV outperformed ERA in maintaining downstream volumetric alignment as measured by ex post Global Relative Accuracy.
为解决固定测试周期模型评估与实际运营规划周期不匹配的问题，该研究提出“预测水平感知的指标退化（MDFH）”框架，基于结构稳定性假设将MAE、RMSE、RMSSE等离线误差指标向前投影至未来预测周期。由此推导出简明的RMSSEh选择器，并提出适用于需求结构异质性的自适应混合选择器AHSIV，同时与多变量排序聚合器ERA进行对比。在Walmart、M3、M4、M5数据集上，使用22个模型和12步预测周期进行实证评估，结果显示RMSSEh和AHSIV在事后全局相对准确性指标下，比ERA更稳定地保持下游体积对齐效果。
LatentWave: JEPA Pretraining for Wireless Foundation Models
Authors: Ahmed Mohamed, Ahmed Aboulfotouh, Hatem Abou-Zeid
First: 2026-06-04T16:39:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:39:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Wireless foundation models have emerged as a promising alternative to building separate models for each wireless task. However, existing approaches rely on masked input reconstruction, which can bias representations toward low-level signal details. In this paper, we propose LatentWave, a wireless foundation model pretrained using a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) on diverse wireless spectrograms and channel state information (CSI). By predicting masked regions in latent space, LatentWave learns representations that are more transferable out of the box across diverse downstream tasks. The proposed architecture employs per-channel patch embeddings with stochastic channel sampling during pretraining, allowing it to process variable antenna counts and improving usability across heterogeneous wireless configurations. We evaluate LatentWave on four downstream tasks: RF signal classification, 5G NR positioning, beam prediction, and LoS/NLoS classification, comparing against a masked-modeling baseline (WavesFM) pretrained on the same data. Additionally, we show that the masking geometry introduces a task-dependent inductive bias: frequency masking strongly favors channel-related tasks such as positioning and beam prediction, while region masking better preserves discriminability for signal classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LatentWave：基于联合嵌入预测架构的无线基础模型预训练
无线基础模型已成为构建针对每个无线任务独立模型的有前景替代方案。然而，现有方法依赖于掩码输入重建，这可能导致表征偏向低级信号细节。本文提出LatentWave，一种在多样化的无线频谱图和信道状态信息（CSI）上使用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）进行预训练的无线基础模型。通过在潜在空间中预测掩码区域，LatentWave学习到更易跨多种下游任务迁移的表征。所提架构采用每通道补丁嵌入，并在预训练期间进行随机信道采样，使其能够处理不同天线数量，提升在异构无线配置中的实用性。我们在四个下游任务上评估LatentWave：射频信号分类、5G NR定位、波束预测和视距/非视距分类，并与在相同数据上预训练的掩码建模基线（WavesFM）进行比较。此外，我们表明掩码几何结构引入了任务相关的归纳偏置：频率掩码对与信道相关的任务（如定位和波束预测）有显著优势，而区域掩码则更有利于信号分类中的判别性保留。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalizable wireless foundation models that avoid low-level signal bias from masked reconstruction, LatentWave introduces a JEPA-based pretraining framework using wireless spectrograms and CSI. It predicts masked latent regions using per-channel patch embeddings and stochastic channel sampling, enabling robust handling of variable antenna setups. Evaluated on four downstream tasks—RF classification, 5G positioning, beam prediction, and LoS/NLoS classification—LatentWave outperforms a masked-model baseline (WavesFM), with masking geometry revealing task-specific inductive bias: frequency masking aids channel tasks, while region masking benefits signal classification.
为解决现有无线模型因掩码重建导致的低层信号偏置问题，LatentWave提出基于JEPA架构的预训练方法，在无线频谱图和信道状态信息上预测潜空间掩码区域。其架构采用每通道嵌入与随机通道采样，支持不同天线配置，提升硬件异构兼容性。在RF分类、5G NR定位、波束预测和LoS/NLoS分类四项下游任务上，LatentWave优于掩码建模基线（WavesFM），且掩码几何结构揭示任务依赖的归纳偏置：频域掩码利于信道相关任务，区域掩码更利于信号分类的判别性保留。
What Makes Two Language Models Think Alike?
Authors: Louis Jalouzot, Christophe Pallier, Emmanuel Chemla, Yair Lakretz
First: 2024-06-18T13:45:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:38:40+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Do architectural and training differences influence the way models represent and process language? Traditional similarity metrics tell us whether two models share a similar representational geometry, but they cannot explain why. Here, we propose a new, simple, approach to address this question. This approach maps neural activity in each model layer onto a set of interpretable linguistic features and quantifies how much each of them drives similarities and differences between models. We use this approach to compare 43 language models across 10 families, including decoder Transformers, State-Space Models, and Recurrent Neural Networks. We find that model-level similarity is driven most strongly by release date, a proxy for general LLM development, and model family, suggesting that linguistic signatures are not primarily shaped by scale or architecture class. Overall, our approach provides a way to link theoretically-motivated symbolic descriptions to neural representations and can readily be extended to other domains such as speech and vision, and to other neural systems such as biological brains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：什么使两个语言模型思考方式相似？
架构和训练差异是否影响模型对语言的表示与处理方式？传统相似性度量仅能告诉我们两个模型是否共享相似的表示几何结构，却无法解释原因。本文提出一种新颖且简单的方法来解决这一问题：将每个模型层的神经活动映射到一组可解释的语言特征，并量化每个特征对模型间相似性与差异性的贡献。我们用该方法比较了43个来自10个家族的语言模型，包括解码器Transformer、状态空间模型和循环神经网络。研究发现，模型层面的相似性主要由发布日期（作为通用大语言模型发展的代理指标）和模型家族驱动，表明语言特征并非主要由规模或架构类别塑造。总体而言，我们的方法提供了一种将理论驱动的符号描述与神经表征相联系的途径，并可轻松扩展至语音、视觉等其他领域，以及生物大脑等其他神经系统。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether architectural and training differences influence how language models represent and process language, going beyond traditional similarity metrics that only reveal geometric resemblance without explaining its cause. The authors introduce a method that maps neural activations across model layers to interpretable linguistic features, then measures how each feature contributes to model similarity or divergence. Applied to 43 models across 10 families—including Transformers, State-Space Models, and RNNs—their analysis finds that model similarity is most strongly correlated with release date and model family, rather than model size or architecture type, suggesting linguistic representations are more influenced by developmental context than structural design. The approach offers a bridge between symbolic linguistic theory and neural computation, with potential for extension to other modalities and biological systems.
该研究探讨了模型架构和训练差异是否影响语言模型对语言的表征与处理方式，突破传统相似性度量仅能揭示几何相似性而无法解释原因的局限。作者提出一种新方法，将模型各层神经激活映射到可解释的语言特征，并量化每个特征对模型间相似性或差异性的贡献。该方法应用于43个来自10个家族（包括Transformer、状态空间模型和RNN）的语言模型，结果发现模型相似性主要由发布日期（作为大模型发展的一般代理）和模型家族决定，而非模型规模或架构类别。这表明语言表征更多受发展背景和家族谱系影响，而非规模或结构类型，且该方法可为符号语言理论与神经表征建立桥梁，并可扩展至语音、视觉等其他模态及生物神经系统。
Detecting Perspective Shifts in Multi-agent Systems
Authors: Eric Bridgeford, Hayden Helm
First: 2025-12-04T17:24:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:38:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative models augmented with external tools and update mechanisms (or \textit{agents}) have demonstrated capabilities beyond intelligent prompting of base models. As agent use proliferates, dynamic multi-agent systems have naturally emerged. Recent work has investigated the theoretical and empirical properties of low-dimensional representations of agents based on query responses at a single time point. This paper introduces the Temporal Data Kernel Perspective Space (TDKPS), which jointly embeds agents across time, and proposes several novel hypothesis tests for detecting behavioral change at the agent- and group-level in black-box multi-agent systems. We characterize the empirical properties of our proposed tests, including their sensitivity to key hyperparameters, in simulations motivated by a multi-agent system of evolving digital personas. Finally, we demonstrate via natural experiment that our proposed tests detect changes that correlate sensitively, specifically, and significantly with a real exogenous event. As far as we are aware, TDKPS is the first principled framework for monitoring behavioral dynamics in black-box multi-agent systems -- a critical capability as generative agent deployment continues to scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体系统中的视角转变检测
结合外部工具和更新机制（或称\textit{智能体}）的生成模型已展现出超越基础模型智能提示的能力。随着智能体应用的普及，动态多智能体系统自然涌现。近期研究已探讨基于单一时间点查询响应的低维智能体表示的理论与实证特性。本文提出时序数据核视角空间（TDKPS），该方法跨时间联合嵌入智能体，并提出若干新颖的假设检验方法，用于在黑盒多智能体系统中检测个体及群体层面的行为变化。我们通过模拟多智能体系统中数字人格演化的情景，刻画所提检验方法的实证特性，包括对关键超参数的敏感性。最后，我们通过自然实验表明，所提检验方法能检测到与真实外生事件高度敏感、具体且显著相关的行为变化。据我们所知，TDKPS是首个用于监控黑盒多智能体系统行为动态的原理性框架——随着生成智能体部署规模持续扩大，这一能力至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to monitor behavioral shifts in evolving multi-agent systems, this paper introduces TDKPS, a framework that jointly embeds agents across time to capture dynamic behavior. It proposes novel hypothesis tests to detect changes at both agent and group levels in black-box systems, characterized through simulations based on evolving digital personas. Experimental results show the tests are sensitive, specific, and statistically significant in detecting behavioral changes triggered by real-world exogenous events, establishing TDKPS as the first principled method for monitoring such dynamics as agent deployment scales.
为监测多智能体系统中的行为变化，本文提出时序数据核视角空间（TDKPS）框架，通过跨时间联合嵌入智能体以捕捉动态行为，并设计了检测个体与群体层面变化的新假设检验方法。仿真实验基于数字人格演化场景，验证了方法对关键超参数的敏感性；实际实验表明，该方法能显著且特异性地检测与真实外部事件相关的行为变化，是首个面向黑盒多智能体系统行为动态监控的系统性框架。
Ensuring Interaction Safety in Multitask Exoskeleton Control: A Simulation-Trained Variable Impedance Framework
Authors: Muyuan Ma, Houcheng Li, Haotian Zhai, Lijun Han, Xinpan Meng, Xiuze Xia, Long Cheng
First: 2026-06-04T16:37:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:37:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Wearable exoskeletons can augment human phys ical capabilities during complex activities. However, ensuring adaptation across diverse tasks while guaranteeing interaction safety remains a critical challenge. To address this, a simulation trained variable impedance control approach with stability guarantees is proposed. First, a simulation-based human exoskeleton motion data generation pipeline is established, utilizing Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) to synthesize human muscle activations while the exoskeleton provides direct compensation for human biological joint torques. Subsequently, the generated dataset is used to train a dual modality policy that fuses semantic instructions with proprioceptive history, enabling the prediction of reference trajectories and variable impedance gains for nine different motion tasks. To guarantee safety, the network outputs are constrained by a stability criterion derived from Lyapunov stability theory, which bounds stiffness variations to ensure the asymptotic stability of the coupled system. Experimental results indicate that the proposed framework reduces metabolic cost in real-world scenarios com pared with standard baseline methods. These findings suggest the feasibility of the proposed framework for safe, multitask exoskeleton control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多任务外骨骼控制中的交互安全性保障：一种基于仿真训练的可变阻抗框架
可穿戴外骨骼可在复杂活动中增强人体物理能力。然而，在保证交互安全的同时实现跨多样任务的自适应仍是一个关键挑战。为此，提出了一种具有稳定性保障的仿真训练可变阻抗控制方法。首先，建立基于仿真的外骨骼与人体运动数据生成管道，利用近端策略优化（PPO）合成人体肌肉激活，同时外骨骼直接补偿人体生物关节力矩。随后，利用生成的数据集训练一种双模态策略，融合语义指令与本体感觉历史，实现对九种不同运动任务的参考轨迹和可变阻抗增益预测。为确保安全，网络输出受Lyapunov稳定性理论导出的稳定性准则约束，将刚度变化限制在合理范围内，以保证耦合系统的渐近稳定性。实验结果表明，该框架在真实场景中相比标准基线方法降低了代谢成本。这些发现表明该框架在安全、多任务外骨骼控制中具有可行性。
Summary / 总结
To enable safe and adaptable control of exoskeletons across multiple tasks, this study proposes a simulation-trained variable impedance framework that combines reinforcement learning with stability guarantees. Using Proximal Policy Optimization, the method generates synthetic human motion data by simulating muscle activations and exoskeleton torque compensation, which is then used to train a dual-modality policy that predicts task-specific trajectories and impedance gains. Safety is ensured by constraining network outputs via a Lyapunov-based stability criterion that limits stiffness variations to maintain system asymptotic stability. Experimental results show the framework reduces metabolic cost compared to baseline methods, demonstrating its practical feasibility for multitask exoskeleton control.
为实现多任务场景下外骨骼的安全与自适应控制，该研究提出一种基于仿真训练的可变阻抗框架并内置稳定性保障机制。方法首先通过近端策略优化（PPO）生成人机协同运动数据，模拟肌肉激活与关节力补偿，随后训练一个融合语义指令与本体感觉历史的双模态策略，用于预测任务特定轨迹与阻抗增益。为确保安全，网络输出受基于李雅普诺夫稳定性理论推导的稳定性准则约束，限制刚度变化以维持系统渐近稳定。实验表明，该框架在真实场景中较基准方法显著降低代谢成本，验证其在多任务外骨骼控制中的可行性。
Visual Commonsense Driven Knowledge Refinements for Scene Graph Generation
Authors: Maëlic Neau, Salim Baloch, Jakob Suchan, Zoe Falomir, Mehul Bhatt
First: 2026-06-04T16:36:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:36:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning-driven Scene Graph Generation (SGG) models excel on frequent relation types but degrade sharply under annotation sparsity, failing to capture reliable visual commonsense knowledge. We propose a model-agnostic, semantically-guided knowledge refinement framework that systematically mines commonsense-grounded constraints from training data - capturing spatial, functional, and qualitative relational regularities - and uses general declarative commonsense reasoning to correct and refine ranked SGG predictions at inference time. The framework requires no manual rule authoring, no model retraining, and transfers across datasets and architectures. On three standard benchmarks, we obtain consistent improvements over strong baselines, demonstrating that structured visual commonsense reasoning over deep scene semantics is a practical and effective complement to purely learning-based scene graph generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于视觉常识驱动的知识精炼的场景图生成
学习驱动的场景图生成（SGG）模型在常见关系类型上表现优异，但在标注稀疏情况下性能急剧下降，无法捕捉可靠的视觉常识知识。我们提出一种模型无关、语义引导的知识精炼框架，系统性地从训练数据中挖掘基于常识的约束——捕捉空间、功能和定性关系规律，并利用通用的声明式常识推理在推理时修正和优化排序后的SGG预测。该框架无需手动规则编写、无需模型再训练，且可跨数据集和架构迁移。在三个标准基准上，我们持续优于强基线模型，证明了基于深度场景语义的结构化视觉常识推理是纯学习驱动场景图生成的有效补充。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of scene graph generation models under sparse annotations and their inability to leverage visual commonsense, the paper proposes a model-agnostic framework that mines commonsense-grounded constraints from training data to capture spatial, functional, and qualitative relational patterns. It applies general declarative commonsense reasoning at inference time to refine ranked predictions without requiring manual rule design or model retraining. 为了解决在标注稀疏情况下场景图生成模型性能急剧下降且无法有效利用视觉常识的问题，该研究提出一种模型无关的框架，从训练数据中挖掘基于常识的约束关系，用于推理时修正预测结果。该方法捕捉空间、功能和定性关系规律，并通过通用的常识推理对排名结果进行校正，无需重训练或人工规则编写。在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法稳定优于现有强基线模型，验证了将结构化视觉常识推理融入深度场景语义理解可有效提升生成效果。
Waypoints Matter: A Systematic Study for Sampling-Based Trajectory Planning
Authors: Josep M. Barbera, Antonio Artuñedo, Jorge Villagra
First: 2026-06-04T16:35:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:35:30+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables; accepted at IEEE ITSC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time autonomous driving commonly relies on sampling-based trajectory planners that link candidate trajectories to target waypoints along the road centerline. The placement of these waypoints directly impacts both the existence and quality of feasible trajectories. Yet, its effect on planner performance remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we treat waypoint placement as a first-class design variable. We hold the trajectory primitive and candidate budget fixed, and systematically sweep three placement strategies (uniform spacing, an augmented Ramer-Douglas-Peucker variant (RDP*), and a novel curvature-conditioned allocation) across 449 configurations and five CommonRoad maps of increasing geometric complexity. Our results show that the nominal inter-waypoint spacing $d_s$ is the primary performance driver, with large differences in planner reliability attributed to placement alone. Uniform sampling at a well-tuned spacing matches or surpasses both RDP* and the centered curvature variant. The curvature variant offers a small but consistent advantage on geometrically complex roads under reliability-first and balanced weightings, while RDP* never outperforms uniform sampling. These findings suggest that $d_s$ should be treated as the dominant tuning parameter, with geometry-aware strategies reserved for curvature-rich corridors where feasibility is the limiting factor.
中文标题/摘要
标题：航点至关重要：基于采样的轨迹规划系统性研究
实时自动驾驶通常依赖于将候选轨迹与道路中心线上的目标航点关联的基于采样的轨迹规划器。这些航点的布置直接影响可行轨迹的存在性和质量。然而，其对规划器性能的影响尚未被充分研究。本文将航点布置视为首要设计变量，在固定轨迹基本单元和候选预算的前提下，系统性地对三种布置策略（均匀间距、改进的Ramer-Douglas-Peucker算法（RDP*）和一种新颖的曲率条件分配策略）在449种配置和五种几何复杂度递增的CommonRoad地图上进行测试。结果表明，名义航点间距 $d_s$ 是主要性能驱动因素，仅凭布置方式即可导致规划器可靠性出现显著差异。在适当调优的间距下，均匀采样表现与RDP*和中心曲率变体相当或更优。曲率变体在几何复杂道路下，于可靠性优先和均衡权重条件下提供微小但稳定的优势，而RDP*从未优于均匀采样。这些发现表明，$d_s$ 应作为主导调参变量，几何感知策略仅在曲率丰富且可行性受限的走廊中使用。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how waypoint placement affects the performance of sampling-based trajectory planners in autonomous driving, motivated by the lack of systematic analysis on this design choice. The authors fix trajectory primitives and candidate budget, then evaluate three placement strategies—uniform spacing, an augmented Ramer-Douglas-Peucker variant (RDP*), and a curvature-conditioned method—across 449 configurations and five CommonRoad maps of increasing complexity. Results show that inter-waypoint spacing (d_s) is the dominant performance factor, with uniform sampling at an optimal spacing matching or outperforming both RDP* and curvature-based methods. The curvature method offers a marginal benefit on complex roads under reliability-focused metrics, while RDP* never surpasses uniform sampling, suggesting d_s should be prioritized as the primary tuning parameter, with geometry-aware methods used only where curvature-rich corridors constrain feasibility.
该研究探讨了采样轨迹规划中路点布置对自动驾驶性能的影响，将其视为首要设计变量，同时固定轨迹基本单元和候选轨迹预算。作者在449种配置和五种几何复杂度递增的CommonRoad地图上，系统评估了三种布置策略——均匀间距、改进的Ramer-Douglas-Peucker算法（RDP*）和曲率条件分配法。结果表明，名义间距d_s是性能主导因素，最优均匀间距的规划效果可媲美甚至超越RDP*和曲率分配法。曲率分配法在几何复杂道路中于可靠性优先和平衡权重下有微小但稳定的优势，而RDP*从未优于均匀采样，提示应优先调优d_s，仅在曲率丰富且可行性受限的路段使用几何感知策略。
End-to-End Subgraph Detection with GraphDETR
Authors: Dexiong Chen, Till Hendrik Schulz, Karsten Borgwardt
First: 2026-06-04T16:33:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:33:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Subgraph detection seeks to identify whether and where instances of query patterns occur within a larger graph. This problem is fundamental across scientific domains and is closely related to subgraph isomorphism, which is NP-complete, limiting combinatorial approaches to small patterns or moderately sized graphs. We introduce GraphDETR, a deep learning framework that formulates subgraph detection as a set prediction problem, analogous to DETR in object detection. GraphDETR encodes the target graph with a graph neural network, and employs a fixed set of learnable query vectors, decoded via a transformer decoder, to predict all pattern occurrences jointly in a single forward pass. This is enabled by training the model end-to-end with bipartite matching. Unlike traditional combinatorial methods that only solve exact structural matching, GraphDETR naturally extends to approximate matching, enabling detection beyond exact pattern correspondence. Empirically, we show that GraphDETR can detect diverse patterns, such as molecular structures, cycles, cliques, and fuzzy patterns of up to 50 nodes, in target graphs with up to 1000 nodes. We further evaluate on molecular functional group detection over the ChEMBL dataset, where GraphDETR predicts the complete set of functional groups per molecule, achieving a strong performance of $\text{AP}_{100} = 91.2$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于GraphDETR的端到端子图检测
子图检测旨在识别查询模式在大型图中的存在及其位置。该问题在科学领域具有基础性，与NP完全的子图同构问题密切相关，限制了组合方法仅适用于小规模模式或中等大小的图。我们提出了GraphDETR，一种深度学习框架，将子图检测形式化为集合预测问题，类似于目标检测中的DETR。GraphDETR通过图神经网络编码目标图，并采用一组固定的可学习查询向量，通过Transformer解码器解码，以单次前向传播联合预测所有模式出现。这得益于端到端训练与二分匹配的结合。与仅解决精确结构匹配的传统组合方法不同，GraphDETR自然扩展到近似匹配，从而实现超出精确模式对应关系的检测。实验证明，GraphDETR可在最多1000个节点的目标图中检测多样化的模式，如分子结构、环、团以及最多50个节点的模糊模式。我们在ChEMBL数据集上进一步评估分子功能基团检测，GraphDETR预测每个分子的完整功能基团集合，达到91.2的AP100性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational hardness of subgraph isomorphism and the need for scalable pattern detection in large graphs, GraphDETR introduces a deep learning framework that treats subgraph detection as a set prediction problem. It encodes the target graph using a graph neural network and employs a transformer decoder with learnable query vectors to jointly predict all pattern occurrences in one forward pass, trained via end-to-end bipartite matching. Experiments show GraphDETR successfully detects diverse patterns—including molecular structures, cycles, cliques, and fuzzy patterns up to 50 nodes—in graphs with up to 1000 nodes, and achieves an AP100 score of 91.2 on molecular functional group detection in ChEMBL, outperforming combinatorial methods by enabling approximate matching.
为克服子图同构问题的计算复杂性及组合方法的局限性，GraphDETR提出将子图检测建模为集合预测问题，借鉴DETR框架。该方法通过图神经网络编码目标图，并利用可学习的查询向量与Transformer解码器，在单次前向传播中联合预测所有模式出现位置，通过端到端的二分匹配训练。实验表明，该方法可检测包括分子结构、环、团及最多50节点的模糊模式在内的多种子图，适用于最多1000节点的图；在ChEMBL数据集上进行分子功能基团检测时，其AP100得分高达91.2，显著优于传统组合方法在可扩展性与近似匹配能力上的表现。
GMBFormer: An NDVI-Guided Global Memory Bank Transformer for Urban Green-Space Extraction from Ultra-High-Resolution Imagery
Authors: Hao Lei, Xi Cheng, Chenlu Shu, Zhiheng Chen, Zhengjie Duan, Haoyu Wang, Zhanfeng Shen
First: 2026-06-04T16:32:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:32:58+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urban green-space extraction from ultra-high-resolution (UHR) imagery is commonly performed patch by patch, which limits semantic reuse among spatially separated but visually similar vegetation patterns. Directly injecting the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) into red-green-blue (RGB) backbones can also blur the roles of visual appearance learning and physical vegetation confidence. We propose GMBFormer, a SegFormer-based framework that replaces adjacency-driven feature propagation with selective, similarity-driven prototype retrieval. Only RGB channels enter the backbone and decoder, while NDVI is decoupled as a physics-informed gate that admits high-confidence vegetation descriptors into a compact global memory bank through momentum updates. During training and inference, the current patch queries stored prototypes through memory-mediated cross-attention, and the retrieved response is integrated with bounded overhead. Experiments use a self-constructed Chengdu UHR dataset with 7,700 labeled 512 x 512 patches and two reduced-label settings derived from the public International Society for Photogrammetry and Remote Sensing (ISPRS) Potsdam dataset. Under the same training and evaluation protocol, GMBFormer obtains mean intersection over union (mIoU)/mean Dice (mDice) scores of 89.25%/94.31%, 92.17%/95.92%, and 83.72%/90.86%, respectively, improving the controlled SegFormer-B4 baseline in each setting. Ablation studies indicate that decoupled NDVI admission, memory retrieval, capacity, and momentum jointly shape the final performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GMBFormer：一种基于NDVI引导的全局记忆库Transformer，用于从超高分辨率影像中提取城市绿地
从超高分辨率（UHR）影像中提取城市绿地通常按块进行，这限制了空间上分离但视觉相似的植被模式之间的语义复用。直接将归一化植被指数（NDVI）注入红绿蓝（RGB）主干网络也会模糊视觉外观学习与物理植被置信度之间的角色。我们提出GMBFormer，一种基于SegFormer的框架，用选择性、相似性驱动的原型检索替代邻接驱动的特征传播。仅RGB通道进入主干网络和解码器，而NDVI被解耦为一个物理信息引导的门控机制，通过动量更新将高置信度植被描述符纳入紧凑的全局记忆库。训练和推理过程中，当前块查询通过记忆介导的交叉注意力检索存储的原型，且检索响应的集成开销可控。实验使用自建的成都UHR数据集（7,700个512×512标注块）及从公开的国际摄影测量与遥感学会（ISPRS）波茨坦数据集衍生的两种低标注设置。在相同训练与评估协议下，GMBFormer分别获得mIoU/mDice分数为89.25%/94.31%、92.17%/95.92%和83.72%/90.86%，在每种设置下均优于控制基线SegFormer-B4。消融研究表明，解耦NDVI输入、记忆检索、容量和动量共同决定了最终性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of patch-wise urban green-space extraction from ultra-high-resolution imagery and the blurring of visual and physical vegetation cues when directly integrating NDVI, the authors propose GMBFormer, a SegFormer-based model that decouples NDVI as a physics-informed gate to selectively admit high-confidence vegetation descriptors into a global memory bank via momentum updates. Only RGB channels pass through the backbone and decoder, while memory-mediated cross-attention retrieves prototypes during training and inference, enabling semantic reuse across spatially separated but visually similar vegetation patterns. Evaluated on a self-constructed Chengdu dataset and two reduced-label ISPRS Potsdam variants, GMBFormer achieves mIoU/mDice scores of 89.25%/94.31%, 92.17%/95.92%, and 83.72%/90.86%, outperforming the SegFormer-B4 baseline in all settings, with ablation studies confirming the critical role of NDVI decoupling, memory capacity, and momentum.
为了解决超高分辨率影像中按块提取城市绿地时语义复用不足以及直接融合NDVI导致视觉与物理特征混淆的问题，本文提出GMBFormer模型，其基于SegFormer架构，将NDVI解耦为物理信息门控，通过动量更新选择性地将高置信度植被描述符存入全局记忆库，RGB通道仅用于主干和解码器。推理时通过记忆介导的交叉注意力检索原型，实现空间分离但视觉相似植被模式的语义复用。在自建成都数据集及两个ISPRS Potsdam简化标签数据集上，GMBFormer分别取得89.25%/94.31%、92.17%/95.92%、83.72%/90.86%的mIoU/mDice分数，全面优于SegFormer-B4基线，消融实验验证了NDVI解耦、记忆容量与动量对性能的关键作用。
Physics in 2-Steps: Locking Motion Priors Before Visual Refinement Erases Them
Authors: Woojung Han, Seil Kang, Youngjun Jun, Min-Hung Chen, Fu-En Yang, Seong Jae Hwang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-04T16:30:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:30:39+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image-to-Video diffusion models leverage input images to generate visually stunning content, yet frequently produce motion that violates physical laws. We reveal a surprising finding: a 2-step generation often exhibits better physical consistency than a 50-step output from the same model. Through spectral analysis, we trace this to phase erosion during denoising; the phase degrades significantly (dropping by $\approx 18\%$ from step 2 to step 50), whereas the magnitude remains relatively stable. Building on this insight, we propose PhaseLock, a training-free framework that preserves the valid motion priors from few-step inference throughout the denoising trajectory. Rather than relying on full-step inference for physical consistency, PhaseLock extracts a motion prior from just 2 steps and enforces it onto high-fidelity generation via Latent Delta Guidance. Our approach effectively mitigates phase degradation, improving physical consistency by an average of 6.2 points across diverse models while largely maintaining visual fidelity, with negligible overhead ($1.06\times$ time, $1.02\times$ memory) and reduced reliance on expensive external guidance methods ($\sim5\times$ time).
中文标题/摘要
标题：两步物理：在视觉细化抹除之前锁定运动先验
图像到视频扩散模型利用输入图像生成视觉惊艳的内容，但常产生违反物理定律的运动。我们发现一个令人惊讶的现象：同一模型的两步生成往往比五十步生成更具物理一致性。通过频谱分析，我们将其归因于去噪过程中的相位侵蚀；相位显著退化（从第2步到第50步下降约18%），而幅度保持相对稳定。基于此洞察，我们提出PhaseLock，一种无需训练的框架，可在去噪轨迹中保留少量步数推断所得的有效运动先验。PhaseLock不依赖完整步数推断以保证物理一致性，而是仅从两步提取运动先验，并通过潜在Delta引导将其施加于高保真生成。该方法有效缓解相位退化，在多种模型中平均提升物理一致性6.2分，同时基本保持视觉保真度，开销微小（时间1.06倍，内存1.02倍），且减少对昂贵外部引导方法的依赖（时间约5倍）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that longer diffusion steps in image-to-video models often degrade physical consistency despite higher visual fidelity, this work investigates why short-step outputs can be more physically plausible. Through spectral analysis, it identifies phase erosion during denoising as the root cause, with phase quality dropping by approximately 18% from step 2 to step 50 while magnitude remains stable. The authors introduce PhaseLock, a training-free method that captures motion priors from just two initial steps and enforces them throughout the denoising process using Latent Delta Guidance. Experimental results show PhaseLock improves physical consistency by an average of 6.2 points across multiple models, with minimal impact on visual quality and negligible computational overhead, while reducing reliance on costly external guidance by about fivefold.
鉴于图像到视频扩散模型中较长的生成步骤常导致物理一致性下降，尽管视觉效果更佳，该研究发现仅2步生成反而优于50步输出。通过频谱分析，研究指出去噪过程中相位衰减是主因，相位从第2步到第50步下降约18%，而幅度保持稳定。作者提出PhaseLock方法，无需训练，从早期2步推理中提取运动先验，并通过Latent Delta Guidance将其贯穿整个去噪过程。实验表明，该方法在多个模型上平均提升物理一致性6.2分，视觉保真度影响极小，计算开销微乎其微（1.06倍时间、1.02倍内存），且将对昂贵外部引导方法的依赖降低约五倍。
An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making
Authors: Yonchanok Khaokaew, Ruochen Kong, Andreas Zufle, Hao Xue, Taylor Anderson, Chandini Raina MacIntyre, Matthew Scotch, Flora D. Salim, David J Heslop
First: 2026-06-04T16:30:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:30:13+00:00
Comments: 12 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modelling individual decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks is crucial for understanding behavioural dynamics and informing effective public health interventions. Prior work has shown that large language models can simulate realistic human behaviour by generating agent decisions based on demographic prompts and situational context. We build on this foundation with a spatially grounded, agent-based simulation framework that integrates LLM-generated decisions about self-reported influenza-like illness into a census-based synthetic population of agents. Location is treated as a central feature: agents are assigned to spatial units within cities, capturing the spatial distributions of different demographic groups using real-world census data and enabling geographically diverse behavioural modelling. We implement and compare three decision scenarios, independent reasoning, household influence, and message framing, and simulate self-reporting outcomes in San Francisco and Atlanta. Results reveal that income and education are the dominant drivers of reporting rate variation, with smaller but consistent effects from geography, LLM model choice, and message framing. Our framework generates synthetic data that captures both social and geographic heterogeneity, supporting spatial epidemiological modelling and bias-aware behavioural analysis.

Comparison of Deep Learning Frameworks For Rice Disease Mapping From UAV Multispectral Imaging
Authors: Yadav Raj Ghimire, Jagrati Talreja, Tewodros Syum Gebre, Timothy Agboada, Shikha V. Chandel, Leila Hashemi Beni
First: 2026-06-04T16:26:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:26:00+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted in IGARSS 2026. Copyright 2026 IEEE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this study, UAV multispectral imagery is used to segment the severity of bacterial leaf blight (BLB) in rice using convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transformer-based models. The evaluated architectures include U-Net with a ResNet- 101 encoder, U-Net++ with EfficientNet-B3 and EfficientNetB7, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer, all trained under a common pipeline with three input configurations (multispectral only, multispectral+NDVI, and multispectral+NDRE). Experiments are conducted using the publicly available BLB dataset with performance reported using mean IoU (mIoU), mean F1 (mF1), mean accuracy (mAcc), precision, and recall. U-Net++ with EfficientNet-B3 achieved the highest performance, with an mIoU of 97.62%. SegFormer obtained lower segmentation accuracy but comparable inference speed. Overall, the results indicate that lightweight CNN backbones remain more reliable for operational BLB monitoring while integration of vegetation indices provides small and consistent improvements. The study also highlights the value of standardised UAV datasets to compare disease mapping methods and encourages the use of CNN architectures for field implementation.
