F3-Tokenizer: Taming Audio Autoencoder Latents for Understanding and Generation
Authors: Dinghao Zhou, Xingchen Song, Di Wu, Pengyu Cheng, Shengfan Shen, Sixiang Lv
First: 2026-06-04T16:25:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:25:07+00:00
Comments: Technical report; early work; 9 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continuous audio autoencoders reconstruct waveforms well but often produce latents with weak structure for understanding, while self-supervised audio encoders capture semantics but are not directly decodable. This mismatch complicates a single audio tokenizer that must support both understanding and generation. We adapt continuous autoencoder latents to this setting with two components: a noise-regularized autoencoder bottleneck and a latent-side representation encoder. The bottleneck uses channel normalization and stochastic perturbation instead of KL-based variational training, yielding scale-controlled continuous latents for reconstruction and autoregressive generation. The representation encoder is trained on frozen autoencoder latents with RQ-MTP and frozen-LLM supervision. The resulting tokenizer provides high-dimensional representations for understanding while preserving normalized continuous latents as generation targets
中文标题/摘要
标题：F3-Tokenizer：驯化音频自编码器潜在表示以实现理解和生成
连续音频自编码器能很好地重建波形，但其潜在表示常缺乏结构，不利于理解；而自监督音频编码器擅长捕捉语义，但无法直接解码。这种不匹配使得单一音频分词器难以同时支持理解和生成。我们通过两个组件对连续自编码器潜在表示进行适配：一个噪声正则化自编码器瓶颈和一个潜在表示侧的表示编码器。瓶颈采用通道归一化与随机扰动，而非基于KL散度的变分训练，从而生成可控制尺度的连续潜在表示，支持重建与自回归生成。表示编码器在冻结的自编码器潜在表示上，结合RQ-MTP与冻结大语言模型监督进行训练。最终的分词器在支持高维语义表示用于理解的同时，保留归一化连续潜在表示作为生成目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between audio autoencoders' strong reconstruction ability and their weak semantic structure, and between self-supervised encoders' semantic strength and lack of direct decodability, this work proposes F3-Tokenizer to unify understanding and generation. It adapts autoencoder latents using a noise-regularized bottleneck with channel normalization and stochastic perturbation to produce scale-controlled continuous representations, and a latent-side encoder trained on frozen latents with RQ-MTP and frozen-LLM supervision. Experiments show the tokenizer achieves high-dimensional semantic representations suitable for understanding while maintaining continuous latents that enable effective autoregressive generation.
为解决音频自编码器重建能力强但语义结构弱、自监督编码器语义丰富但无法直接解码的问题，本文提出F3-Tokenizer统一理解与生成任务。方法包括：用通道归一化与随机扰动替代KL散度训练的瓶颈结构，生成尺度可控的连续潜变量；并训练一个潜变量侧表示编码器，基于冻结的自编码器潜变量与RQ-MTP及冻结大语言模型监督。实验表明，该分词器在保持连续潜变量用于自回归生成的同时，能提供高维语义表示用于理解任务。
Where Should Knowledge Enter? A Layered Framework for Knowledge Infusion in Multimodal Iterative Generative Mo
Authors: Renjith Prasad, Chathurangi Shyalika, Anushka Pawar, Amit Sheth
First: 2026-06-04T16:24:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:24:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal generative models produce fluent outputs but remain unreliable when generation must respect structured, domain-specific, or safety-critical knowledge. Existing methods incorporate knowledge through mechanisms such as prompt augmentation, guidance, latent editing, or fine-tuning, yet they are typically categorized by technique rather than by the component of the generative process they modify. We argue that knowledge infusion in iterative generative models is fundamentally anintervention-layer problem. Since thegenerative process unfolds as a trajectory of internal states, knowledge can act on four structurally distinct components of this process: the input/output boundary, the transition function, the intermediate state, and the model parameters. This maps to four intervention layers: surface, trajectory, latent, and parametric infusion. We instantiate the framework in diffusion models, map representative methods to all four layers, and derive design principles for multi-layer composition. In a controlled safety-alignment experiment using a multimodal knowledge graph with two diffusion backbones, we implement three of the four layers cumulatively, surface (input-side and output-side) and trajectory--latent (mid-generation). We show empirically that each additional layer addresses failure classes that prior layers cannot reach, reducing knowledge-violating outputs by 70.97% compared to vanilla generation and empirically confirming the framework's complementarity prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：知识应从何处注入？多模态迭代生成模型中知识注入的分层框架
多模态生成模型虽能产生流畅输出，但在生成必须遵循结构化、领域特定或安全关键知识时仍不可靠。现有方法通过提示增强、引导、潜在空间编辑或微调等方式引入知识，但通常按技术分类，而非按其修改生成过程的组件分类。我们认为，迭代生成模型中的知识注入本质上是干预层问题。由于生成过程表现为内部状态的轨迹，知识可作用于该过程四个结构上不同的组件：输入/输出边界、转换函数、中间状态和模型参数。这对应四个干预层：表面层、轨迹层、潜在层和参数层。我们在扩散模型中实例化该框架，将代表性方法映射到所有四层，并推导出多层组合的设计原则。在使用双扩散骨干网络的多模态知识图谱的受控安全对齐实验中，我们累计实现其中三层：表面层（输入侧和输出侧）和轨迹-潜在层（生成中段）。实证表明，每增加一层即可解决前一层无法覆盖的失败类别，相较原始生成，知识违规输出减少70.97%，实证验证了框架的互补性预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of multimodal generative models when respecting structured knowledge, this work proposes a layered framework that categorizes knowledge infusion by intervention layer rather than technique. It identifies four distinct layers—surface, trajectory, latent, and parametric—corresponding to where knowledge can act in the generative process. Implemented in diffusion models, the framework maps existing methods to these layers and demonstrates that cumulative application of surface and trajectory-latent layers reduces knowledge-violating outputs by 70.97% compared to vanilla generation, empirically validating the complementarity of layered interventions.
为解决多模态生成模型在遵循结构化知识时的不可靠性，本文提出按干预层而非技术分类的知识注入框架。该框架定义了四个干预层——表面层、轨迹层、潜在层和参数层，对应生成过程中知识可作用的四个结构化组件。在扩散模型中实现该框架，将现有方法映射至各层，并实证表明叠加表面层与轨迹-潜在层可使知识违规输出减少70.97%，验证了分层干预的互补性。
Maximising the Set-Piece Return: Optimising Football Corner Tactics with Graph Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Sean Groom, Michael Groom, Francisco Belo, Axl Rice, Liam Anderson, Victor-Alexandru Darvariu, Shuo Wang
First: 2026-06-04T16:22:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:22:44+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning is increasingly employed for the evaluation of football tactics. However, existing approaches focus on characterising historical actions or analyst-specified counterfactual scenarios. In this work, we seek to go beyond the imitation of historically observed patterns towards discovering new generalisable player configurations and strategies. To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最大化定位球回攻：利用图强化学习优化足球角球战术
机器学习正越来越多地用于评估足球战术。然而，现有方法主要关注刻画历史动作或分析人员指定的反事实场景。本研究旨在超越对历史模式的模仿，探索新的可泛化的球员配置与策略。为此，我们聚焦于优化角球进攻套路，将决策问题建模为：中心策略调整进攻球员位置与速度，以最大化首次触球射门概率。不同于经典优化方法仅解决孤立配置，我们贡献了一种在图结构数据上运行的强化学习架构，可生成调整任意初始球员位置的通用策略。在超过3000个英超角球数据上评估，我们的方法在匹配推理预算下显著优于基线优化技术。结果表明，图强化学习可将定位球分析从历史评估与模仿转向奖励驱动的战术发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to move beyond historical imitation in football tactic evaluation, this work uses graph reinforcement learning to optimize corner kick routines by dynamically adjusting player positions and velocities to maximize first contact shot probability. Unlike traditional optimization methods that target fixed setups, the proposed architecture operates on graph-structured player data to generate a generalizable policy adaptable to arbitrary starting configurations. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, the method significantly outperforms baseline optimization techniques under matched inference budgets, demonstrating the potential of graph RL to drive reward-based tactical discovery in set-piece scenarios.
为突破单纯模仿历史战术的局限，本文采用图强化学习优化角球战术，通过动态调整球员位置与速度以最大化首次触球射门概率。该方法将决策建模为图结构的策略，可适应任意初始球员配置，不同于传统优化方法仅针对固定阵型。在3000余场英超角球数据上测试，该方法在相同推理预算下显著优于基线优化方法，表明图强化学习可推动角球分析从历史评估转向奖励驱动的战术发现。
Detectability in Diversity: Improved Canary Crafting for Privacy Auditing in One Run
Authors: Mathieu Dagréou, Aurélien Bellet
First: 2026-05-26T17:06:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:21:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Privacy auditing aims to empirically assess privacy leakage in machine learning models using membership inference attacks (MIAs), and to derive lower bounds on differential privacy (DP) parameters. Recent one-run auditing methods address the high cost of standard approaches by relying on a single training run with multiple "canary" points whose inclusion or exclusion must be detected by the auditor. In this work, we study the problem of efficiently crafting canaries for one-run privacy auditing. Motivated by recent theoretical insights suggesting that interference between canaries contributes to weaker leakage estimates compared to multi-run methods, we propose to optimize canaries to be both highly detectable and minimally interfering. Our approach combines a greedy initialization based on influence functions with a bilevel optimization procedure that maximizes distinguishability while promoting diversity in embedding space, enabling the use of computationally efficient bilevel algorithms. Experiments show that our method achieves stronger privacy leakage estimates at a lower computational cost than existing canary crafting approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多样性中的可检测性：面向单次运行隐私审计的改进型Canary构造
隐私审计旨在通过成员推断攻击（MIAs）实证评估机器学习模型中的隐私泄露，并推导差分隐私（DP）参数的下界。近期的单次运行审计方法通过依赖单次训练运行并包含多个“Canary”点（其是否被包含需由审计者检测），以降低标准方法的高成本。本文研究如何高效构造Canary以支持单次运行隐私审计。受近期理论见解启发，即Canary间的干扰会削弱相比多轮方法的泄露估计，我们提出优化Canary使其兼具高可检测性与最小干扰性。我们的方法结合基于影响函数的贪心初始化与双层优化过程，在最大化可区分性的同时促进嵌入空间中的多样性，从而支持计算高效的双层算法。实验表明，我们的方法在更低计算成本下实现了比现有Canary构造方法更强的隐私泄露估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that canary interference weakens privacy leakage estimates in one-run auditing, this work proposes a method to craft canaries that are both highly detectable and minimally interfering. It combines greedy initialization via influence functions with bilevel optimization to maximize distinguishability while promoting embedding diversity, enabling efficient computation. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach yields stronger privacy leakage estimates compared to existing methods, while reducing computational cost.
鉴于单次运行审计中canary相互干扰会削弱隐私泄露估计，本文提出一种兼顾高可检测性与低干扰性的canary构造方法。该方法结合基于影响函数的贪心初始化与双层优化，以最大化可区分性并促进嵌入空间多样性，从而实现高效计算。实验表明，该方法在降低计算成本的同时，比现有方法获得更强的隐私泄露估计。
Function-Space Priors for Bayesian Neural ODEs with Application to Vessel Trajectory Prediction
Authors: Jaeyeong Lee, Wonmo Koo, Heeyoung Kim
First: 2026-06-04T16:21:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:21:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vessel trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data is essential for maritime situational awareness, yet it remains challenging due to irregular sampling, missing reports, and complex dynamics. Beyond accurate point forecasts, maritime applications also demand well-calibrated uncertainty estimates for reliable decision-making. Bayesian Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) offer a principled framework for continuous-time trajectory modeling with uncertainty quantification by placing a prior over the neural vector field parameters. However, the commonly used isotropic Gaussian weight prior fails to encode informative structural properties of vessel dynamics, such as smoothness and locality. Existing function-space Bayesian neural network methods address this limitation for static mappings, but do not transfer directly to Neural ODEs, where the primary quantity of interest is the trajectory rather than the vector field itself. In principle, one could place a Gaussian process (GP) prior directly over ODE solutions, but this requires propagating distributions through a nonlinear ODE solver, which is analytically intractable. To address this challenge, we adopt a practical approach that imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at a finite set of measurement points. Specifically, we augment the standard weight-space variational objective with a kernel-based regularizer that penalizes deviations of the vector field from the structure implied by a GP prior. To handle long and irregular AIS trajectories, we further combine this function-space regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while maintaining global consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于贝叶斯神经常微分方程的功能空间先验及其在船舶轨迹预测中的应用
从自动识别系统（AIS）数据预测船舶轨迹对海上态势感知至关重要，但因采样不规则、报告缺失及动力学复杂而具有挑战性。除准确的点预测外，海上应用还需可靠的不确定性估计以支持可靠决策。贝叶斯神经常微分方程（ODE）通过在神经向量场参数上施加先验，提供了一种连续时间轨迹建模并量化不确定性的原理性框架。然而，常用的各向同性高斯权重先验无法编码船舶动力学的结构信息，如平滑性和局部性。现有功能空间贝叶斯神经网络方法虽可解决静态映射的此局限，但无法直接应用于神经ODE，因神经ODE的核心关注点是轨迹而非向量场本身。原则上，可直接在ODE解上施加高斯过程（GP）先验，但需通过非线性ODE求解器传播分布，这在分析上不可行。为应对这一挑战，我们采用实用方法：在有限测量点处的向量场上直接施加基于GP核的先验。具体而言，我们在标准权重空间变分目标中增加基于核的正则化项，以惩罚向量场偏离GP先验所暗示结构的偏差。为处理长且不规则的AIS轨迹，我们进一步将此功能空间正则化与概率多重射击相结合，从而在保持全局一致性的同时解耦时间片段上的推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable vessel trajectory prediction from irregular AIS data and the demand for calibrated uncertainty estimates, this work proposes a function-space prior approach for Bayesian Neural ODEs. Instead of using isotropic Gaussian weight priors that fail to capture vessel dynamics’ smoothness and locality, it imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at discrete measurement points, regularizing the model via a kernel-based penalty. To handle long, irregular trajectories, it combines this regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while preserving global consistency. Experiments show improved trajectory accuracy and uncertainty calibration compared to standard Bayesian Neural ODEs and other baselines.
为应对AIS数据采样不规则、缺失报告及船舶运动复杂性带来的轨迹预测挑战，本文提出一种结合函数空间先验的贝叶斯神经常微分方程框架。传统方法使用各向同性高斯权重先验难以捕捉船舶运动的平滑性和局部性，本文转而直接在离散测量点处的向量场上施加基于高斯过程核的先验，通过在变分目标中加入核正则化项进行约束。为处理长时程与不规则轨迹，进一步结合概率多段法，实现时间片段间独立推断的同时保持全局一致性。实验表明，该方法在轨迹预测精度与不确定性校准方面优于标准贝叶斯神经ODE及其他基线方法。
EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading
Authors: Zhihao Wu, Linhai Zhang, Taiyi Wang, Runcong Zhao, Peter Andrews, Cesare Aloisi, Yulan He
First: 2026-06-04T16:20:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:20:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EDIT：基于证据诊断的干预训练用于规则忠实的LLM评分
可靠的评分标准评分不仅需要准确的分数预测，还需要每个判断都基于评分标准和学生答案的证据。现有的信用分配和干预方法，主要设计用于自包含的推理任务（如数学推理），在此场景下表现不佳，因为它们无法识别评分推理出错的位置，也无法追踪模型在推理过程中对最终分数信念的变化。我们提出证据诊断干预训练（EDIT），一种两阶段框架，用于训练更符合评分标准的LLM评分器。首先，EDIT-SFT 利用模型内部信号（最终分数的后验信念和输入锚定得分）定位有问题的推理步骤，然后仅修订这些局部步骤，并借助评分标准检查表进行修正。其次，EDIT-RL 通过信念引导的奖励塑造校准评分器，惩罚有害的信念漂移，同时允许有益的探索。在两个真实世界、多学科评分基准上的实验表明，EDIT 在领域内和领域外划分上均一致优于强大的监督微调和强化学习基线，消融研究证实内部状态诊断驱动了这些性能提升。
Summary / 总结
To improve LLMs' ability to grade student responses according to rubrics, the paper addresses the gap that existing methods fail to diagnose where grading reasoning goes wrong or track belief drift during reasoning. EDIT introduces a two-phase training framework: EDIT-SFT uses internal model signals like posterior mark beliefs and input-grounding scores to identify flawed reasoning steps, then revises them using rubric checklists; EDIT-RL further refines the grader via reward shaping that penalizes harmful belief drift while permitting beneficial exploration. Experiments on two real-world grading benchmarks show EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines across both in-domain and out-of-domain settings, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics are key to its performance gains.
为提升大语言模型在评分任务中对评分标准的遵循度，本文提出EDIT框架，包含两个阶段：EDIT-SFT利用模型内部信号（最终得分后验信念与输入相关性分数）定位推理中的错误步骤，并仅针对这些局部步骤结合评分标准进行修正；EDIT-RL则通过信念引导的奖励塑造优化评分器，惩罚有害信念漂移同时允许有益探索。在两个真实多学科评分基准上的实验表明，EDIT在域内与域外测试中均显著优于强监督微调与强化学习基线，消融实验验证了内部状态诊断是性能提升的关键因素。
"Chi nas dal soch el sent de legn" -- Auditing Text Corpora for Lombard
Authors: Edoardo Signoroni, Pavel Rychlý
First: 2026-06-04T16:20:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:20:14+00:00
Comments: Submitted to TSD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Several of the world's languages are still under-resourced in terms of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. This is mostly due to the lack of high-quality datasets to train, develop, and evaluate systems and models for several tasks, such as Machine Translation (MT). We conduct a manual audit of the parallel and monolingual corpora available for Lombard, an under-resourced language continuum from Italy. Our analysis reveals that the perceived abundance of web-scraped data is an illusion, with massive datasets plagued by severe language misidentification, boilerplate text, and non-linguistic noise. Furthermore, we analyze the orthographic composition of the valid Lombard portions across web-scraped datasets, curated corpora, and benchmarks. Our findings show conflicting orthographical systems and severe representational bias across all corpora: high-quality data is heavily skewed towards Western Lombard varieties, with Eastern ones left on the margins. This underscores the need for variety-aware, community-driven data curation rather than purely quantity-driven scraping.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“从木头中生长出的感官”——对伦巴第语平行语料库和单语语料库的审计
世界上许多语言在自然语言处理（NLP）工具方面仍资源匮乏，这主要是由于缺乏高质量数据集，用于训练、开发和评估多种任务（如机器翻译）的系统和模型。我们对意大利伦巴第语（一种资源匮乏的语言连续体）可用的平行语料库和单语语料库进行了手动审计。分析表明，网络抓取数据的“丰富性”是一种假象，大规模数据集充斥着严重的语言误识别、模板文本和非语言噪声。此外，我们分析了网络抓取数据集、精选语料库和基准测试中有效伦巴第语部分的拼写构成。研究发现，所有语料库均存在拼写系统冲突和严重表征偏倚：高质量数据严重偏向西部伦巴第方言，东部方言则被边缘化。这凸显了需要以方言意识、社区驱动的数据整理，而非单纯以数量驱动的抓取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of high-quality NLP resources for under-resourced languages like Lombard, this study manually audits available parallel and monolingual corpora to assess their reliability and linguistic representativeness. The analysis exposes that web-scraped datasets, often perceived as abundant, suffer from severe misidentification, boilerplate content, and non-linguistic noise. Orthographic examination across datasets reveals inconsistent writing systems and a strong bias toward Western Lombard varieties, marginalizing Eastern ones. These findings highlight the inadequacy of quantity-focused data collection and advocate for community-driven, variety-aware curation to produce more equitable and linguistically accurate corpora.
鉴于意大利伦巴第语等资源匮乏语言缺乏高质量的自然语言处理数据，本研究对现有平行语料和单语语料进行了人工审计，评估其可靠性和代表性。分析发现，常被误认为丰富的网络爬取语料存在严重语言误标、模板文本和非语言噪声问题。对各语料库中有效伦巴第语部分的正字法分析进一步揭示了书写系统的不一致和对西方伦巴第方言的严重偏向，边缘化了东方方言。研究结果强调应采用社区主导、方言意识的数据整理，而非单纯依赖数量驱动的网络爬取。
Performance Evaluation of GraphCast for Medium-Range Weather Forecasting over Brazil
Authors: Wolfgang R. Rowell, Lucas S. Kupssinskü
First: 2026-06-04T16:19:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:19:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The paradigm of global weather forecasting is rapidly shifting with the emergence of Machine Learning Weather Prediction models (MLWP). While these data-driven architectures demonstrate remarkable global skill, regional benchmarks in the Global South remain scarce, leaving their efficacy in complex, highly convective environments largely unverified. This study evaluates the performance of GraphCast operational against the deterministic ECMWF IFS HRES as baseline across four distinct Brazilian climatic sub-regions. Utilizing a scalable, cloud-native pipeline and the WeatherBench-X framework for benchmarking weather models, we assess selected tropospheric variables ($T_{850}$, $Q_{850}$, $Z_{500}$) over four selected seasonal windows, employing the operational IFS analysis as the ground truth to calculate the statistical metrics for both models. Results reveal a regime-dependent skill profile. During the austral winter, GraphCast underperforms in the medium range (lead days 2-7) for $Z_{500}$ when resolving fast-propagating baroclinic systems over southern Brazil, but regains an advantage in the extended range, where its inherent smoothing of chaotic small-scale variability becomes beneficial under deterministic skill metrics. Conversely, during the austral summer wet season, GraphCast accurately captures large-scale moisture transport while intrinsically dampening the high-frequency convective variability that degrades deterministic NWP temperature forecasts. These findings establish a baseline for Brazil and define the specific physical boundaries that will guide future ``tropicalization'' efforts, aiming to optimize these foundational AI models for regional resilience.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GraphCast在巴西中程天气预报中的性能评估
随着机器学习天气预测模型（MLWP）的出现，全球天气预报范式正迅速转变。尽管这些数据驱动架构在全球范围内表现出色，但在全球南方地区的区域基准仍稀缺，其在复杂、强对流环境中的有效性尚未得到充分验证。本研究评估了GraphCast在四个不同巴西气候子区域中，相对于确定性ECMWF IFS HRES基准模型的性能。利用可扩展的云原生管道和WeatherBench-X框架对天气模型进行基准测试，我们评估了四个选定季节窗口内选定的对流层变量（$T_{850}$，$Q_{850}$，$Z_{500}$），以操作性IFS分析作为地面真值，计算两个模型的统计指标。结果揭示了其技能表现具有气候模式依赖性：在南半球冬季，GraphCast在南部巴西快速传播的斜压系统中，对$Z_{500}$的中程预报（提前2-7天）表现欠佳，但在延伸范围预报中，其对混沌小尺度变率的平滑特性在确定性技能指标下反而更具优势；而在南半球夏季湿润季节，GraphCast能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，同时内在抑制高频率对流变率，从而改善确定性数值天气预报的温度预测。这些发现为巴西建立了基准，并定义了特定物理边界，将指导未来“热带化”工作，旨在优化这些基础AI模型以增强区域韧性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate machine learning weather models in underrepresented tropical regions, this study compares GraphCast with ECMWF IFS HRES across four Brazilian climatic zones using WeatherBench-X. The evaluation focuses on tropospheric variables over seasonal windows, using IFS analysis as ground truth. Results show GraphCast underperforms in winter for mid-range baroclinic systems but excels in extended range due to its smoothing of chaotic variability. In summer, it accurately captures moisture transport while suppressing convective noise that degrades traditional forecasts, establishing a regional performance baseline for future tropical AI model optimization.
为评估机器学习天气模型在热带地区的表现，本研究在巴西四个气候区对比了GraphCast与ECMWF IFS HRES模型，使用WeatherBench-X框架和实况分析作为基准。针对对流层变量在季节窗口内的表现，结果表明GraphCast在冬季2–7天的500百帕位势高度预报中因难以捕捉快速发展的斜压系统而表现较差，但在延长预报期时，其平滑小尺度混沌波动的优势显现。夏季则准确模拟了水汽输送并抑制了破坏确定性预报的高频对流噪声，为巴西地区建立了性能基准，并指明了未来热带化AI模型优化的物理边界。
Attack Detection using Time Series Foundation Models
Authors: Sribalaji C. Anand, Anh Tung Nguyen, George J. Pappas
First: 2026-06-04T16:19:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:19:24+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the problem of attack detection in cyber-physical systems without any knowledge of the plant model or its structure. A remotely located plant transmits sensor measurements to an operator over a network that is assumed to be under attack. We consider two classes of attacks: model-free replay attacks and model-based stealthy attacks. For the latter, we derive closed-form expressions for the optimal stealthy attack policy against a $χ^2$ detector, for both linear and nonlinear systems. We then propose a model-structure-free detector based on TimesFM, a time-series foundation model developed by Google Research, which serves as a surrogate residual generator operating in a zero-shot fashion. We show empirically that the TimesFM-based detector achieves a comparable or superior attack detection performance. The efficacy of the proposed approach is demonstrated numerically on the IEEE 14-bus power system. We also demonstrate that TimesFM predictions can serve as a substitute for corrupted measurements, a practical mitigation technique when classical redundancy assumptions fail.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于时间序列基础模型的攻击检测
本文解决了在无任何被控对象模型或结构知识的条件下，对物理信息系统中的攻击检测问题。一个远程被控对象通过网络向操作员传输传感器测量值，该网络被假设处于攻击状态。我们考虑两类攻击：无模型重放攻击和基于模型的隐蔽攻击。对于后者，我们推导出针对χ²检测器的最优隐蔽攻击策略的闭式表达式，适用于线性和非线性系统。随后，我们提出了一种基于TimesFM（由Google Research开发的时间序列基础模型）的无模型结构检测器，该检测器作为零样本操作的残差生成代理。实证研究表明，基于TimesFM的检测器可实现相当或更优的攻击检测性能。该方法在IEEE 14节点电力系统上的数值实验中得到了验证。此外，我们还证明TimesFM的预测可作为被破坏测量值的替代，这是一种在经典冗余假设失效时实用的缓解技术。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect cyber-attacks in cyber-physical systems without prior knowledge of plant dynamics, this work proposes a model-structure-free attack detection method using TimesFM, a time-series foundation model. It addresses both model-free replay attacks and model-based stealthy attacks, deriving optimal stealthy attack policies for $χ^2$ detectors in linear and nonlinear systems. The TimesFM-based detector operates in zero-shot fashion as a surrogate residual generator and empirically achieves comparable or better detection performance than traditional methods, validated on the IEEE 14-bus power system. Additionally, TimesFM’s predictions can substitute for corrupted sensor data, offering a practical mitigation strategy when redundancy assumptions are invalid.
该论文旨在无需依赖被控系统模型结构的情况下检测网络攻击。提出一种基于TimesFM时间序列基础模型的无模型结构检测方法，该模型作为零样本残差生成器，用于识别重放攻击或隐蔽攻击引发的异常。方法推导了针对$χ^2$检测器的线性和非线性系统最优隐蔽攻击策略，并实验证明TimesFM检测器性能与传统方法相当或更优。在IEEE 14节点电力系统上的数值实验验证了该方法的有效性，同时表明TimesFM预测可替代被攻击的传感器数据，作为冗余假设失效时的实用缓解手段。
Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation
Authors: Yohann Benchetrit, Marlène Careil, Simon Dahan, Hubert Banville, Stéphane d'Ascoli, Jean-Rémi King
First: 2026-06-04T16:18:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:18:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Brain decoding is limited by the availability of labeled neural data, and remains challenging in low-data regimes. To address this issue, we investigate whether and when brain decoding can be boosted by augmenting small fMRI datasets with synthetic data generated by a pretrained model of fMRI responses to stimuli. We use TRIBE v2, a large encoding model pretrained on more than 1000 hours of fMRI responses to video, audio and language. For each dataset, we evaluate systematic grids that show how the performance of image decoders varies with the amount of synthetic data used for training. Our results, based on two datasets (the 7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset and 3T fMRI BOLD5000), show up to 68% improvement in Top-10 image-retrieval accuracy compared to decoders trained only on real data. Importantly, the proportion of augmented data required to reach a given image decoding performance needs to be adjusted depending on the data source. Surprisingly, image decoders trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can perform above chance in some settings, suggesting that TRIBE v2 can support zero-shot brain-to-image decoding. Together, these results show how large-scale models of the fMRI responses to sight, sound and language may provide a foundation to improve the data efficiency for image decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过TRIBE v2数据增强提升脑到图像解码性能
脑解码受限于标注神经数据的可用性，在数据量少的情况下仍具挑战性。为解决此问题，我们研究了通过用预训练的fMRI响应刺激生成模型合成数据来增强小规模fMRI数据集是否能提升脑解码性能。我们使用TRIBE v2，一个在超过1000小时的视频、音频和语言fMRI响应上预训练的大型编码模型。对每个数据集，我们评估系统性网格，展示图像解码器性能随训练中合成数据量的变化。基于两个数据集（7T fMRI自然场景数据集和3T fMRI BOLD5000）的结果显示，与仅使用真实数据训练的解码器相比，Top-10图像检索准确率最高提升68%。重要的是，达到特定图像解码性能所需的合成数据比例需根据数据源调整。令人惊讶的是，在某些情况下，仅使用合成fMRI数据训练的图像解码器可超越随机水平，表明TRIBE v2可支持零样本脑到图像解码。这些结果共同表明，大规模fMRI响应模型（针对视觉、听觉和语言）可能为提升图像解码的数据效率提供基础。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the scarcity of labeled fMRI data for brain-to-image decoding, this study investigates whether augmenting small datasets with synthetic fMRI data generated by the pretrained TRIBE v2 model can improve decoding performance. By systematically varying the proportion of synthetic data across two real fMRI datasets, the authors demonstrate that image retrieval accuracy can increase by up to 68% compared to models trained solely on real data. Crucially, the optimal ratio of synthetic to real data depends on the source dataset, and surprisingly, models trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can still achieve above-chance performance in some cases, indicating potential for zero-shot decoding.
为解决fMRI标注数据稀缺导致的脑图像解码性能受限问题，本研究探索了利用预训练的TRIBE v2模型生成的合成fMRI数据增强小规模数据集是否能提升解码效果。通过在两个真实fMRI数据集（7T自然场景数据集和3T BOLD5000）上系统调整合成数据比例，研究发现图像检索准确率最高可提升68%（相比仅用真实数据训练）。重要的是，合成数据与真实数据的最佳比例因数据源而异，且令人惊讶的是，仅用合成fMRI数据训练的模型在某些情况下仍能超越随机水平，表明TRIBE v2支持零样本脑图像解码。
Drive-KD: Multi-Teacher Distillation for VLMs in Autonomous Driving
Authors: Weitong Lian, Zecong Tang, Haoran Li, Tianjian Gao, Yifei Wang, Zixu Wang, Lingyi Meng, Tengju Ru, Zhejun Cui, Yichen Zhu, Hangshuo Cao, Qi Kang, Tianxing Chen, Kaixuan Wang, Yu Zhang
First: 2026-01-29T05:41:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:17:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous driving is an important and safety-critical task, and recent advances in LLMs/VLMs have opened new possibilities for reasoning and planning in this domain. However, large models demand substantial GPU memory and exhibit high inference latency, while conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often struggles to bridge the capability gaps of small models. To address these limitations, we propose Drive-KD, a framework that decomposes autonomous driving into a "perception-reasoning-planning" triad and transfers these capabilities via knowledge distillation. We identify layer-specific attention as the distillation signal to construct capability-specific single-teacher models that outperform baselines. Moreover, we unify these single-teacher settings into a multi-teacher distillation framework and introduce asymmetric gradient projection to mitigate cross-capability gradient conflicts. Extensive evaluations validate the generalization of our method across diverse model families and scales. Experiments show that our distilled InternVL3-1B model, with ~42 times less GPU memory and ~11.4 times higher throughput, achieves better overall performance than the pretrained 78B model from the same family on DriveBench, and surpasses GPT-5.1 on the planning dimension, providing insights toward efficient autonomous driving VLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Drive-KD：面向自动驾驶的多教师知识蒸馏框架
自动驾驶是一项重要且安全关键的任务，近期大语言模型/视觉语言模型（VLMs）的发展为该领域的推理与规划开辟了新可能。然而，大模型需大量GPU内存且推理延迟高，传统监督微调（SFT）常难以弥合小模型能力差距。为此，我们提出Drive-KD框架，将自动驾驶分解为“感知-推理-规划”三元组，并通过知识蒸馏迁移能力。我们识别层特定注意力作为蒸馏信号，构建能力特异的单教师模型，超越基线。进一步，我们将单教师设置统一为多教师蒸馏框架，并引入非对称梯度投影以缓解跨能力梯度冲突。大量评估验证了方法在不同模型家族与规模下的泛化能力。实验表明，我们的蒸馏模型InternVL3-1B，GPU内存需求减少约42倍、吞吐量提升约11.4倍，在DriveBench上整体性能优于同家族78B预训练模型，且在规划维度超越GPT-5.1，为高效自动驾驶VLMs提供启示。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for efficient and scalable autonomous driving systems, Drive-KD introduces a multi-teacher knowledge distillation framework that decomposes driving tasks into perception, reasoning, and planning modules. It leverages layer-specific attention as a distillation signal to train capability-focused single-teacher models, then unifies them under a multi-teacher setup with asymmetric gradient projection to resolve cross-capability conflicts. Experimental results demonstrate that the distilled InternVL3-1B model requires 42 times less GPU memory and delivers 11.4 times higher throughput than its 78B predecessor, while achieving superior overall performance on DriveBench and outperforming GPT-5.1 in planning.
为提升自动驾驶系统的效率与可扩展性，Drive-KD提出一种多教师知识蒸馏框架，将驾驶任务分解为感知、推理与规划模块，并以层特定注意力作为蒸馏信号构建能力导向的单教师模型，再通过非对称梯度投影统一多教师架构以缓解跨能力梯度冲突。实验表明，蒸馏后的InternVL3-1B模型仅需原78B模型约42倍更少的GPU内存，推理吞吐量提升11.4倍，整体性能优于原模型，并在规划维度超越GPT-5.1。
Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation
Authors: Zehua Cheng, Wei Dai, Jiahao Sun
First: 2026-06-04T16:16:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:16:51+00:00
Comments: 18 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Probabilistic inference over spatially embedded variables requires beliefs that respect $SE(3)$ symmetry, yet existing equivariant networks produce only scalars and vectors -- not the rank-2 precision tensors needed for anisotropic uncertainty, and single-component messages collapse multi-modal energy landscapes to physically meaningless averages. We introduce Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework whose messages are equivariant Gaussian mixture models with sufficient statistics that transform exactly under $SE(3)$. Rank-2 precision matrices are synthesised via equivariant outer products, ingested through differentiable spectral decomposition, and kept tractable by a greedy KL-based mixture reduction that provably commutes with $SE(3)$. On GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs, ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage at 0.090 $\mathring{A}$ error with sub-second latency -- over $100\times$ faster than diffusion baselines at higher accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, vanilla loopy BP diverges at 15+ agents while ENBP converges with near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (${\sim}10^{-7}$ vs.\ $10^{-1}$ for augmented baselines).
中文标题/摘要
标题：等变神经信念传播
在空间嵌入变量上的概率推理需要尊重$SE(3)$对称性的信念，但现有等变网络仅生成标量和向量——而非用于各向异性不确定性的秩-2精度张量，且单分量消息将多模态能量景观压缩为物理无意义的平均值。我们引入等变神经信念传播（ENBP），一种因子图框架，其消息为在$SE(3)$下精确变换的等变高斯混合模型，具有充分统计量。秩-2精度矩阵通过等变外积合成，经可微谱分解输入，并通过贪心KL基混合缩减保持可处理性，该方法在$SE(3)$下可证与变换可交换。在GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs上，ENBP在0.090 $\mathring{A}$误差下实现98.9%构象覆盖率，延迟低于一秒——比扩散基线快100倍以上且精度更高。在多体机器人推理中，普通环形BP在15+个代理时发散，而ENBP收敛，碰撞率接近零，等变误差达机器精度（${\sim}10^{-7}$，对比增强基线的$10^{-1}$）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for spatially symmetric probabilistic inference in physics and robotics, this work introduces Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework that propagates equivariant Gaussian mixture messages under SE(3) symmetry. Messages are constructed using rank-2 precision tensors synthesized via equivariant outer products, decomposed spectrally, and pruned via KL-based mixture reduction that preserves symmetry. Experiments on GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs show ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage with 0.090 Å error and sub-second latency, outperforming diffusion models by over 100x in speed while maintaining accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, ENBP converges reliably even with 15+ agents, achieving near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (≈10⁻⁷) compared to 10⁻¹ for non-equivariant baselines.
为解决3D空间中概率推理需保持SE(3)对称性的需求，本文提出Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation（ENBP），一种因子图框架，其消息为在SE(3)下精确变换的高斯混合模型，携带秩2精度张量以表征各向异性不确定性。消息通过等变外积合成，经可微谱分解处理，并通过贪心KL混合压缩保持对称性。在GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs数据集上，ENBP实现98.9%构象覆盖率，误差0.090 Å，延迟低于1秒，比扩散模型快100倍且精度更高。在多体机器人推理任务中，ENBP可在15+智能体下稳定收敛，碰撞率接近零，等变误差达10⁻⁷，远优于10⁻¹的增强基线方法。
Learning to optimize with guarantees: a complete characterization of linearly convergent algorithms
Authors: Andrea Martin, Ian R. Manchester, Luca Furieri
First: 2025-08-01T16:56:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:16:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The design of many classical optimization algorithms is driven by the certification of linear convergence rates over classes of optimization problems. In this paper, we consider the problem of improving the average-case performance of an algorithm over a specific distribution of problem instances. While this task can be tackled by embedding trainable components into the algorithm updates, a key challenge is to preserve worst-case guarantees across the entire problem class. For classes of composite optimization problems, we show that all linearly convergent algorithms can be parametrized in terms of a baseline linearly convergent algorithm, and a set of trainable, exponentially-decaying modifications to its update rule; crucially, this parametrization excludes all-and only-the algorithms that do not converge linearly. Our results apply to improving the average-case performance of classical algorithms such as gradient descent for nonconvex, gradient-dominated functions; Nesterov's accelerated method for smooth, strongly convex functions; and projected gradient methods for optimization over polyhedral feasible sets. We illustrate how our characterization can be used for learning to optimize with linear convergence and feasibility guarantees. Numerical results showcase benefits over classical optimizers when solving ill-conditioned systems of linear equations and running a model predictive control scheme on a linear dynamical system.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习带保证的优化：线性收敛算法的完整刻画
许多经典优化算法的设计以在优化问题类上证明线性收敛速率为目标。本文考虑在特定问题实例分布下提升算法的平均性能。虽然可通过在算法更新中嵌入可训练组件来实现，但关键挑战在于保持整个问题类的最坏情况保证。对于复合优化问题类，我们证明所有线性收敛算法均可参数化为一个基准线性收敛算法及其更新规则的可训练、指数衰减修改；关键在于，该参数化仅包含且仅包含非线性收敛算法。我们的结果适用于改进经典算法的平均性能，如用于非凸、梯度主导函数的梯度下降法；用于光滑强凸函数的Nesterov加速法；以及用于多面体可行集优化的投影梯度法。我们展示了该刻画如何用于学习带线性收敛与可行性保证的优化方法。数值结果表明，当求解病态线性方程组或在线性动态系统上运行模型预测控制时，相比经典优化器具有优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to enhance average-case performance of optimization algorithms while preserving worst-case linear convergence guarantees, this paper introduces a parametrization framework that characterizes all linearly convergent algorithms for composite optimization problems as modifications of a baseline algorithm with exponentially decaying adjustments. The method ensures that only linearly convergent algorithms are included, thereby enabling learning-based optimization without sacrificing theoretical convergence guarantees. Experimental results demonstrate improved performance over classical methods in solving ill-conditioned linear systems and running model predictive control on linear dynamical systems.
为在保持最坏情况线性收敛保证的前提下提升优化算法的平均性能，本文提出一种参数化框架，将复合优化问题中所有线性收敛算法表示为基准算法加上指数衰减调整的变体，确保仅包含线性收敛算法，从而支持带收敛保证的学习优化。实验表明，该方法在求解病态线性系统和线性动态系统的模型预测控制中，优于经典优化器。
Symmetric Divergence and Normalized Similarity: A Unified Topological Framework for Representation Analysis
Authors: Yan Wang, Tianyang Hu
First: 2026-06-04T16:14:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:14:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TMLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Topological Data Analysis (TDA) offers a principled, intrinsic lens for comparing neural representations. However, existing paired topological divergences (e.g., RTD) are limited by heuristic asymmetry and, more critically, unbounded scores that depend on sample size, hindering reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. To address these challenges, we develop a unified topological toolkit serving two complementary needs: fine-grained structural diagnosis and robust, standardized evaluation. First, we complete the RTD framework by introducing Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite. Beyond resolving the theoretical asymmetry of prior variants, SRTD consolidates diagnostic information into a single, comprehensive cross-barcode signature. This allows for precise localization of structural discrepancies and serves as an effective optimization objective without the overhead of dual directional computations. Second, to enable reliable benchmarking across heterogeneous settings, we propose Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS). By measuring the rank correlation of hierarchical merge orders, NTS yields a scale-invariant metric bounded between -1 and 1, effectively overcoming the scale and sample-dependence of unnormalized divergences. Experiments across synthetic and real-world deep learning settings demonstrate that our toolkit captures functional shifts in CNNs missed by geometric measures and robustly maps LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, offering a rigorous, topology-aware perspective that complements measures like CKA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对称发散与归一化相似度：一种统一的拓扑框架用于表示分析
拓扑数据分析（TDA）为比较神经表示提供了一种原则性、内在的视角。然而，现有的成对拓扑发散（如RTD）受限于启发式非对称性，更重要的是，其无界得分依赖于样本量，阻碍了跨场景的可靠基准比较。为解决这些问题，我们开发了一套统一的拓扑工具，满足两个互补需求：精细结构诊断与稳健标准化评估。首先，我们完善RTD框架，引入对称表示拓扑发散（SRTD）及其高效变体SRTD-lite。SRTD不仅解决了先前变体的理论非对称性，还将诊断信息整合为单一、全面的跨条形码签名，从而精确定位结构差异，并作为无需双向计算开销的有效优化目标。其次，为实现异构设置间的可靠基准比较，我们提出归一化拓扑相似度（NTS）。通过测量分层合并顺序的秩相关性，NTS生成一个介于-1和1之间的尺度不变度量，有效克服了未归一化发散的尺度和样本依赖性。在合成与真实世界深度学习场景中的实验表明，我们的工具集捕捉到了几何度量所遗漏的CNN功能变化，并在距离饱和下稳健地映射LLM谱系，提供了一种严谨、拓扑感知的视角，补充了CKA等度量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing topological divergences—such as asymmetry and unbounded scores dependent on sample size—the paper introduces a unified topological framework for analyzing neural representations. It proposes Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite to resolve asymmetry and consolidate structural diagnostics into a single cross-barcode signature, enabling precise discrepancy localization and efficient optimization. Additionally, it introduces Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS), a scale-invariant metric derived from rank correlation of merge orders, bounded between -1 and 1, to enable reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. Experiments on synthetic and real-world deep learning tasks show that the toolkit detects functional shifts in CNNs overlooked by geometric measures and robustly captures LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, providing a topology-aware alternative to metrics like CKA.
为解决现有拓扑距离指标存在的不对称性与样本规模依赖导致的无界性问题，本文提出一套统一的拓扑分析框架。该框架包含对称表示拓扑距离（SRTD）及其高效变体SRTD-lite，用于消除不对称性并整合结构诊断信息为单一跨条形码签名，实现结构差异的精准定位与高效优化。同时，提出归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），通过层级合并顺序的秩相关性构建尺度不变、范围在-1至1之间的度量，有效克服无归一化距离的尺度与样本依赖问题。在合成与真实深度学习任务中，该工具包成功捕捉到几何度量忽略的CNN功能变化，并在距离饱和下稳健映射LLM谱系，为CKA等指标提供拓扑视角的补充。
StoryVideoQA: Scaling Deep Video Understanding with a Large-Scale, Multi-Genre and Auto-Generated Dataset
Authors: Zhengqian Wu, Zhixian Liu, Aodong Chen, Jingyang Zhang, Ruizhe Li, Hanlin Ge, Zhongyuan Wang, Chunxia Xiao, Chao Liang
Venue: International Journal of Computer Vision (2026)
First: 2026-06-04T16:12:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:12:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IJCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video question answering (VideoQA) aims to answer questions about given videos. While existing approaches excel on factoid VideoQA, they struggle with deep video understanding (DVU), which requires the comprehension of complex storylines. This challenge arises from the inherent long-range video content, multi-faceted question types, and instance-level story elements, all of which constrain the scale and diversity of manually constructed DVU datasets. These difficulties constrain the scale and diversity of manually-constructed DVU dataset. To address these, we previously introduced StoryMind to automatically construct DVU datasets with balanced fine-grained topics. Though it can generate high-quality question-answer pairs (QAs) for TV series, it suffers significant performance degradation when handling longer and more complex movies. In this paper, we further design StoryMindv2, an enhanced multi-agent collaboration framework to generate high-quality DVU datasets for both TV series and movies. By integrating a novel supervisor-guided generation mechanism and a refined multi-reviewer voting strategy, the framework is utilized to construct StoryVideoQA, the largest DVU dataset to date, featuring over 363K QAs on 393.2 hours diverse story videos including TV series (avg. 1,635 seconds) and movies (avg. 7,878 seconds). Comprehensive evaluations of 20 state-of-the-art VideoQA methods on this large-scale benchmark reveal that they cannot fully maintain long-range character associations or construct a coherent understanding of complex storylines. To bridge this gap, we propose PlotTree, a novel video understanding agent, re-organizing long-range video content into a hierarchical plot structure, enabling efficient storyline reasoning on StoryVideoQA. Project page: https://github.com/nercms-mmap/StoryVideoQA/
中文标题/摘要
标题：StoryVideoQA：通过大规模、多类型且自动生成的数据集扩展深度视频理解
视频问答（VideoQA）旨在回答给定视频的问题。现有方法在事实型VideoQA上表现优异，但在深度视频理解（DVU）上表现不佳，后者需要理解复杂的情节。这一挑战源于视频内容的长程性、问题类型的多面性以及情节元素的实例级特性，这些因素限制了人工构建的DVU数据集的规模和多样性。为解决这些问题，我们此前提出了StoryMind，可自动构建具有平衡细粒度主题的DVU数据集。尽管它能为电视剧生成高质量的问答对（QAs），但在处理更长、更复杂的电影时性能显著下降。本文进一步设计了StoryMindv2，一种增强的多智能体协作框架，用于为电视剧和电影生成高质量的DVU数据集。通过集成新颖的监督引导生成机制和优化的多评审员投票策略，该框架构建了StoryVideoQA，目前规模最大的DVU数据集，包含393.2小时多样化故事视频（电视剧平均1635秒，电影平均7878秒）上的36.3万组QAs。对20种最新VideoQA方法在该大规模基准上的全面评估表明，它们无法充分保持长程角色关联或构建对复杂情节的连贯理解。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了PlotTree，一种新型视频理解智能体，将长程视频内容重组为层次化情节结构，从而在StoryVideoQA上实现高效的情节推理。项目页面：https://github.com/nercms-mmap/StoryVideoQA/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing VideoQA models in handling deep video understanding (DVU) due to sparse, manually constructed datasets, this work introduces StoryMindv2, a multi-agent framework that generates high-quality, large-scale DVU datasets by integrating supervisor-guided generation and refined multi-reviewer voting. The resulting StoryVideoQA dataset contains over 363K question-answer pairs across 393.2 hours of diverse TV series and movies, enabling comprehensive evaluation of 20 state-of-the-art VideoQA models. Results show these models fail to preserve long-range character associations or grasp complex storylines, prompting the proposal of PlotTree, a novel agent that structures video content hierarchically to enable coherent storyline reasoning.
为解决现有视频问答模型在深层视频理解（DVU）任务中因人工构建数据集规模小、多样性不足而表现不佳的问题，本文提出StoryMindv2多智能体框架，结合监督引导生成与多评审投票机制，构建了包含363K问答对、覆盖393.2小时影视内容的大型DVU数据集StoryVideoQA。在20个SOTA模型的评测中，它们均难以保持角色长程关联或理解复杂叙事。为此，作者提出PlotTree新模型，将视频内容结构化为层级剧情树，显著提升对StoryVideoQA中复杂故事线的推理能力。
TokenMizer: Graph-Structured Session Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Context Management
Authors: Shweta Mishra
First: 2026-06-04T16:12:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-04T16:12:28+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 10 figures. Code and benchmark available at https://github.com/Shweta-Mishra-ai/tokenmizer
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) deployments for long-horizon tasks face a fundamental constraint: context windows are finite while productive work sessions are not. When history exceeds the Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW), critical structured information - architectural decisions, task transitions, file histories - is silently discarded. Existing mitigations treat history as flat text, destroying the relational structure that makes sessions resumable. We present TokenMizer, an open-source proxy system that models LLM session history as a typed knowledge graph. The schema defines 14 node types and 7 edge types. A hybrid extraction pipeline populates the graph incrementally, while a three-tier checkpoint system serializes it into compact resume blocks. An 8-layer compression pipeline reduces context overhead, and a semantic cache reduces repeated-query latency. Evaluated on a controlled benchmark of 21 sessions spanning 5 domains, TokenMizer demonstrates significant token economy. It produces resume blocks averaging 78 tokens (range: 42-124) - 2x smaller than evaluated baselines (159-170 tokens) - while achieving higher decision recall (+9-17 percentage points). Crucially, baselines only preserve that a technology was mentioned; TokenMizer preserves the rationale. Across all sessions, TokenMizer achieves mean task recall 51.0%, decision recall 46.6%, and file recall 58.7%. Variance reflects domain heterogeneity: explicit imperative phrasing (software engineering) scores higher than implicit reasoning (research). Ablation studies show fuzzy label matching is the dominant improvement factor (+33 pp task recall). The heuristic compression achieves 47.3% token reduction with zero external dependencies. TokenMizer provides a queryable alternative to text-retention baselines at half the token cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TokenMizer：用于长周期LLM上下文管理的图结构会话记忆
长周期任务的大语言模型（LLM）部署面临根本性约束：上下文窗口有限，而实际工作会话无止境。当历史记录超出最大有效上下文窗口（MECW）时，关键结构化信息（如架构决策、任务转换、文件历史）将被静默丢弃。现有缓解方案将历史视为扁平文本，破坏了使会话可恢复的关联结构。我们提出TokenMizer，一个开源代理系统，将LLM会话历史建模为带类型的图知识库。该图包含14种节点类型和7种边类型。混合抽取管道增量填充图，三级检查点系统将其序列化为紧凑的恢复块。8层压缩管道降低上下文开销，语义缓存减少重复查询延迟。在涵盖5个领域的21个受控会话基准测试中，TokenMizer显著节省令牌。其平均生成恢复块仅78个令牌（范围42-124），仅为基线（159-170令牌）的一半，同时决策召回率提升9-17个百分点。关键在于，基线仅保留技术被提及的事实，而TokenMizer保留其推理依据。所有会话中，TokenMizer平均任务召回率51.0%，决策召回率46.6%，文件召回率58.7%。方差反映领域异质性：明确指令式表述（如软件工程）得分高于隐式推理（如研究）。消融研究表明模糊标签匹配是主要改进因素（任务召回率+33个百分点）。启发式压缩实现47.3%令牌减少，且无外部依赖。TokenMizer以一半令牌成本提供可查询的替代方案，优于文本保留基线。
Summary / 总结
TokenMizer addresses the challenge of managing long-session context in LLMs by modeling session history as a typed knowledge graph with 14 node types and 7 edge types, preserving structured relationships like architectural decisions and file histories that flat-text methods discard. It uses a hybrid extraction pipeline to incrementally populate the graph, a three-tier checkpoint system to serialize it into compact resume blocks, and an 8-layer compression pipeline with semantic caching to reduce token overhead and latency. Evaluated across 21 sessions from five domains, TokenMizer generates resume blocks averaging 78 tokens — half the size of baselines — while boosting task recall by 9–17 percentage points and achieving mean task recall of 51.0%, decision recall of 46.6%, and file recall of 58.7%. Ablation studies confirm fuzzy label matching as the key contributor to performance gains, and the heuristic compression achieves 47.3% token reduction without external dependencies.
TokenMizer 为长时任务中 LLM 上下文窗口有限的问题提供解决方案，通过将会话历史建模为包含14种节点和7种边的结构化知识图谱，保留如架构决策和文件历史等关键结构化信息。它采用混合抽取管道动态构建图谱，三阶段检查点系统将其压缩为紧凑的恢复块，并结合8层压缩管道与语义缓存降低开销与延迟。在五个领域21个会话的评估中，TokenMizer 生成的恢复块平均仅78个token（范围42–124），仅为基线（159–170 token）的一半，同时任务、决策、文件召回率分别达51.0%、46.6%、58.7%，且能保留推理依据而非仅提及内容。模糊标签匹配带来33个百分点的任务召回提升，启发式压缩实现47.3%的token减少，无需外部依赖。
